BAMANANKAN FASARI ƝUMANW — USER KA LATIGƐW
Sɛbɛn in bɔra MonActivite_Bamanankan.txt la
Taamasiyɛn kunba: 572
Fasari ɲuman yɛrɛyɛrɛ: 421

=== FASARI 1 (taamasiyɛn 2) — 2026-10-01 22:33:23 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman

A bɛ tile 4 bɔ

Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara : Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw – bɛ Kuntaalasurunya kɔnɔ:

✅ Denbaya 13000 ni kɔ minnu bɛ mara 56 kɔnɔ, olu bɛna se k’u ka so ni gazi jɔw cɛsiri ani ka minɛnw sɔrɔ minnu ka kan. Gofɛrɛnaman bɛ ka rubulu miliyari 1,2 wɛrɛ di gazi sɔrɔli waleyali kama. Nin dɛmɛ in dabɔra denbayabaw kama, kɛlɛdenya baara kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen senfɛmɔgɔw n’u ka denbayaw, ani nalomanya bana kulu fɔlɔw, ani mɔgɔ minnu ka sɔrɔ ka dɔgɔ, ani minnu bɛ denmisɛnw ladon minnu ka kɛnɛyako gɛlɛyara.

✅ Ka bɔ ŋaniya talenw na Finfin baara bɛna yiriwa ka da fɛɛrɛ kuraw jusigilan kan. Yɔrɔla Gofɛrɛnaman wuli la k'a jɔ ni nin baarada in yiriwali waleya kurayali ye fo ka se san 2050 ma. Wa a bɛ tali kɛ minɛn kuraw donni fɛɛrɛ la, ani fɛɛrɛ minnu bɛ k'u yɛrɛma, ani jatedenya yɔrɔw; ani dogolen kuraw yiriwali; ani finfin chimie yɔrɔw ni konaya furakɛli; ani baarakɛyɔrɔ lakanani ɲɛnabɔli. I n'a fɔ labɛnw bɛ senna walasa ka dɔ fara finfin bɔli kan ni 50% ye ni san 2050 seli ye.

✅ Gofɛrɛnaman ye foroba ka baarakɛnafolo bolodalen sɔn san 2027–2029. Sigida ka jaakuya dafali bɛ to ka kɛ fɔlɔfɔlɔko jɔnjɔn ye. Nafolo bilala sen kan walasa ka denbayaw dɛmɛ minnu bɛ ni denmisɛnw ye ani ka kɛnɛyako ni kalan yiriwa. Jateminɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen bɛna kɛ jamana ka laɲiniw dafali la, o la, fɛɛrɛbɔ ɲɛmɔgɔya sɔrɔli fana sen b'o la. Ka fara marabolow bɛɛ ka dɛmɛ toli kan i n'a fɔ a tun bɛ ka cogo min san 2026 kɔnɔ.

----------------------------------------

Ni dolari miliyari 600 digili ye

=== FASARI 3 (taamasiyɛn 6) — 2026-09-27 10:17:26 CEST ===
Cogo caman bɛna daminɛ Irisi jamana na, baarakɛlaw ye minnu bɛ baara kɛ filimu, dɔnkilida, dilancogo ani daɲɛw dilanni baara wɛrɛw la. Gofɛrɛnaman ye fɛɛrɛ dɔ ta walasa ka daɲɛw sɔrɔ yiriwali siratigɛ la fo ka se san 2036. Yɔrɔ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw, yiriwali baaradaw, ani daɲɛw kuluw bɛna sigi senkan maraw kɔnɔ. Kalan waleyali, degeli, ani baarakɛlaw yiriwali walew bɛna bonya kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la. Hakilila-jɔ-sɛbɛnw lakanani fana bɛna barika sɔrɔ walasa ka mɔgɔ wɛrɛw ka baara kɛcogo ta k'a sɔrɔ a tigi ma sɔn o bɛna kɛlɛ ka ɲɛ. Irisi ja lamagata dilannikɛyɔrɔw bɛna dɛmɛ wɛrɛ sɔrɔ, n’u ka tɔnw ka sɔrɔta saalenw hakɛ bɛna dɔgɔya ka se kɛmɛsaradala saba ma. An b’a jira k'an bɛna Irisi filimu ɲumanw ye minnu suguya ɲɛ kɔrɔtalen don kosɛbɛ minnu ɲɛsinnen bɛ denbayaw ma sinemasow la, telew la ani bɔlɔlɔ kan. Ka fɛn dilanni baara kura daminɛ ani ka waridonna sama, o bɛna nɔgɔya Irisi jamana na. Mikhail Mishustin ye fɛɛrɛbɔ dɔ kɛ jamana ka jagokɛlaw ka ɲɛtaa kan. Fɛɛrɛ fɔlɔ min bɛ kɛ ka nafolodonni nisɔndiya bonya, o ye jamana ka misali ye, min ye laɲini cogoyaw ye jago kɛli kama ; nin misali in bɛ dɛmɛ don ka jagokɛyɔrɔ ɲuman dilan ani ka dɔ bɔ marako gɛlɛyaw la. Nafolodon siratigɛ kura daminɛni tora a ɲɛnakoba ye. Walasa k’o sɔrɔ, an ka kan ka t’a fɛ ka cogoya nɔgɔmanw, minnu bɛ se ka fɔ ka ɲɛ, ka ɲɛsin waridonnaw ma, ka jateminɛ kɛ jagokɛlaw ka ɲininiw na, an ka maraw magow, ani jamana bɛɛ ka nafaw. Taama kɛli Crimée mara lamini na, o bɛna nɔgɔya ka taa a fɛ. Sira min bɛ Tavrida foroba siraba cɛ ka taa Crimea woroduguanfan kɔgɔjida la, o bɛ ka jɔ kokura o gun kan, ni rubulu miliyari saba n'a kunkanfɛn duuru bilala o baara in na. O sira bɛna tɛmɛ Simferopol, Alushta ani Yalta fɛ. Nin ŋaniya in bɛna kɛ sababu ye ka bolifɛnw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ siraw ɲɛ ka taa a fɛ Kirime duguw ni dugubaw cɛ, k’a sɔrɔ a bɛna taama sira ɲumanw di yaala yaalakɛlaw ma gun in kɔnɔ. San kura ɲɛnajɛw bɛna kɛ tile 11 kɔnɔ; Irisikaw bɛna lafiɲɛbɔ waati daminɛ desanburukalo tile 31 ka se Zanwuyekalo tile 10. Mikhail Mishustin ye a bolonɔ bila sariya dɔ la min ɲɛsinnen bɛ foroba lafiɲɛ donw ni lafiɲɛ donw ma san 2027. Feburuyekalo la, lafiɲɛ don saba bɛ tugu ɲɔgɔn na k’a ta tile 21 na ka se tile 23 ma. Marisikalo la, lafiɲɛbɔ bɛ daminɛ wɔɔrɔnan na ka Taa seeginnan ma. Mɛkalo ɲɛnajɛw bɛɛ lajɛlen bɛna kɛ tile wɔɔrɔ ye, u tilalen don waati fila ye: Mɛkalo tile 1–3 ani Mɛkalo tile 8–10. Tile saba lafiɲɛbɔ wɛrɛ bɛna kɛ zuwɛnkalo la, k’a ta tile 12 la ka se tile 14 ma. Nowanburukalo la, lafiɲɛ don naani bɛna kɛ, k’a ta tile 4 na ka se tile 7 ma.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 4 (taamasiyɛn 8) — 2026-09-20 15:09:47 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya 

Sɛtanburukalo tile 18 san 2026, Mali minisiriw ka tɔnba ka lajɛ min ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ kɛlɛbolo Ɲɛmaaba Asimi Goyita bolo, o sɔnna :

San 2027 baarakɛnafolo bolodalen : dɔ farali sɔrɔ ni musaka fila bɛɛ kan, ka fara dɛsɛ dɔgɔyali dɔɔnin kan.

Jamana ka kaso fɛɛrɛ dɔ (2026–2030).

Lasigidenw sigili minisiriso damadɔ kɔnɔ.

Laseliw : Taamaden minnu b' Ameriki jamana kɔnɔ o funankɛninw ka forobajɛkulu dɔ ka lajɛ, bɔlɔlɔ jatedenya u ka wasa kama, sendon silamɛya yiriwali waribon ka lajɛ dɔ la,

ani kelenya kalo daminɛni, ka fara yɛrɛ tanga kan sumayaba bana ko la.

=== FASARI 5 (taamasiyɛn 10) — 2026-09-17 17:11:17 CEST ===
Ameriki kɛlɛbolo bɛ labɛn cogoya dan na ka fara Iran bonni kan, wa a jɛɲɔgɔnw bɛ fanga sɔrɔli laɲiniw na

 Tom Hanson ye kunnafoni di ka bɔ USS George Washingtonni, pankuruntalan kɔnɔ min bilala Kɔrɔnfɛla cɛmancɛ jamanaw na. yɔrɔla Ameriki baarakɛla 50.000 bɛɛ bɛ ka labɛn mara kɔnɔ walasa ka kɛlɛ cogoba a mana kɛ waati o waati ye.

=== FASARI 6 (taamasiyɛn 12) — 2026-09-15 17:42:49 CEST ===
Faransi jamana kɔkanko kunnafoni labanw

A Tiɲɛ ni kuntaalasurunya ye (fo ka se san 2026 sɛtanburukalo tile 15 ma):

Finlande farala Faransi ka nukliyɛri balili baara ɲɛtaa kan foroba la (a kɛra jamana 10nan ye min sen b'o la).

O wale in laɲini ye ka Erɔpu jamanaw ka balili sinsin k’a sɔrɔ a ma OTAN falen ani k’a sɔrɔ a ma nukliyɛri marifaw carin Finlande jamana kɔnɔ bɛn waati la.

Jamanakuntigi Makɔrɔn ni Kanada minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ Mark Carney ka lajɛ dɔ bolodara sɛtanburukalo tile 20 Saint-Pierre ani Miquelon walasa ka jɛkafɔ sinsin fanga, lafasali ani damanmafɛn nafamaw na, ani fɛɛrɛw Israɛl sigiyɔrɔw ka fɛn bɔtaw sinaya la.

Nin ye tiɲɛ dannayataw ye sisan Faransi jamana kɔkanko fɛɛrɛw la.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 7 (taamasiyɛn 14) — 2026-09-15 09:10:00 CEST ===
Ameriki jamana kɔkankow kibaruya labanw

 Ni kuntaalasurunya ye kojugu (kabini Sɛtanburukalo tile 15, san 2026):

Koba minnu bɛ senna sisan Ameriki jamana kɔkankow la:

Kɛlɛ n' Iran ye,o bɛ senna: Gosiliw falen falenni dansigilen don; Trump bɛ ban bɛnkan bɛɛ la "mi ka fɛgɛ" wa bɛ jɛgɛn ni toli ye, ani k'a poro taji kan; wa Washingtonni bɛ ka Gɛrɛntɛ kura jaakuya k'a sɔrɔ a bɛ k'a damakasi marifakisɛ dɛsɛ fɛ.

Irisi ni Ikɛrɛni: Trump ye bɛnkan dɔ laseli kɛ walasa ka binkanniw dabila fanga sɔrɔyɔrɔw kan, ka sɔrɔ Zelenskyy bɛ k’a sinsin dalakan tiɲɛw kan ka bɔ Moskow fɛ.

Kanada: Ameriki ka saalen kura, Wa  Ottawa bɛ k'a ɲɛsin ka taa Erɔpu fan fɛ walasa k'a ka dulonni dɔgɔya Washingtonni na.

Nin de ye ko jamakisɛ ye sisan.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 8 (taamasiyɛn 20) — 2026-09-06 15:26:26 CEST ===
Kɔkankow kibaruya bagennenw Faransi ko la, olu kuncɛlen surunya la (fo ka se sɛtanburukalo tile 5 san 2026):

Kore Worodugu jamana na ka bɔnye: Kore Worodugu jamanakuntigi Lee Jae-myung bɛna taa bɔ Faransi (sɛtanburukalo tile 6-9) walasa ka ɲɔgɔnye dɔ kɛ ni Makɔrɔn ye ani ka filimuko lajɛba dɔ kɛ, walasa ka jɛɲɔgɔnya basigi fɛɛrɛ lakana, fɛɛrɛbɔ ani sɔrɔko.

Lakanalitɔn ɲɛmaaya : Faransi bɛna ONU ka lakanatɔn ɲɛmɔgɔya ta san 2026 sɛtanburukalo kuntaala bɛɛ kɔnɔ.

Jɛɲɔgɔnya ni Angletɛri ye : Jɛɲɔgɔnya sinsinin Bayeux geseko jirali senfɛ, k’a sɔrɔ basigibaliyaw bɛ senna ka ɲɛsin dɛnkaboli ma min tɛ sariya kɔnɔ jisira fɛ.

Ko wɛrɛw : Makrɔn ka barow ni OTAN ye Ikɛrɛni ko la, Faransi bɛ minɛni kɛ n'a ka ɲininiw ye Kɔgɔji madalen gunw kan Vanuatu ɲɛkɔrɔ, ani basigibaliyaw tɛmɛnenw n' Iran n' Alizeri ye jɔnyako la.

Nin ye kɔkanko barokun bagennenw ye sisan ni surunya ye.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 9 (taamasiyɛn 23) — 2026-09-05 20:49:17 CEST ===
Ameriki jamana kɔkan kibaruya labanw kuncɛlen surunya la ni tiɲɛ ye (Sɛtanburukalo tile 5, san 2026):

Iran: Kɛlɛ bɛ senna waati ni waati.

Trump b’a ko dɔgɔya, k’a wele k'o ye “ko fitinin”, ye k’a sɔrɔ a musaka tɛmɛna dolari miliyari 37,5 kan ani Amerikika 18 fagara.

Gazoli sɔngɔw sera a danna Ameriki jamana na, k’a kun kɛ basigibaliyaw ye.

Venezuela: Ameriki ka taji bεnkanba min kɛra ni mara ye yen, o kɔrɔfɔra Venezuela jamana Fanga sinamatɔnw fɛ, k’a kun kɛ a ye Bɛɛjɛfanga yɛlɛma bɛɛ sumaya.

Ikɛrɛni: Ameriki ka cidenw bɛ Moskow ni hɛrɛ laɲini ye, ka fara waati dɔɔnin kɔnɔna na, kɛlɛ dabilali bɛna kɛ sanfɛla la, taama in senfɛ.

Nin ye kɔkanko  yiriwali ye sisan; a tɔw ye kɔnɔnakow ye walima kalatakow.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 10 (taamasiyɛn 33) — 2026-08-30 15:51:39 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamana kɔkan kibaruya labanw (Utikalo tile 30, san 2026)

Xi Jinping sera Kirgistan jamana na Shanghai jɛ-ka-baara jɛkulu ka lajɛba la, a ni Putin ka kan ka ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ, o kɔfɛ a bɛ taa bɔ Misira jamana na.

Jiwoyo balawuw ni bɔgɔ yuuruli ani Nepali jamana dancɛ la: Mɔgɔ kɛmɛ caman fagara ani tununnenw; Wa Sinuwa jamana bɛ ka dɛmɛ ni dɔnnikɛlaw ci.

Uzbekistan ye Sinuwa jamana ka kɛlɛkɛ-pankurun J-10CE san.

Sinuwa jamana bɛ ka Ameriki ka Gɛrɛntɛ kura kɛlɛ Iran n’a jɛɲɔgɔnw kan.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 11 (taamasiyɛn 40) — 2026-08-29 19:25:22 CEST ===
Ɛndujamana kɔkan kibaruyaw laban - Utikalo tile 29, san 2026:

Modi bɛ Cɛma Azi jamana na: A Bɔra Uzbekistan, o kɔ fɛ Kirgistan ka Taa Shanghai jɛ-ka-baara jɛkulu ka lajɛba la, ka fara ɲɔgɔnye jigiyalen dɔ kan ni Putin ye.

Sinuwa jamana: Bɛnkan sɔrɔla tomi 8 kan dancɛw kumaɲɔgɔnyaw la (Doval-Wang Yi), kumaɲɔgɔnya bɛ ka taa ɲɛ, taasira kalaman kura, wa kumaɲɔgɔnya nata bɛna kɛ Ɛndujamana na san 2027.

Nepali jamana: Ɛndi jamanaden kɛmɛ caman tununna jiwoyoba juguw kɔfɛ; Ɛndujamana bɛ ka dɛmɛ ni kisi jɛkuluw ci.

Ameriki: Javelin misili sanni bɛnkan min bɛ kɛ ka kɛlɛkɛwotorow kɛlɛ  (292 kuru miliyɔn caman sɔrɔ ɲɔgɔn).

Nin de ye yiriwali bagennenw ye.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 12 (taamasiyɛn 51) — 2026-08-25 10:47:45 CEST ===
Faransi jamana kɔkan kibaruya labanw

Kuma surun na ko jugu (Faransi jamana kɔkan kibaruya labanw ka se Utikalo tile 25, san 2026):

Salman denkɛ Mohamɛdi ka taama ka taa Pari (Utikalo tile 23-24):

Lajɛ min kɛra ni Makɔrɔn ye, n’o ye jɛkamiirikalandenw ka tɔnba ka lajɛ fɔlɔ ye, lafasali ni wariko bɛnkanw (o la, erowari miliyari 6 bɛ kɛ ɲɛnajɛ n bɛ diyayɔrɔw la), ani jɛkafɔ welekanw ka ɲɛsin kɔkanko ɲɛnabɔli ma ni Iran ye, ni ka marifaci dabila Gaza, ani ka dɛmɛ don Liban ni Siri Basigi la.

Basigibaliya ni Iran ye: Faransi ye Iran kɔkankokɛlaw gɛn , k’a kɛ dansago tajurusara ye Faransi kɔkankokɛlaw kan Tɔharani.

Ikɛrɛni dɛmɛni: Makɔrɔn ye layidu ta ko a bɛna kunbɛnnikɛlan misiliw ci Risi jamana ka cunkanni kura kɔfɛ.

Jɛɲɔgɔnya ni Misira ye: Weeleli jamana kɔkankokɛla minisiri fila cɛ, u y’a jira ko u bɛ dɛmɛ don Siri jamana ka yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya n’a ka basigi la.

Nin ye tiɲɛ tomiw ye ani tilennen.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 13 (taamasiyɛn 55) — 2026-08-24 23:31:51 CEST ===
Ameriki jamana kɔkan kibaruya labanw

Kuma surun na (ko min bɛ sen na sisan):

Ameriki ye sɔrɔko Gɛrɛntɛ wuli ka jɔba dɔ daminɛ Iran sinaya la bi, n’a tɔgɔ ye ko « Sɔrɔko baara gerunni», a kun ye ka Iran ka sɔrɔko kan dɛrɛ ɲɛ bɛɛ ma ani k’a ka nafolo sɔrɔyɔrɔw bɛɛ tigɛ (taji, sanu, donni, jatedenya wariw, an' a ɲɔgɔnnaw).

Ameriki ka saalen 50% min bɛ Kanada fɛnw kan, n’u nafa bɛ se dolari miliyari 20 ma, o donna baara la, kumaɲɔgɔnya tiɲɛnen kɔfɛ, wa Kanada y'a ta sara o cogo la.

Ninnu ye Ameriki jamana kɔkan kibaruyaw kunbaba fila ye sisan.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 14 (taamasiyɛn 61) — 2026-08-22 14:22:09 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman

Utikalo tile 16, 18h03 

 Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara.  Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ Sɛnɛgali kɔnɔ:

 Mikhail Mishustin y'a sen don Irisi jamana bɛɛ ka forobajɛkulu la min tɔgɔ ye ko "Dugu misɛnninw ni tariku sigiyɔrɔw yiriwali."  Lajɛba in senfɛ, minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ y’a jira ko yɔrɔ 120000 ɲɔgɔn ɲɛnabɔra jamana foroba ka baarabolodalen « Ka dugu sigida lafiyalen dilan » waleyali senfɛ.  Mikhail Mishustin fana ye forobajɛkulu in ka jirali lajɛ, min ye ɲɛtaa baarabolodalenw jira mara kuraw ni dankanw na.

 Sɔrɔko hɔrɔnyalen mara fanga bonyalen don walasa ka Belgorod, Bryansk ani Kursk maraw bɛɛ lajɛlen minɛ.  Jagokɛlaw bɛna se ka wariko baarabolodalenw waleya fɛn dilanni ni sɛnɛko siratigɛ la, ka kɛɲɛ ni fɛɛrɛ ɲumanw ye.

 Gofɛrɛnaman ye fɛɛrɛ tigɛ walasa ka kɔrɔnfɛla yɔrɔjan jamanaden hakɛ fɛɛrɛ tigɛ fo san 2030. Sigidamɔgɔw bɛna t’a fɛ ka nafa sɔrɔ so dɔnɔ waleyaliw la minnu ka fisa, u den fɔlɔ bangeli sarali, ani marabolo ka bangekɔlɔsi nafolokun den filanan bangeli kama.   Walasa ka baarakɛlaw sama ka na yɔrɔjanw na, waleya minnu bɛ wele ko « Zemsky furakɛlikɛla », « Zemsky kɔrɔtɔkokɛla», « Zemsky karamɔgɔ », « Zemsky laadala baarakɛla » ani « Zemsky degelikɛla » fana bɛna t’a fɛ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 15 (taamasiyɛn 66) — 2026-08-20 16:41:43 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamana (n’a ka tɔnw i n’a fɔ Skylark ani Novasky) Olu bɛ ka doroni kɛlɛli fɛɛrɛw labɛn tiɲɛ na minnu sinsinnen bɛ tanni tilennen kan (lamaga kunbɛnna), n'i m'o dɔn o ye, doroni fitinin ye min ka teli, a bɛ jugu ka Pankurunw tan k’u mɛrɛnkɛ k’a sɔrɔ a ma pɛrɛnnikɛlanw ye walima k'u ɲangami dɔrɔn.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 16 (taamasiyɛn 73) — 2026-08-19 20:25:55 CEST ===
Ɲɔ, Keninge ani Narakata bɛ ka sɛnɛkɛlaw dɛmɛ cogo min na ka jiko gɛlɛya kunbɛn
Sanji minnu bɛ na kɔfɛ, ja, ani waati juguw bɛ nɔ bila sɛnɛ na Ɛndujamana bɛɛ kɔnɔ, sɛnɛkɛlaw bɛ ka u ɲɛsin laadala sumankisɛw ma i n’a fɔ Ɲɔ, Keninge ani Narakata. I n'a fɔ u bɛ ji dɔɔnin de jaakuya ani ka fɛn caman kɛ, o sumanw minnu bɛ se ka waati cogoya kunbɛn, olu bɛ ka dɛmɛ don ka faratiw dɔgɔya ka sɔrɔ ka dumuni ni balodɛsɛ lakanani dɛmɛ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 17 (taamasiyɛn 153) — 2026-08-11 21:10:12 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamanakuntigiya ka kibaruyaw labanw

Kuma surun na (fo ka na se Utikalo tile 11, san 2026):

Xi Jinping ka taama makɔnɔnen Ameriki jamana na sɛtanburukalo la (24nan ɲɔgɔn) ka jaabi di Trump ka taama ma Pekin Mɛkalo la, 

ni jago kumaɲɔgɔnyaw bɛ senna.

Tilema Beidaihe lajɛw bɛ senna walasa ka kɔnɔnajɛya kɛ.

Fɛɛrɛtɔnw ka lajɛ min kɛra zuluyekalo laban na, o ye sɔrɔko ɲɛtaa kunbabaw sigi senkan san tilancɛ filanan na, wa tɔnw ka lajɛ dafalen bɛna kɛ ɔkutɔburukalo la.

Wuli ka jɔ min bɛ senna ka ɲɛsin nɔgɔlenya kɛlɛli ma kɛlɛcɛw la, o bɛ senna,

Ani Sinuwa jamana bɛ ka dɛmɛn don Brezili ma diɲɛ jagotɔnba kɔnɔ.

Labɛnw bɛ senna Shanghai jɛ-ka-baara jɛkulu ka lajɛba kama Bishkek Utikalo laban na.

=== FASARI 22 (taamasiyɛn 256) — 2026-08-10 13:16:27 CEST ===
Ameriki Sɛnɛko minisiri Brooke Rollins bɛ kuma a ka cɛsiriw kan kabini a ye fanga ta:

A y’a daminɛ ni lajɛ tilennenw kɛli ye ni sɛnɛkɛlaw ni baganmaralaw ye walasa k’u ka magow faamuya ani k’u baara kɛwalew la.

A ye ko gɛlɛn dɔ ciyɛn ta : yɛlɛmali ka bɔ sɛnɛko jago tɔnɔ na ka taa dɛsɛ jateminɛlen na min bɛ se dolari miliyari 50 ɲɔgɔn ma, ka fara bɔta musakaw kɔrɔtali kan ani ɲɛmaasoko damantɛmɛ.

A ye min kɛ: A ye jagokɛɲɔgɔnya 20 kɛ, ka sugu kura da yɛlɛ, ka dɛmɛ dolari miliyari 40 ni kɔ di sɛnɛkɛlaw ma, ka sɛnɛ lakanani sira sinsin, ka sɛnɛfɛnw basigi bonya, ka taabolo nɔgɔya, ka musakaw dɔgɔya, ka sɛnɛ lakana ka bɔ bagabagali la i n’a fɔ Diɲɛ tumu kura.

O kɔlɔlɔ: Jagokɛyɔrɔ caman, musakaw dɔgɔyali, lakana sira fangama, ani Sɛnɛko Minisiri min bɛ sɛnɛkɛlaw lamɛn.

Fɛɛrɛ nata:
Sɛnɛkɛlaw sendon sɛnɛko minisiriso ka sɛgɛsɛgɛliw la, o jigira ka bɔ 85-90% na ka se 40% ɲɔgɔn ma. O de kama, a bɛ baara kɛ walasa ka kunnafonidi fɛɛrɛw kuraya walasa ka dɔ bɔ doni na sɛnɛkɛlaw kan ani ka tiɲɛni ni jɛlenya yiriwa, ka kɛɲɛ ni ɲɔgɔnyew ye sɛnɛko kɛnɛbaw ni ko kɛtaw la.

=== FASARI 30 (taamasiyɛn 594) — 2026-07-30 13:13:26 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman
Zuluyekalo tile 26, nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 18:00
Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ sɛgɛsɛgɛli kɔnɔ:

✅Gofɛrɛnaman bɛna Belgorod mara dɛmɛ walasa a ka fanga sɔrɔyɔrɔw ka baara sabati. Wa mara in bɛna sɔrɔli kɛ min bɛna tɛmɛ rubulu miliyari 8,6 kan walasa ka kalaya jɔyɔrɔ sumanikɛlanw san, ani kuran tilalikɛla sumanikɛlanw min bɛ se ka ta ka taa, ani kuran yɛlɛmalikɛlanw.

✅Fɛn bɔliyɔrɔ kuraw ni baarakɛyɔrɔw bɛna dilan Khakassia ni Tatarstan jamanaw na. Wa tiɲɛna Gofɛrɛnaman ye dɛmɛ don fɛɛrɛ fila la walasa ka sɔrɔko yɔrɔ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw yiriwa.

✅Irisi jamana bɛna yelikɛ dɔ farali la jamana kɔnɔ fɛn suguya ɲɛ kɔrɔtali la. Nin kama, gɔfɛrɛnaman ye jamana ka waleya labɛn sɛnɛ yiriwali kama. Wa Foro misɛnnin tigiw minnu b’a fɛ ka baarabolodalenw kɛ walasa ka nɔnɔ bɔli teliya, olu bɛna dɛmɛ sɔrɔ min bɛ se rubulu miliyɔn 265 ma. Wa nafolo ninnu bɛ se ka kɛ ka dɔ fara bagankulu hakɛ kan, ani ka minɛnw san, ni ka baganbalow dɛsɛ ɲɛnabɔ. Ka fara o kan, dɛmɛ bɛ di sɛnɛkɛlaw ma minnu bɛ bɔ dankan maraw la ani fɛnw bɔlikɛlaw minnu sen bɛ sɛnɛfɛnw bɔli dɛmɛ waleyali la.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 31 (taamasiyɛn 657) — 2026-07-29 11:02:48 CEST ===
N b’a fɛ fɔlɔ ka n ka foliba lase jamanakuntigi Goïta ma, a ka bisimilali duman ni balimaya la. I n’a fɔ a bɛ kɛ cogo min na, a y'an bisimila Mali jatigila hakili lakika la. I n'a fɔ Aw b’a dɔn, Senegali ni Mali sirilen bɛ tariku jɛɲɔgɔnyaw fɛ, ka bɔ teriya ni balimaya la. Olu ye sigiɲɔgɔnya ɲuman jɛɲɔgɔnyaw ye, ani somɔgɔya sirijuruw ni joli ni teriya ni laadalakow ani jɛ-ka-baara.

Wa o jɛɲɔgɔnyaw ye, ka taa dugukoloko kɛrɛfɛ an'an ka tariku jɛlen, olu de bɛ kɛ jɛɲɔgɔnya kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen ni misali ye, o min b'an ka jamanaw siri ɲɔgɔn na. Nin de kama Ne ka sɔrɔli Bamakɔ bi, o bɛɛ bɛ nin balimaya in taasira kɔnɔ. N nana ka taa bɔ a ye ni bonya ye, wa k'a kuraya a ye, a n’a ka mara ye, ka fara balima Malidenw kan, an ka sangafo jɛlen bɛ kɔsa in na, cunkanni juguw la o min ye kɛlɛcɛ damadɔw faga.

An b'an biri bonya kɔsɔn u kosumani na ani ka Ala delili k'u datugu n'a ka hinɛ fɛrɛlen ye. I n'a fɔ N y’a jira fana jamanakuntigi Goïta la Senegali ka jalakiba la, min tigɛlen don, ani min tɛ wuli ka bɔ a nɔ na, nin wale jugu in na, ani jitɔya ni baganya, o min bɛ ka juguya ka taa fɛ, N b’a ni balima Maliden denkɛw bɛɛ tanu, Cɛsiri ni nafolo minnu kɛra, ani saraka minnu kɛra, walasa ka hɛrɛ ni lafiya sabati nin balima jamana in kɔnɔ.

Wa tiɲɛna, an ye tuma sɔrɔ ka baro kɛ hakilina wɛrɛw kan an fila ka ɲɔgɔnye senfɛ, minnu ɲɛsinnen bɛ hɛrɛ ni lakanako ma marabolo fitininw kɔnɔ, nka kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, fɛɛrɛw n’a kɛcogo minnu bɛ se ka jatigɛwale kɛlɛ ka ɲɛ, o min bɛ ka taa a fɛ ka jɛnsɛn ani ka an ka jamanadenw bɛɛ bagabaga. Bari, i n’a fɔ n y’a fɔ cogo min na ka tɛmɛ, Senegali labɛnnen don ka ban ka Mali dɛmɛ nin gɛlɛya in na, fo an jɛlen ka se ɲɔgɔn fɛ ka gɛlɛyaw ni bagabagaliw minnu bɛ hɛrɛ ni basigibaliya kunbɛn, mara fan bɛɛ kɔnɔ, o to yen ani Saheli mara bɛɛ kɔnɔ, i k'a dɔn nin bagabagali ninnu, i n’a fɔ an bɛnna a kan cogo min na, a tɛ danyɔrɔ dɔn, wa a bɛ ka juguya ka da an ka farali kan.

I n'a fɔ An ye baro kɛ fana jɛkafɔ sinsinni siraw kan walasa ka gɛlɛyaw kunbɛn ɲɔgɔn fɛ, jamana fila ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ ani marabolo jɛkuluw kɔnɔ, Mali ni Senegali ye minnu kɔnɔmɔgɔw ye. Wa bɛ n nisɔndiya kosɛbɛ jamanakuntigi ka hakilinaw lajɛlen dafalen na an ye baro kɛ ko kunbaba minnu kan, ni balimaya ni ye ani jɛɲɔgɔnya, an ka cɛsiriw kɛli kelen ye fɛ.

N y’a fɔ jamanakuntigi Goïta ye ko a bɛ se k'a banban Senegali ka dɛmɛn kan min tɛ wuli. Wa N y’a gɛlɛya kokura an sɔrɔli n’an ka cɛsiri jira Mali la, wa b'o cogo la ka fara jamana tɔw bɛɛ kan, walasa ka ɲɔgɔn ka gɛlɛyaw ɲɛnabɔ ɲɔgɔn fɛ. I n'a fɔ An ye baro kɛ fana UEMOA ni OMVS ka ko taasira kan, wa an bɛnna a kan ka baara kɛ n’an jɛɲɔgɔnw ye minnu bɔra balima jamana wɛrɛw la, walasa ka jamanakuntigiw ka lajɛba dɔ sigi senkan, joona, walasa ka wariko ni sɔrɔko jɛ-ka-baara sabati, ka fara ji nafolo ɲɛnabɔli kan, ba jurujan fɛ o min b'an ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ.

Jamana fila ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ jɛɲɔgɔnya : Ka jɛkafɔ sabati ni Alizeri (Enerji ni Jago) ani Nizeriya ye, ani kɔgɔji lakanani jɛkafɔ ni Faransi.

Kɔlɔsili: Diplomasi jɛkafɔba kɛli juruko gɛlɛya cɛma ani san 2026 Dakari lajɛba labɛnni.

=== FASARI 41 (taamasiyɛn 727) — 2026-07-14 11:16:31 CEST ===
Ameriki ka fanga minisiriso

Ameriki kɛlɛbolo ni jamanakuntigi Trump ka cɛsiriw sababu la, taji bɛ ka ta ka tɛmɛ Babɔlɔn koronyɔrɔ fɛ.

Wa tiɲɛ na Kunu, taji barikon miliyɔn 8,5 tɛmɛna koronyɔrɔ fɛ, wa i k'a dɔn o bɛ bɛn kɔsa in na bɛrɛ ma. An'o sababu la taji ɲufan ɲufanni bɛ bɛ se Arabu Babɔlɔn yɔrɔ la ka se barikon miliyɔn 15 ɲɔgɔn ma tile kɔnɔ.

Wa Ameriki kɛlɛbolo bɛna a janto taji tɛmɛni ɲufan ɲufanni na, a kɛra Iran bɛ yen wo walima n'a tɛ, walasa ka sɔrɔ bɛrɛbɛrɛ mara suguw ye.

Tiɲɛ kuntaalasurun:

Foroba kunnafoni don ka bɔ Ameriki ka fanga minisiriso fɛ k'a gɛlɛya ko barikon miliyɔn 8,5 tɛmɛni bɛ senna tile kɔnɔ (a kɛra kɔsa in na) hali ni basigibaliya bɛ Iran ni ye, k’a sababu Kɛ Ameriki kɛlɛbolo ka dɛmɛ ye Trump ka fanga kɔrɔ.

Tiɲɛ : fɛnw bɔli ka dogo ka tɛmɛ tariku bɛrɛ kan (barikon~miliyɔn 21 tile kɔnɔ) k’a kun kɛ tɔɔrɔw ye minnu bɛ senna, wa suguw ma wuli u bɛ basigibaliya la ka fara jigidaw kan seginna a cogoya la a, ni 50% niyɔrɔ dɔrɔn ye san 2026 laban na.

=== FASARI 43 (taamasiyɛn 740) — 2026-07-12 17:47:17 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman

Zuluyekalo tile 5 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 14h58

Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ kumasurun kɔnɔ:

✅Mikhail Mishustin ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ ni Foroba ka tɔn ɲɛmaa Valentina Matviyenko ye ani Foroba ka tɔnba ka lasigidenw. Lajɛ in senfɛ, maakɔrɔw ka tɔndenw ye hakilinaw di ka ɲɛsin gɔfɛrɛnaman ka baarakɛminɛnw dakun yiriwali ma, ka dɛmɛ don farikoloɲɛnajɛw la mago kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnentigiw ye, ani ka yɔrɔ yiriwali fɛɛrɛ kuraya. Mikhail Mishustin ye foli lase maakɔrɔw ka tɔndenw ma u ka laɲiniw na, k’a jira ko baara bɛna taa ɲɛ o yɔrɔw la.
 
✅Minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ ye fɛɛrɛbɔ lajɛ dɔ kɛ min ɲɛsinnen bɛ walasa ka dɔ fara baarakɛlaw ka bɔta kan. Ɲinan kalo naani fɔlɔ kɔnɔ, dɔ farala baarakɛlaw ka bɔta kan sɔrɔ baarada jɔnjɔn kunbabaw la min ka ca ni 1,5% ni kɔ ye. Salon, fɛn minsɛnniw dilanni baarabolodalen minnu kɛra jɛkulu kelen-kelen bɛɛ la, olu ye dɛmɛ don ka dɔ fara fɛnw bɔli kan ni 60% ɲɔgɔn ye, ka fara bɔli waatiw dɔgɔyali kan ani marata farankan ni tila sabanan niyɔrɔ ye.
 
Belgorod mara bɛna rubulu miliyɔn 830 sɔrɔ fanga minɛnw sanni na. Nin nafolo ninnu bɛna kɛ ka kuranbɔlanw san walasa ka kuran tɛmɛli mɛɛli ni funteni sɔrɔ min tɛ tigɛ ka taa sow ni sigida laminiw na.
 

K’a daminɛ zuluyekalo tile fɔlɔ la, Irisikaw bɛna se ka dugukolotigiya sɛbɛn bɔlɔlɔ kan ni biometriki kunnafoniw ye. Nin bɛna a to ka dugukolow tɔgɔ sɛbɛnni baara kɛli nɔgɔya ani ka sira kɛ ka labɔli kɛ walasa ka baarakɛɲɔgɔnya kun fila lakana. Ni nin baara in kɛli, Biometriki kunnafoni dannayalenw ka kan ka di. A bɛ se ka kɛ Waribon minnu dabɔra a kama walima ni mɔgɔ tɔgɔ sɛbɛn baara tilennen fɛ. Wa ka na tɔgɔsɛbɛn na o ma dabila a bɛ sen na.

💰 Ka Ameriki sɛnɛkɛlaw dɛmɛ ani ka baarakɛyɔrɔ 125.000 kɛ ka labɔli kɛ

=== FASARI 49 (taamasiyɛn 778) — 2026-07-06 12:59:43 CEST ===
Degeliw daminɛna San kura kama : Sinuwa jamana ka hɔrɔnya kɛlɛbolo kuluw bɛ kɛlɛba dege walasa ka kɛlɛcɛ ŋanaw dilan.

Zanwuyekalo tile 4, san 2026 20:27

Zanwuyekalo tile 4, Kɛlɛ degeliw daminɛna san kura in kama Sinuwa jamanadenw ka hɔrɔnya kɛlɛbolo fan bɛɛ kɔnɔ. Kɛlɛcɛw ni kɛlɛɲɛmaakuluw ye welekan jaabi joona, k’u ɲɛsin kɛlɛ degelikɛnɛw ma walasa ka dege gɛlɛnw kɛ ni kɛlɛ labɛnni dafalen ye, ka kɛlɛcɛ ŋanaw dilan u bɛɛ lajɛlen na minnu bɛ se ka kɛlɛ cogoba ani ka se sɔrɔ kɛlɛw la.

San kura kalan don fɔlɔ la, Sinuwa jamana ka hɔrɔnya kɛlɛbolo ka kulu 79nan kɛlɛbolo ka kulu dɔ ye kɛlɛ kɛnɛ na delgeliw dafalen kɛ kɛlɛkɛnɛ kalanso dɔ la. Kɛlɛcɛw ni kɛlɛɲɛmaakulu ye mɔgɔ kelen-kelen ni kuluw ka kalansen tan ni kɔ waleya minnu ɲɛfɔlen don kalanbolodalen kɔnɔ, O ye dɔ fara u ka se kan k'u yɛrɛlabɛn ani ka jaabi di kɛlɛ kɛnɛ kan.

Kɔgɔji nɛrɛman dankan la, Kɔgɔji kɛlɛkurun Nanchang, ni jɛ-ka-baara ye ni kɛlɛkurun Xining ani Weifang ye, o ye ɲɛminɛ, ani mɛrɛnkɛlanw ni farataaw wɛrɛw, degeliw tun ye marifaci ye ni kɔgɔji ŋɔlɛw ye kɔgɔji kɔnɔ ani dankanda la, ani marifa kisɛ ɲɛnamaw degeliw ni marifakisɛ yɛrɛkɛtaw ye minnu bɛ se ka nɔɔnikɛlanw kɛlɛ. I n'a fɔ u ye jɛ-ka-baara kɛ cogo minna ni sanfɛla fanga fana ye walasa ka cogoya degeliw kɛ sanfɛla ni kɔgɔji ɲufanniw kama, walasa ka kɛlɛcɛw ka seko kɔrɔbɔ ka bɔ degeli la ka taa kɛlɛ la.

Degeli tile fɔlɔ la, kelen kelenw bɛɛ ye wele da ka kɛlɛ degeli daminɛ san kura in kama. K’a ta Gobi cɛncɛn fugan kan ka taa a bila kungo tulenbaw kɔnɔ, k’a ta kulufara jikuruma la ka taa a bila Sinuwa jamana worodugu Kɔgɔji dankanda la, kɛlɛɲɛmaakuluw ni kɛlɛcɛw y'u folonkonto ka don ni farifoni ye degeliw la ani kɛlɛ labɛnw na. Kɛlɛ kɛnɛ degelikɛyɔrɔw falen tun bɛ lamaga la.

Pankurunw ni dugukolo-ni-sanfɛ-misili kuluw ye baara kɛ, ani radariw minnu tugulen don sanfɛla fanga la, ani ka fara bolofara wɛrɛw kan, jɛɲɔgɔnya degeliw minnu kɛra ni Pankurun suguya caman ye, Walasa k’u ka kɛlɛ baara ɲɛdɔnw kakaya kɛlɛ kɛnɛ sigida la min ye tiɲɛ ye.

Mobili bolifɛnko kulu ye min tugulen don minɛnw dɛmɛ fanga la min ye jɛɲɔgɔnya kɛ degeliw la o y'a sinsin yɔrɔbaw ni gɛlɛyaw kan dɛmɛ tali baara la waati nɛnɛ gɛlɛnw na. Wa o tun ye kɛlɛ kɛcogo degeliw tiɲɛ ye, yɔrɔla ka kɛlɛɲɛmaakuluw ni kɛlɛcɛw ka kɔrɔtɔko jaabi sekow yiriwa sigida gɛlɛnw na.

Wa San kura kalan don fɔlɔ la, kɛlɛɲɛmaakuluw ni Yunnan polisiw ka kɛlɛcɛkulu ye baara kɛ minnu ye maramafɛntigiw ye degeli kɛrɛnkɛrɛnw na ko caman na, ani fɛn min bɛ ka bɔ o la kisi baaraw kɔrɔtɔko tuma na ani jatigɛwale kɛlɛli, minnu tugulen don kɛlɛ degeli taasira la walasa ka gosili fanga dɔ dilan min tɛ magaya.

Wa misili fanga y'a ka fanga carin taasira damadɔw fɛ ka taa a ka kɛlɛyɔrɔw la, Wa a y’u tila-tila kɛlɛ degeliw kɛyɔrɔw la minnu tɛ kelen ye. Wa a ye baara kɛ o waati kelen na degeli tiɲɛw na yɔrɔ jɔnjɔnw ni gɛlɛyabaw kan i n’a fɔ marabolow cɛla tikitakiw ani cunkanni telima, O ye dɔ fara baaraw kɛli dafalenya kan kelenw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ ani maramafɛnw ni minɛnw ani bɔlɔlɔ lakana labɛnw.

Zuwɛnkalo tile 28 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 17h32

Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun in kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ kumasurun kɔnɔ:

Sanfɛkalanso lakanayɔrɔw bɛna jɔ ka ban Nizhny Novgorod, Ufa, Chelyabinsk ani Yuzhno-Sakhalinsk. Gofɛrɛnaman bɛna kɛrɛnkɛrɛnni kɛ min ka ca rubulu miliyari 31,5 ni kɔ ye nin mago in kama.

taama wɔɔrɔnan dafara ka bɔ bɔlɔlɔ kalata la Irisi jamana bɛɛ la foroba kɛnɛw sugandili kama. Sendonni kɛra kalata la, o min kɛra farancɛ kɔnɔ ka bɔ Awirilikalo tile 21 la ka taa se Zuwɛnkalo tile 12 ma, min ka ca ni mɔgɔ miliyɔn 15,8 ye. Sigidamɔgɔw ye foroba kɛnɛw sugandi u ka maraw kɔnɔ o min bɛna yiriwa ka ban san nata la.

Gofɛrɛnaman bɛna rubulu miliyari 60 kɛrɛnkɛrɛn i n'a fɔ juruw minnu ka nɔgɔn sɛnɛkɛlaw ma. Nin juruw bɛ di bɔlikɛlaw ma bɔli kama ani sɛnɛfɛnw bɔta dilannikɛlaw ma, ani baganw ka furaw, ni anzimuw, ni baganbalow, ani balo farankan taw ma.

Sariyasen dɔ bɛna sigi senkan walasa ka keguya dilannen fɛɛrɛw labɛn Irisi jamana kɔnɔ. Wa tiɲɛ na Gofɛrɛnaman ye sariyasun baarabolodalen dɔ di nin mago in kama wasabulon ma. Nin sariya in bɛ hakilina jɔnjɔnw don sariyasunba kɔnɔ.n'i ma o dɔn o de ye: keguya dilannen ye ani misali jɔnjɔnbaw. Wa a keguya dilannen misaliw yiriwabagaw bɛna kɛ sigidamaaw ye minnu labɛnnen don ka jamana ka dɛmɛ sɔrɔ.

=== FASARI 57 (taamasiyɛn 816) — 2026-07-02 19:07:22 CEST ===
Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmaa, kɛlɛbolo ɲɛmaaba Assime Goïta, ye bi wula fɛ, Cadi jamanakuntigi ka ciden kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen, Sofa Mama Idris Debi itno bisimila. fatime Algine Garfa, n’o ye minisiri ciden ye jamana kɔkankow minisiri fɛ, n’o ye Farafinna jɛkafɔ ni Cadi jamana kɔkanmɔgɔw ka baara ye, o nana foroba weleli cikan lase ka bɔ Cadi jamana jamanakuntigi fɛ, k'a ka taa a sen don diɲɛ jiko lajɛba la min bolodara ka kɛ Njamena zuluyekalo tile 15 ani 16 san 2026. Nin lajɛ in bɛna mɔgɔw lajɛ walasa ka jaabi bɛnnen sɔrɔ Farafinna gɛlɛyaw kan ji sɔrɔli, ɲɛnamaya sɔrɔyɔrɔ. Cadi minisiri ye kuma ta Sira bacili fɛ lajɛ in kɔfɛ. An sera Bamakɔ bi, an bɛ ka cikan ta ka bɔ Cadi jamana ɲɛmaa, jamana ɲɛmaa, Sofa Mohamed Idriss Debi itno fɛ, ka taa a jɛɲɔgɔnna fɛ, Mali jamanakuntigi, jamanakuntigi kɛlɛbolo ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta.

An ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ ni jamanakuntigi ye, ka welekan lase a ma ka bɔ a ka Cadi jamanaɲɔgɔn fɛ, walasa a ka se ka a sen don Farafinna jiko forobajɛkulu dɔ la min bɛna kɛ Njamena, Cadi jamana faaba la, zuluyekalo tile 15 ni 16.

Ko jɔnjɔn jumɛnw de bɛ sen na nin forobajɛkulu in kɔnɔ?
I n’a fɔ aw b’a dɔn cogo min na, ko jɔnjɔn minnu bɛ nin forobajɛkulu in kɔnɔ, olu ye ko an ka Sahel jamanaw ka gɛlɛya kelenw bɛ ɲɔgɔn na, a kɛra sigidako ye wo, a kɛra waati cogoya ye wo,
wa o de kosɔn a tun nafa ka bon nin jamana ninnu ka fara ɲɔgɔn kan walasa ka miiri fɛɛrɛw la minnu bɛ sabati ani minnu bɛ kɛ ɲɔgɔn fɛ. O la tiɲɛ na, a nafa tun ka bon kosɛbɛ Cadi jamana ɲɛmaa k’a ka Mali ɲɔgɔn wele ka na nin jɛkafɔ in na
min bɛna na ni fura jɛlenw ye an balimaw ka Saheli jamanaw ma.

Jamanakuntigi dusu tun ka di kosɛbɛ, wa a sen tun bɛ ji sɔrɔli ko la kosɛbɛ
ani gɛlɛya minnu bɛ an ka dugukolotigiw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ.
Jamanadenw bɛ ka kan fe kɛ.
Yala nin ye jateminɛba ye gɛlɛyaw bɛɛ kan minnu bɛ Sahel kan bi wa?
Tiɲɛ don, i n’a fɔ Mali jamana ɲɛmaa y’a fɔ cogo min na ka tɛmɛ, ji ye nafolo nafamaba ye, o de kama, gɛlɛya don Saheli jamanaw bɛɛ bolo, o de kama, an ka kan k’a ɲɛnabɔ ɲɔgɔn fɛ.

=== FASARI 58 (taamasiyɛn 819) — 2026-07-02 19:04:16 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Kuluba | Zuluyekalo tile fɔlɔ, san 2026 | #Kɔkanko

KULUBA: Jamanakuntigi Goïta ye Sofa Déby ka ciden kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen bisimila

Jamanakuntigi ye Ɲɛmaa Fatime Aldjineh Garfa bisimila, n’o ye Cadi minisiri ka ciden ye, ani jamanakuntigi Mahamat Idriss Déby Itno ka ciden kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen, Kuluba masaso la, arabadon. Ɲɛmaa Garfa ye welesɛbɛnba dɔ ta ka taa Farafinna jiko forobajɛkulu la min bolodara ka kɛ zuluyekalo tile 15 ni 16 san 2026, Njamena.

Kuluba masaso ye mɔgɔ duuru ka cidenjɛkulu sɔrɔ arabadon zuluyekalo tile fɔlɔ san 2026, n’o ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ Ɲɛmaa Fatime Aldjineh Garfa bolo, n’o ye minisiri ciden ye Cadi jamana kɔkankow minisiri fɛ, min bɛ Farafinna jamanaw ka donko ni Cadi jamana kɔkankow ɲɛnabɔ. A kɛlen ka kɛ Cadi jamanakuntigi ka ciden kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen ye, n’o ye Sofa Mahamat Idriss Déby Itno ye, a bisimila jama la, a ka bonyali kɛlɛbolo ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta fɛ, n’o ye wuliliko ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, jamanakuntigi.

Lajɛba in kɛra jamanakuntigi ka ladilikɛla ŋanaw ɲɛkɔrɔ : jamana kɔkankow minisiri, tɔnba ɲɛmɔgɔba ka tɔnba, jamanakuntigi ka minisiri dannayabaga, kɔkanko ladilikɛla, ani kunnafoniko ladilikɛla min bɛ jamanakuntigi fɛ.

Ɲɛmaa Fatime Aldjineh Garfa ye a yɛrɛ ka cikan ta ka bɔ Sofa Mahamat Idriss Déby Itno fɛ ka taa a bila a ka Mali jɛɲɔgɔn ma. Nin cikan in kɔnɔko la : welekan dɔ kɛra jamanakuntigi Goïta ye walasa a ka se ka a sen don Farafinna jiko forobajɛkulu la, min bɛna kɛ Njamena, Cadi jamana faaba la, zuluyekalo tile 15 ni 16 san 2026.

Lajɛ in kɔfɛ, Cadi jamana ka ciden kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen ye ko minnu ɲɛfɔ, minnu bɛ nin kɔkanko baara in kɔnɔ. Forobajɛkulu in nafa bɛ bɔ dugukoloko ni waati cogoya jɛlen na : Sahel jamanaw bɛɛ lajɛlen bɛ sigida ni jiko gɛlɛya kelenw sɔrɔ, minnu bɛ juguya waati jiginni fɛ.

"An ka Sahel jamanaw ka gɛlɛya kelenw bɛ ɲɔgɔn fɛ, a kɛra sigidako ye wo, a kɛra waati cogoya ye wo. O de kama, a nafa tun ka bon nin jamana ninnu ka fara ɲɔgɔn kan walasa ka hakilina falenfalen kɛ furaw kan minnu bɛ sabati ani minnu bɛ kɛ ɲɔgɔn fɛ", o ye Ɲɛmaa Fatime Aldjineh Garfa ka ɲɛfɔli ye, min ye Cadi jamanakuntigi ka ciden kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen ye.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 59 (taamasiyɛn 823) — 2026-07-02 16:48:35 CEST ===
Se sɔrɔli minnu kɛra ani sɛnɛ baarada yiriwali ni togodaw Sinuwa jamana na

Nin y'a kuncɛlen ye tiɲɛ na ani jateden jɔnjɔnw minnu kofɔlen kuntaalasurunya la:

Suman kisɛw bɔli : A ye jatebɔ kura dan dɔ sigi senkan min sera yuan miliyari 1429,8 ma, o tɛmɛna yuan tiriliyɔni 1,4 hakɛ kan san filanan in na ka tugu ɲɔgɔnna.

Baganw ka bɔli : Sogo bɔli sera (lɛw, misiw, baganmisɛnniw, ani kɔnɔw) tɔni miliyɔn 107,52 ma, ni do farali san o san ta kan o niyɔrɔ ye 4,2% ye.

Balo lakana: Suguya ɲɛ bugunatigɛ se sɔrɔli sɛgɛsɛgɛli ani sɛnɛfɛn bɔtaw lakana o niyɔrɔ sera 98% ma.

Faantanya kɛlɛli ani baara sɔrɔli: kɔkanna baarakɛla minnu bɛ ta baara la o kundama basigira Ka bɔ faantanya dancɛ la caman yɔrɔ min ka ca ni maa miliyɔn 32 ye.

Fɛɛrɛbɔ ni Sɛnɛ keguma:

Dɔnniya ɲɛtaa dɛmɛni ani fɛɛrɛbɔ o niyɔrɔ sera sɛnɛko la, a ka ca ka tɛmɛ 64% kan.

 Masinw hakɛ bɛɛ lajɛlen sera Sɛnɛfɛnw kama ka taa 76,7% la.

Sɛnɛkɛ doroniw jateda tɛmɛna pankurun 300.000 kan.

Ɲɛni ni tigiyali: Togodala dugukoloko dɔnɔni samana ka se san 30 wɛrɛ farankan ma, mara 7 kɔnɔ minnu bɛ ka kɔrɔbɔ, o bɛ kɛ sababu ye ka dɔnɔni basigi kafo sɛnɛkɛla denbayaw bolo min ka ca ni miliyɔn 25 ye.

Kibaruya min bɔra Mali foroba jabaraniso la san 2026 baarakɛnafolo bilasirali lajɛba kan Mali kɔnɔ, min ɲɛsinnen bɛ san caman baarakɛnafolo ni sɔrɔko waleyali sɛbɛn (2027-2029) jirali ma Fasodenw ka tɔnba (CNT) la.

Tomiba minnu nana Jateminɛ in kɔnɔ:

Baarakɛnafolo ni sɔrɔko jateminɛ : Lajɛ in kun tun ye ka jamana ka wariko siraw ɲɛfɔ san saba nataw kɔnɔ (2027-2029) ani ka kuma sɔrɔko jiralanw kan i n’a fɔ yiriwali hakɛ, nafolosɔrɔbaliya, ani baarakɛnafolo dɛsɛ.

Sɔrɔko ni nafolo minisiri (Alusenu Sanu) ka kumaw : Minisiri ye fɛɛrɛ kerunnen jira walasa ka sɔrɔ yiriwali kɛ tiɲɛ ye, ka fara gɛlɛyabaw ni faratibaw jirali kan, Olu minnu ni mara b’a ɲini ka minnu ɲɛnabɔ.

Baarada fɔlɔw: Baro in sinsinnen na baarada ɲɛnamaw kan i n’a fɔ lakana ni lafasali, sɛnɛ (o min bɛ jate jujɔn yiriwali kama), ani fanga ni kuran baarada yiriwali walasa ka faso ka dilannikɛyɔrɔw dɛmɛ.

=== FASARI 65 (taamasiyɛn 848) — 2026-07-02 01:26:37 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamana
Hong Kong gofɛrɛnaman y’a ka wariko baarakɛnafolo laseli kɛ san 2026-2027 nafolosɔrɔta la.

Laseliba min nana nin kunnafoni in kɔnɔ:

Duuru fɔlɔ bolodalen: Hong Kong b’a fɛ k’a ka san duuru bolodalen fɔlɔ labɛn walasa ka bɛn ani k'a dafa ni cogoya tilennen ye ni Sinuwa jamana ka san duuru bolodalen 15nan ye.

Sɔrɔ yiriwali : Hong Kong ka sɔrɔ yiriwali bɛɛ lajɛlen sera salon 3,5% ma, wa a bɛ ka bɔ jigiyaw la ɲinan yiriwali bɛna kɛ 2,5% ni 3,5% cɛ.

Nafolodonni kokurabɔli kɔnɔ ani keguya dilannen na : Hong Kong dolari miliyɔn 220 bɛna kɛrɛnkɛrɛn walasa Ka faso ka kokurabɔ-yɔrɔ fɔlɔ sigi sen kan walasa ka dilanni kɛ dugukoloba kɔkan, ni ka fara Hong Kong dolari miliyari 10 bilasirali nafolo dɔ daminɛ kan walasa ka dɛmɛ don baaradaw la keguya dilannen, ani robow, ni dilannikɛyɔrɔ minnu bɛ ka dilan.

=== FASARI 66 (taamasiyɛn 853) — 2026-07-01 16:16:53 CEST ===
Ikɛrɛni: Wari sɛbɛnw bɔli n'a tiɲɛcogo

Kuma surun na ani cogoya tilennen:

1. Sɛbɛnw bɔli ni bulu tiɲɛni (Kurayali kɛcogo kɔrɔlen) .

Sɛbɛnw bɔli: Waribon sanfɛla ka sɛbɛnbɔlan o bɛ bulu kuraw dɔrɔn bɔ i n'a fɔ falenlan bulu tiɲɛnenw ni kɔrɔlenw kama o min foosira ka bɔ sugu la.

Tiɲɛni: bulu kɔrɔw ni faralenw bɛ mugumugu, k'a yɛlɛma ka taa npuulu kartɔnma dɔ kɔnɔ, k'a jeni.

O kɔlɔlɔ: A tɛ dɔ
Fara Wari hakɛ kan jamana kɔnɔ, ani min bɛ da o kan a tɛ nafolosɔrɔbaliya si sababuya.

2. "Warimisɛn bɔli" Marali (Falen farati).
Tiɲɛ : tuma minna ni jamana dɛsɛra k’a ka musakaw dafa (baarakɛnafolow dɛsɛ), i k'a dɔn tiɲɛ na a mago bɛ "sɛbɛn bɔli la" warimisɛn kura (tuma caman na a bɛ kɛ masin ta ye, jatedenw dɔrɔn de bɛ jatew kɔnɔ) walasa ka saraw kɛ n'o ye  ani juruw k’a sɔrɔ bɔli tiɲɛman ma a kunbɛn walima sanu dɔ.

Kɔlɔlɔ : Wari bɛ caya sugu kɔnɔ an'o b'a nafa dɔgɔya, o la sɔngɔw bɛ kɔrɔta (funun).

Diɲɛ Waribon ka kunnafoni kuncɛlen : Nafolosɔrɔbaliya jigiya dɔgɔyara Ikɛrɛni jamana na, o kɔrɔ ye ko jamana mago tɛna jɔ fɛɛrɛ filanan na (warimisɛn bɔli k’a sɔrɔ a ma dɛmɛ sɔrɔ walasa ka dɛsɛ in dafa), o to yen o kɔrɔ ye k'a bɛna wasa ni fɛɛrɛ fɔlɔ dɔrɔn de ye n'i ma o dɔn o de ye kurayali ye tuma ni tuma bulu tiɲɛnenw na

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 67 (taamasiyɛn 857) — 2026-07-01 08:04:55 CEST ===
Trump bɛ ka kɛlɛ baarakɛnafolo belebeleba labɛn tariku kura kɔnɔ san 2027 kama.

Kuma surun na, tiɲɛ min bɛ nin ŋaniya kɔfɛ, o bɛ munumunu tomikunba saba de kan:

1. Kɔnɔkow ni laɲini jɔnjɔn :

Ameriki jamanakuntigi Donald Trump b’a ɲini ka lafasali baarakɛnafolo kɔrɔta san 2027 kama ka se tiriliyɔni 1,5 ɲɔgɔn ma (k'a sɔrɔ a tun ye tiriliyɔni 1 ɲɔgɔn ye sàn 2026 kɔnɔ). Laɲini tɛ ka dɔ fara kɛlɛcɛw jateda dɔrɔn, Ayi, ka kɛlɛ-fɛɛrɛko tarabasoli kuraya ka misili-kɔlɔsilikɛlan jɔ min yiriwalen don ("Sanu poroli")  ka dɔ fara waridonni kan sanfɛla maramafenw na,ani doroniw, ani ka kɔgɔji-kɛlɛbolow kuraya walasa k'a ɲɛsin ɲɔgɔndan fangaw ma i n'a fɔ Sinuwa jamana ni Irisi jamana.

2. Nafolo sɔrɔcogo (wari in bɛna bɔ min?):
Laɲini in bɛ banban bɔyɔrɔ kunba fila kan i k'a dɔn tiɲɛ na o bɛ sɔsɔliba lawuli:

Saalenw: Ameriki jamana fanga b’a fɛ ka baara kɛ ni seginta kunbabaw ye walasa ka saalen kura jaakuya dunaw ka fɛnw kan walasa ka kɛlɛbolo musaka.

Kɔnɔnata tigɛli : Baarakɛnafolo sigida dugubakɔnɔmaaya baarada baarakɛnafolo bɛna dɔgɔya (i n’a fɔ kɛnɛya, kalan, ani sigidamɔgɔw dɛmɛni) ni niyɔrɔ ye o ka surun 10% ɲɔgɔn na walasa k’o nafolo yɛlɛma ka taa Lakana minisiriso (Pentagon) la. 

3. Se fɛrɛlen bɛ Wasabulon kɔnɔ (Congress) :
K'a dɔn k'a fɔ ko Jama ka tɔn (Trump ka Tɔn) o bɛ caman mara  ta wasabulon kɔnɔ ani maakɔrɔw la, k'a dɔn k'a fɔ ko nin sariyasun in tɛmɛni fɛrɛ ani ka sɔn a ma o bɛ jate kɔrɔtaba ye kosɛbɛ, hali n’a y’a sɔrɔ sinaya jugu bɛ yen ka bɔ Bɛɛjɛfangatigiw fɛ k’a sababu kɛ dugubakɔnɔmaaya ka baarakɛnafolow dɔgɔyali ye.

=== FASARI 69 (taamasiyɛn 866) — 2026-06-30 18:11:00 CEST ===
Wuluwuluji Saniyali baara Turiki jamana na

Baarabolodalen in kuncɛlen filɛ nin ye i kɔrɔ tomi jujɔn 4 kan:

Ji lasegincogo kokura : Afyonkarahisar yɔrɔ bɛ wuli k'a jɔ ni mɛtɛrɛ kubik 44000 Furakɛli ye tile kɔnɔ ka bɔ sokɔnɔ wuluwulujiw la ani k'a kona walasa a ka se ka ɲɛ sɔnni kama.

Kuran saniyalen : jateminɛna k'a dɔn k'a fɔ ko sɛnɛkɛdugukolow bɛ sɔrɔ yɔrɔkɔrɔtalenw na, ji bɛ digi ka taa a ma ni tile kuran jɔyɔrɔ ka baara ye min fanga ye megawati 1,6 ye, walasa ka kuran kɛ ka labɔli kɛ fu an'a saniyalen.

Nafa fila sɔrɔli sɛnɛ kama : Baarabolodalen in bɛ sɔnni kɛ dunmu 9050 la (o bɛ bɛn ɛkitɛri 905 ma), wa ji saniyalen bɛ dɔ fara sɛnɛfɛnw sɔrɔta kan k'a dɔn k'a fɔ ko azote ni fosifɔri (nɔgɔ yɛrɛyɛrɛ) bɛ sɔrɔ a yɛrɛ la.

Ɲɛmɔgɔya : Nin bɛ jate baarabolodalenba Turiki jamana kɔnɔ ka bɔ yɔrɔla dugukolo kɛnɛw minnu bɛ sɔn ka bɔ wuluwuluji saniyalenw na, wa a bɛ misali tiɲɛ dɔ jira walasa ka sigida lakana ani ka ji kɛ ka labɔli kɛ.

=== FASARI 70 (taamasiyɛn 873) — 2026-06-29 12:17:44 CEST ===
Kremlin Kibaruyaw

Lajɛ dɔ senfɛ ni minisiriɲɛmɔgɔso ni taji tɔnw ka lasigidenw ye, jamanakuntigi y’a fɔ k'a gɛlɛya ko minɛli dafalen ka kan kɛ ni taji labɔli waatibolodalenw ye  sɛnɛfɛnw bɔlikɛlaw ye.

Wa a y’a fara a kan ko:"Ferekew ma wuli ye jɔlen don bolifɛn sofaw ni tɔnw ɲɛ u bɛɛ ka kan; Ka fara nimisa kan, taji feerekɛyɔrɔw bɛ ka yeli kɛ ɲɔgɔn koronni juguw la, i n'a fɔ a bɛ gɛlɛya tuma bɛɛ ka suguya bɛnnen sɔrɔ ka bɔ tajiw la.

Sisan ko : Jɔrɔnanko min bɛ ka bonya fanga lakanani na, k’a sɔrɔ taji sɔrɔyɔrɔba bɛ ka jigin ka se barikon miliyɔn 357 ma, o ye hakɛ ye min bɛ jate « farati yɔrɔ » ye dugukoloko fɛɛrɛ basigibaliya cogoya kɛli la.

Fɛɛrɛ jɔyɔrɔw:

Donald Trump: o ka yeta bɛ jɔ a b’a ye k'a fɔ ko taji sɔngɔw kɔrɔtali, o ye waati fɛrɛlen ye walasa ka Ameriki ka fanga baarada sinsin, hali n'o kɔlɔlɔw bɛ sannikɛlaw jufa kan.

=== FASARI 83 (taamasiyɛn 1050) — 2026-06-22 01:14:16 CEST ===
Ba dilannen janyalenba Turiki jamana na ani min bɛ ɲɛnamaya jolisira jira Anatolia Worodugu-Kɔrɔn Baarabolodalen (GAP) kama.

Tiɲɛ jɔnjɔnw ni kunnafoniw minnu bɔra o kunnafoni na, olu filɛ nin ye k’a surunya:

Janya n'a sangali: Jibolisira in janya bɛ se sisan kilomɛtɛrɛ 221 ma, wa a ka jan ka tɛmɛ Suez Jibolisira kan ni kilomɛtɛrɛ 28 ye, an'a bɛ bɛn Panama Jibolisira hakɛ siɲɛ saba ma. A bɛ ka bɔ jigiyaw la a janya ka se kilomɛtɛrɛ 265 ma baarabolodalen in dafa waati n'a dafara.

Ji bɔyɔrɔ: Ji bɛ woyo ka don Jibolisira kɔnɔ ka bɔ Atatürk barazi la, min bɛ balo ka bɔ Efrate baji la, ka tɛmɛ wo kunbaba fila fɛ walasa ka se Şanlıurfa ani Mardin wolon ɲumanw na.

Sɛnɛ nɔfɛkow : Ji sɔrɔli ye dɛmɛba don sɛnɛfɛnw suguya caman na (i n’a fɔ kɔɔri, kaba, alikaama, ani sunbalasiyɔ, ka fara nakɔfɛnw ni jiridenw kan) ani ka dɔ fara fɛn bɔtaw kan, o kɛra sababu ye sɛnɛkɛlaw bɛ se ka kanni kɛ fo siɲɛ saba san kɔnɔ.

Kɛnɛ sɔnta: Baarabolodalen in laɲini ye ka kɛnɛ bɛɛ lajɛlen min min bɛ se dunmu miliyɔn 2,47 ɲɔgɔn na (O bɛ bɛn hectare 247.000 ma). Sɔnni jɔ dyɛlɛ la kaban o min datuguli kɛ dunmu 900.000 ɲɔgɔn na (o bɛ bɛn hectare 90.000 ɲɔgɔn ma)  fo ka na Se sisan ma.  

Sɔrɔko seginni : kɛnɛ minta in bɛ dɛmɛ don sisan ka Turki lira miliyari 2,2 don sɔrɔko la san o san, k’a sɔrɔ kabini a baara daminɛna, o baarabolodalen in segintaw bɛɛ lajɛlen ye min tɛmɛna Turki lira miliyari 14,7 kan.

Ɲɛfɔli minsɛnma: Baarabolodalen: Sɔnni labɛnw kurayali (badawolo numanbolo fan fɛ) ka ɲɛsin Kartalkaya jitɔnyɔrɔ ma min bɛ Kahramanmaraş dugu kɔnɔ.

Nafa : ka duunamu sɛnɛ dugukolo 137.560 sɔn, ani ka 40% labɔli kɛ ka bɔ ji tununenw na.

Kɔlɔsili: Baarabolodalen in bolodara kabini san 2025 (a daminɛ na, a musaka tun ye lira miliyari 2,5 ye), wa sisan laseli (zuwɛnkalo tile 17, san 2026) ye baarakɛcogo walima musaka kura ye. Nin tɛ weeleda nkalonma ye, ayi kunukurun don ka bɔ"Ji bɔnɛni fu" baarabolodalen na.

✅Fɛnw ni baarakɛminɛnw jɛnsɛnni suguw ni bɔlɔlɔ ni Jatedenya baarakɛminɛnw fɛ sisan, o bɛ 5% ni kɔ bɔ sigida ka bɔta bɛɛlajɛlen na jamana ye ka kaban. Mikhail Mishustin ye fɛɛrɛbɔ lajɛ min ɲɛmɔgɔya kɛ, a kumana Jatedenya bɔlɔlɔ sigidaw dayɛlɛli n’u yiriwali ɲɛtaa kan.

✅Ka marabolow dɛmɛ sigidako gɛlɛnw ɲɛnabɔli la, o bɛ jate gɔfɛrɛnaman ka ɲɛtaa kunba ye. ✅Kaare ni tramway kuraw bɛna don Irisi maraw la. Rubulu miliyari 11 ɲɔgɔn bɛna bila foroba bolifɛnw yiriwali kama mara naani kɔnɔ.

✅Gofɛrɛnaman ka tɔndenw y’u sen don diɲɛ sɔrɔko forobajɛkulu ka fɛɛrɛbɔ la san 2026.

Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw kumasurunya kɔnɔ:

Mikhail Mishustin ye kuma ta CIPR-2026 lajɛba 11nan na Nizhny Novgorod, wa a ye yiriwali tigɛlenw fana dɔn Risi ka tɔnw fɛ o jirali in na. San wɔɔrɔ tɛmɛnenw kɔnɔ, Dɔ fara la fɛɛrɛbɔ dɔnniyaw baarada niyɔrɔ kan Ka bɔ sigida ka fɛn bɔta mumɛ na jamana ye, wa jamana kɔnɔ waleyakɛlanw ni baarakɛminɛnw feereli cayara ka se siɲɛ 4,5 ma.

Ani Gofɛrɛnaman sɔnna baara bolodalen dɔ ma  sirako lakanani yiriwali fɛɛrɛ in waleyali kama fo ka se san 2030 ma, an'o min kɔnɔ, siraba minnu bɛ tɛmɛn siraw kan, olu jɔli fana sen bɛ o la, Ani ka dɔ fara taasiraw jateda kan, ani cɛtigɛyɔrɔw, walasa ka ɲɔgɔn koron nɔgɔya dugubaw kɔnɔ, ni ka dan sigi Kasaraw la ani ka baaraw labɛncogo ɲɛ.

Minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ y’a sen don CIS ka gɔfɛrɛnaman ɲɛmɔgɔw ka lajɛ dɔ la, min kɛra Ashgabat. Irisi jamana ka jagokɛta ni CIS jamanaw ye, o cayara ni 6,5% ɲɔgɔn ye Zanwuyekalo–Feburuyekalo san 2026. A ka taama senfɛ, Mikhail Mishustin ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ ni Turkmenistan jamanakuntigi n’a minisiriɲɛmaaso ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, Serdar Berdimuhamedov.

=== FASARI 112 (taamasiyɛn 1918) — 2026-05-19 22:47:20 CEST ===
Mali sɛnɛko minisiriso

*🇲🇱 JAMANA KA DONKO SƐGƐSƐGƐLI DAMINƐNI FOROBA LA SƐNƐKO KALANSOW (CAA), SAN 2026 WAATI 🌾*

📒 Karidon, Mɛkalo tile 17, san 2026, Askia Mohamed cɛmancɛkalanso la Bamakɔ.

Sɛgɛsɛgɛlikɛyɔrɔ 9 la, jamana fan bɛɛ la, mɔgɔ 1026 bɛ ka ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ yɔrɔ 220 sɔrɔli la san 2026 jamana ka donko sɛgɛsɛgɛli la, n’o ye sɛnɛko kalansobaw ye (CAA).

Laseli fɔlɔ in senfɛ, sɛnɛko minisiri, dɔgɔtɔrɔ Ibrahima Samakɛ, n’a jɛɲɔgɔnw ka bɔ baganmara ni jɛgɛko minisiriso la, n’o ye Youba Ba ye; Fasodenw ka kalanko, Amadu Sy Savane; Lamini, Doumbia Mariyam Tangara; ani Baara ni foroba baarakɛlaw, Fassoun Coulibaly.

Jamana ka donko sɛgɛsɛgɛli daminɛni laseli forobayali kan sɛnɛko kalansow (CAA), san 2026 kalan. Sɛnɛ minisiri, n’o ye dɔgɔtɔrɔ Samake ye, ale ye bataki foroko fɔlɔ da wuli, kɔrɔbɔli sɛbɛnnen bɛ min kɔnɔ.

ɲininikɛlaw bɛna sɛbɛnni, jatebɔ ani ɲɛnamayako sɛgɛsɛgɛliw kɛ o don kelen na.

Gofɛrɛnaman ɲɛmaa duuru ninnu ye laadilikan nafamaw di kandidaw n’a kɔlɔsibagaw ma, k’u sinsin nafa kan min bɛ Mali fangatigiba dɔw bila ɲɛɲini sɛgɛsɛgɛliw ni ɲɛɲiniw na, ka jɛlenya dafalen ni lafiya sabatili sinsin.

U ye dusu don ɲininikɛlaw kɔnɔ fana u k’u cɛsiri ka ɲɛtaa sɔrɔ ani k’u yɛrɛ ka cɛsiri jira, o min bɛna kɛ sababu ye ka ɲɛmɔgɔ nataw ka seko ni dɔnko sabati togodala yiriwali siratigɛ la n’u donna jamana baarakɛlaw ka baara la.

Hakilijigin na, sɛnɛko kalansow bɛ Kayes, Koulikoro, Ségou, ani Koutiala maraw la.

=== FASARI 129 (taamasiyɛn 2076) — 2026-05-14 14:32:32 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman

Mɛkalo tile 10 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 17 waati

Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ sɛbɛn kɔnɔ:

✅Dɔnniya duguw Dubna ani Korolev bɛna nafolo sɔrɔ yiriwali bɛɛjɛfanga kama. O dɛmɛn ninnu bɛna kɛ sababu ye ka ɲininikɛyɔrɔw bonya, ka fɛn bɔli yɔrɔw kɛ kura ye, ani ka sigida baarakɛlaw yiriwa.

✅Ɲinan, denmisɛn miliyɔn 5,75 bɛna sababu sɔrɔ ka lafiɲɛbɔ kɛnɛyasow la. Gofɛrɛnaman ye dɔ fara Furakɛlikɛla kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw kan denmisɛnw ka buguw furakɛli yɔrɔw la. Irisi jamana ka mɔgɔw lakanani baarada bɛ buguw saniya cogoya kɔlɔsi. Nɛgɛjurusira dɔ sigira sen kan walasa bangebagaw ka se ka lajɛ kɛ bugu sugandili kan: 8 (800) 250-28-90.

✅ Sisikurun minnu bɛ taa ni mɔgɔw ye, olu ka wotoro kura bɛna don maraw kɔnɔ, wa, layini bɛɛ ka taamasɛbɛnw bɔlɔlɔ kan.

O ɲɛfɔlen don dugu kɔkan nɛgɛsira yiriwali hakilina kɔnɔ fo san 2035, min sɔnna gɔfɛrɛnaman fɛ.

=== FASARI 130 (taamasiyɛn 2089) — 2026-05-14 10:40:57 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Minisiriw ka tɔnba ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ lajɛ gansan na arabadon mɛkalo tile 13 san 2026, a ka hakilijagabɔso kɔnɔ Kuluba masaso kɔnɔ, ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ, n’o ye wuliliko ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, jamanakuntigi.

Minisiriw ka tɔnba ka fɔta (san 2026 mɛkalo tile 13) kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw kuncɛlen filɛ nin ye :

1. Baarakɛda fanw (Saalenw): .

Saalen ɲɛnabɔliba yiriwali n’a sariyaw kurayali ye ani ka dɔ fara a ka hadamadenya seko kan san duuru nataw kɔnɔ walasa ka wariko lajɛcogo ɲuman sabati.

2. Kɛlɛcɛw sigili:

Kolonɛli Dɔgɔtɔrɔ Souleymane Coulibaly sigili ka kɛ kɛlɛcɛw kalanso ɲɛmɔgɔba ye.

3. Marabolo jɛ-ka-baara (AES Sahel): .

Jɛkafɔ ni Burukina Faso ni Nizɛri ye walasa ka jamanadenw ka pankurun sariyaw bɛn ɲɔgɔn ma walasa ka taama ni sɔrɔko jɛɲɔgɔnya nɔgɔya jɛkulu jamanaw cɛ.

 4.Labɛn samiya kama:

Jibolisiraw saniya baaraba dɔ daminɛ Bamakɔ faaba kɔnɔ (a ka ca ni mɛtɛrɛ 348.000 ye) walasa ka sanjiba bali, k’a daminɛ mɛkalo tile 14 na.

5. Sigidamɔgɔw ka fɛɛrɛw (Seliba): .

Ka "saraka baganw feereli labɛn sɔngɔ dɔgɔyalen na" k'a ta mɛkalo tile 20 na ka se tile 27 ma Bamakɔ ni jamanaw kɔnɔ, walasa ka wariko doni nɔgɔya denbayaw kan.

 6. Kɛnɛya ni sigida:

Dengue bana cogoya dɔgɔyali sɛbɛnna.

"Jamanadenw ka kumaɲɔgɔnya" baarabolodalen daminɛni walasa ka bɛn ni sendonni nafaw yiriwa funankɛninw cɛ.

Duniya denbayaw ka donba (mɛkalo tile 15) ni sinsin ye dɔrɔguta kɛlɛli kan ani waati yɛlɛma nɔfɛkow.

=== FASARI 134 (taamasiyɛn 2141) — 2026-05-12 08:09:50 CEST ===
Marɔku suguba dɔ... Munna saga sɔngɔ wulila Fkih Ben Salah sugu tɔgɔba la?

Mɛkalo tile 11, san 2026

A tiɲɛ surunyalen: Saga sɔngɔ wulila Fkih Ben Salah sugu la (suguba dɔ ka bɔ Marɔku) ka da fɛnw bɔli musakaw caya kan, Kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la  Baganbalo sɔngɔw, O wulila kosɛbɛ (saga kun kelen baloli musaka bɛ se dirham 300 ɲɔgɔn ma kalo kɔnɔ).

Ɲɛfɔli teliyalenw : Sisan sɔngɔw : Saga misɛnninw bɛ daminɛ dirham 2000 ɲɔgɔn na, saga cɛmancɛw bɛ daminɛ dirham 3000-5000 ɲɔgɔn na, Wa Saga Ɲuman bɛ se dirham 8000-10000+ ma.

O kunba ye : Dɔ farali Baganbalow sɔngɔ kan, ka fara fɛn wɛrɛw kan i n’a fɔ ja tɛmɛnen, wa o tɛ “sagatigiw ka sugandili” ye.

O nɔfɛko : Jamanadenw ka ɲininiw dɔgɔyali (a kɔlɔsira ko u ma sɔn) ni lahiya seli surunyani ye, ani dɔ farala dirham 300-500 kan ni i y’a suma ni dɔgɔkun tɛmɛnenw ta ye. Dɔw bɛ "cɛmancɛlaw" jalaki.

Mali sɛnɛ: Yiriwali fɛɛrɛ bɛɛjɛfanga dɔ kɛra Nizɛri ɲɛmaaso bolo Mali kɔnɔ san 2021-2030 kɔnɔ, O kɛra lajɛ dɔ senfɛ min kɛra sɔrɔko, sigidako ani laadalakow ɲɛnabɔli jɛkulu fɛ.

Nin ye ɲɛfɔli surun ye o yɔrɔ kolomaw kan:

1. Fɛɛrɛbɔ Laɲiniw
Balo lakana sɔrɔli : Laɲini in kun ye ka Mali kɛ "balo jiginɛ" ye Farafinna tilebinyanfan fɛ, a kɛtɔ ka dɔ fara sɛnɛfɛnw kan, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la malo.

Fɛnsɔrɔsiraw yiriwali : Labɛn in kɔnɔ, jibolisiraw ni baraziw ɲɛnabɔli bɛ kɛ walasa ka ji sɔrɔli sabati sɛnɛkɛlaw bolo.

2. Barokun kunbabaw
Sɛnɛ kurayali : Ka fɛɛrɛ kuraw don walasa ka dɔ fara sɔrɔ kan ani ka dɔ bɔ tiɲɛni na.

Sɛnɛkɛlaw dɛmɛni : Ka bagajiw kɛ ka labɔli kɛ, kisɛw ani sɛnɛkɛminɛnw (i n’a fɔ suman kannaw) walasa ka baarakɛlaw ka ɲɛnamaya ɲɛ ka taa a fɛ nin baarada in na.

Yiriwali mɛɛli : Ji ni sigida nafolo lakanani ani lamini ka fara sɛnɛ dugukolow fɛrɛli kan.

3. Jɛɲɔgɔnya ni jɛ-ka-baara
Nizɛri ɲɛmaaso(Samba Boukary Tounkara) Jɛkafɔ nafa kan ni sɔrɔko tɔnba ye Ka furaw sɔrɔ gɛlɛyaw la minnu bɛ sɛnɛkɛlaw kan Ani togodala baarada yiriwali bɛɛ la.

Kuma surun na : A bɛ san tan bolodalenba dɔ sɛbɛn min kun ye ka tarabaso ni sɛnɛ ye Mali kɔnɔ "Nizɛri ɲɛmaaso" yiriwali fɛ walasa ka yɛrɛwasa ni sɔrɔ yiriwali sɔrɔ.

Gofɛrɛnaman ye sɛbɛn bolodalen caman ta minnu kun ye ka mara kuraya ani ka jamana ka yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sinsin:

marabolosigi: Fanga dili ladilanni ka bɔ Jamana la ka taa sigida marabolow ma (Dugutigibulonw, Marabolow, Bamakɔ mara) ka tugu "Kafo" ni Bamakɔ mara Dugutigibulonw bɔli la marako siratigɛ la. O ɲɛsinnen bɛ dilannikɛyɔrɔw ni Jagokɛlaw ma.

Lafasali

Minisirisow

Fanga ni ji: Mohamɛdi SOGODOGO (Baarakɛlaw kuntigi).

=== FASARI 150 (taamasiyɛn 2267) — 2026-05-04 04:09:39 CEST ===

Laɲini kunbabaw kalan taabolo kelen-kelen bɛɛ la:

* * Kalan jɔnjɔn : ka sinsin Keguya dilannen kalanw dicogo n’u jɛnsɛnni kan kosɛbɛ walasa ka daɲɛw miirili yiriwa kalandenw ye. 
* 
* Kalan sanfɛla : Ka daɲɛw dilanni seko ɲumanw yiriwa ni Keguya dilannen donli ye kalan bɛɛ lajɛlen kalanbolodalenw na ani ka baarakɛlaw tilatilali ɲɛ.

* Baarakɛkalan : Ka sinsin Keguya kurayali kan laadala dilannikɛyɔrɔw kama, ani ka baarakɛlaw yiriwa minnu bɛ se kosɛbɛ walasa ka se ka taa ɲɛ ni dilannikɛyɔrɔw yɛlɛmani ye.

Ka kalan kɛ ɲɛnamaya kuntaala bɛɛ kɔnɔ: Kalanfɛnw suguya ɲumanw yiriwa walasa Ka Keguya dilannen kalanni cogoya kelenw labɛn bɛɛ ye

Karamɔgɔw yiriwali ani ka Labɛn dafalen jɔ:

* Waleya "ka Jatedenya dɔnniya baarabolodalen ɲɛ karamɔgɔw ye",Ani ka fɛn bɛɛ lajɛlen sɛmɛntiya degeli la ni ka dama kɛɲɛni kɛ karamɔgɔw bɛɛ ye.


*  Ka bɔlɔlɔw dilaan ani kalan fɛnsɔrɔsira kegumanw walasa ka ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo kura dilan Keguya dilannen ni kalanko kama.

Nin labɛn in kun ye ka kalanjɛ dɔnniya ni sɛbɛnni hakɛw yiriwa Jamana fan bɛɛ kɔnɔ ani ka Kalan kurayali sinsin n’o ye ka Keguya fɛɛrɛbɔ don ɲɔgɔn na ni cogoya dafalen ye.

=== FASARI 152 (taamasiyɛn 2275) — 2026-05-01 22:42:31 CEST ===
Faransi kibaruya dɔ y’a jira ko gɛlɛya dɔ bɛ yen min bɛ nakɔfɛn nafama dɔw feereli la i n’a fɔ Leek ni pɔmitɛri Faransi. Nin ye hakilina kunbabaw kuncɛlen ye:

1. Leek gɛlɛya

Fɛn ɲinini dɔgɔyali : Jagokɛlaw bɛ ka ŋunan feereli dɔgɔyaliba la (a sera a hakɛ cayalenba siɲɛ wolonwula ma). O sababuba ye waati bɛrɛbɛn ye; "Yɔrɔla fɛnw tacogo bɛ dɔgɔya Nakɔfɛnw naji, o min ni Leek bɛ jate kɛfɛn koloma a la nɛnɛ duman waati.

Bɔli labɔlen : O kɔfɛ, ɲinan suman sɔrɔta cayara kosɛbɛ, o kɛra sababu ye ka sɛnɛkɛlaw bila gɛlɛya la.

Kɔrɔtɔko ɲɛnabɔcogo : Sɛnɛkɛlaw y'u sen don leek feereli la sɔngɔ "jenilen" na. min ka dɔgɔ ni €10 ye kilo 5 la (a bɛ se ka kɛ sɔngɔ sabatilen tilancɛ ye) walasa k’u ka jiginnɛ saniya sanni a ka falen, ka kɛ fɛn ye min tɛ se ka feere.

2. Pomitɛrɛ gɛlɛya

Maralenw tonen ɲɔgɔn kan: Salon suman tigɛlen hakɛ camanba (tɔni 5 ɲɔgɔn forow dɔ la kelen kɔnɔ) o tolen don marayɔrɔ la fo awirilikalo, o tɛ laadalako ye. Sɔngɔ dɔgɔyali: Sɛnɛfɛnw bɔli kojugu kɛra sababu ye ka sɔngɔ dɔgɔya, o kɛra sababu ye ka feereli kɛ nafa tɛ sɛnɛkɛlaw bolo.

Balo tiɲɛni: Sɛnɛkɛla dɔw diyakuyara k’u ka sɛnɛfɛnw tilancɛ fili, k’a sɔrɔ u hakɛ tɔ tolenw y’a daminɛ ka falen, o kɛra sababu ye k’u ma se ka feere jago la.

Fɛɛrɛ minnu tigɛra: Sɛnɛkɛla dɔw bɛ ka pɔmitɛri bɔrɔw di u ka sannikɛlaw kantigiw ma fu walasa u kana tiɲɛ pewu ani ka yɔrɔ fɛrɛ suman tigɛta kuraw ye.

Kuncɛli : Nin kunnafoni in bɛ gɛlɛyaw jira minnu bɛ Faransi sɛnɛkɛlaw kan, k’a sababu kɛ waati yɛlɛma ye (fonɛnɛ duman), o min bɛ ka nɔ bila fɛnw tacogo la, wa u bɛ na ni wariko bɔnɛ ye ani sɛnɛfɛnw tiɲɛni.

=== FASARI 154 (taamasiyɛn 2332) — 2026-04-30 14:19:24 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Nin ye hakilina jɔnjɔnw ye minnu bɔra minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ ka lajɛ la min kɛra awirilikalo tile 29 san 2026 :

1. Faso ka sangafo ni bonya
Lajɛ ye bonya da Lakana minisiri Sadio Camara kan, min sara awirilikalo tile 25 binkanniw kɔfɛ, a salen kɔfɛ, a kɔrɔtara ka kɛ kɛlɛbolo ɲɛmaaba ye.

2. Fanga (Kuran) .
Mali ye juru sɔrɔ min bɛ se Faransi ka Farafinna CFA miliyari 39,5 ɲɔgɔn ma ka bɔ Farafinna yiriwali waribon na. Nin hakɛ bɛna kɛ ka kɔgɔduguyanfan kuran tali taasira sɛnɛyɔrɔ dɔ jɔ min bɛ se kilovolti 225 ma, n’a kun ye ka kuran sɛnɛcogo sabati an’a mɛɛli sinsin Bamakɔ.

3. Togodala baarada (Baganmara ni mɔni) .
Faso ka lajɛw : Lajɛba caman daminɛni k’a ta mɛkalo tile 4 na ka se zuluyekalo tile 29 ma san 2026, walasa ka baganmara ni mɔn baaradaw kuraya.

Duniya jɛkamiirikalandenw ka jɛkafɔ: Mali ka sendonko lajɛba ye sɛnɛko jirali la Marɔku jamana kɔnɔ walasa ka Mali baganw ka ciyɛn suguyaw yiriwa.

4. Sigida ni Kɛnɛya

Kunnafonidi : Baarakɛyɔrɔw labɛnni (mɛkalo tile 14-15) Fasokanw baaracogo kan Kibaruyaw ɲɛfɔli la.

Kɛnɛya: Dengue banakisɛw dɔgɔyali kɔlɔsira, ani ka weele kɛ ko mɔgɔw k'u janto u yɛrɛ la.

=== FASARI 155 (taamasiyɛn 2397) — 2026-04-29 17:38:42 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Jɛɲɔgɔnya fɛɛrɛbɔlen Irisi ni Mali cɛ
Sisan gɛlɛya in ɲɛkɔrɔ, Irisi jamana b’a ka cɛsiri barikama jira Mali la. Igor Gromyko, n’o ye Irisi jamana lasigiden ye Mali la, ale y’a sinsin a kan ko jɛkafɔ min bɛ ka bonya jamana fila ninnu cɛ, a ka lajɛ senfɛ ni Tɛmɛsira jamanakuntigi Assimi Goïta ye.

Baro kɛra yɔrɔ kolomaw kan:
Kɛlɛ jɛ-ka-baara: Irisi n’a ka kɛlɛcɛw bɛ ka baara kɛ ɲɔgɔn fɛ ni Mali kɛlɛbolokulu (FAMa) ye walasa ka jatigɛwale binkanniw kɛlɛ, minnu bɛ ka caya Mali kɔnɔ sisan.

Baro kɛra sisan kow kan : Lajɛ in ye sababu di ka baro kɛ lakanako kan ani ka jɛɲɔgɔnya jɛɲɔgɔnyaw sabati.

Kɔkanko dɛmɛni: Lasigiden y’a ka foli lase Mali ni Risi kɛlɛcɛw ka ɲɛtaa ma, k’a jira ko Irisi bɛna to Mali jɛɲɔgɔn sabatilen ye.

Nin lajɛ in b’a jira ko jamana fila bɛɛ ka ŋaniya jɛlen don k’u ka jɛɲɔgɔnya sabati walasa ka gɛlɛya kelenw ɲɛnabɔ, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la lakana ni mara sabatili siratigɛ la.

Tarata, Awirilikalo tile 28, san 2026

Baarakɛcogo ɲuman sɛnɛ sɔnni ji la, o sera 0,583 ma, wa Ji baaracogo ɲuman sɛnɛko la, o bɛ ka ɲɛtaa sɔrɔ ka taa a fɛ ; ani Baara daminɛna baarabolodalen kura wolonwula kan san 2025.

=== FASARI 167 (taamasiyɛn 2800) — 2026-04-24 10:44:15 CEST ===
Ameriki Kalifɔrni sɛnɛkɛlaw sulen don Koloradugu baji ji kɔnɔ...
 Sɛnɛkɛlaw bɛ k'u yɛrɛ labɛn walasa ka Koloradugu baji ji bila - CalMatters
 Sɛnɛkɛlaw bɛ ka jiko fɛɛrɛ kuraw sɔrɔ, bawo Koloradugu baji bɛ ka dɔgɔya.
Jiko gɛlɛya ɲɛnabɔli kɔrɔ ye ka dɔ bɔ Kalifɔrni foro fɛnw na...
 Koloradugu baji ji bilali tun bɛ se ka kɛ Ka bɔ Kalifɔrni forow la ka se...
 Masaya wolon bɛ k'a ɲɛsin Kalifɔrni, o min ka joba bɛ Koloradugu baji gɛlɛn na, o bɛ degun na walasa ka jiko kɛcogo ɲɛ, jibɔ dɔgɔyali cɛma 20% kabini san 2000. Ja min bɛ senna, o mara in bɛ degunba la min bɛ ka Yɛlɛma, k’a sɔrɔ a ka ji tilancɛ ɲɔgɔn bɛ alfalfa ni misiw balo dɛmɛ.  Hali ni sɛnɛkɛlaw ka jogo kɔrɔw bɛ u bolo, u bɛ ka degun ka taa a fɛ walasa ka kurayali kɛ ani ka ji lakana.

 Jiko gɛlɛya ni degunko yɔrɔ kolomaw
 Sɛnɛfɛnw sɔrɔli dɔgɔyali: Koloradugu baji, min nafa ka bon kosɛbɛ Tilebi sɛnɛko la, o jibɔ dɔgɔyara ni 20% o bɛ yen kabini san 2000, k’a sababu kɛ ja ye min bɛ sɔrɔ waati cogoya fɛ. 

 Masaya wolon jɔyɔrɔ: Masaya mara bɛ marali kɛ sɔnni kama (IID) Kalifɔrni jamana ka niyɔrɔ 70% la ka bɔ Koloradugu baji ji la, o kɔrɔ ye min ka surun akere sen miliyɔn 3 la, o min b’a to a ka baarakɛcogo ɲuman nafa ka bon kosɛbɛ. 
 
 Ji baaracogo : O ji in yɔrɔba dɔ bɛ kɛ ka clover ni baganbalo wɛrɛw sɛnɛ Nɔnɔ ni sogo misiw kama, O kɛra sababu ye ka ɲininiw kɛ walasa ka fɛn caman yɛlɛma sɛnɛ kɛcogo la ani balo ɲinini. 

 Sariya/tariku josariyaw : Sɛnɛkɛlaw bɛ baara kɛ ni "ka kɔn ka bolomafaraw di" - o kɔrɔ ye ka kɛ fɔlɔ ye waati kɔnɔ — min bɛ fɔlɔya di u ma, fo n'a kɛra nin sariyakolo in bɛ ka kɔrɔbɔ k’a sababu kɛ gɛlɛyaba ye jiko la. 

 Ji lakanani cɛsiriw musakaw : Walasa ka dɛsɛ in ɲɛnabɔ, Masaya marabolo banna sɔnni bɛnkanw na Ji tali kama, yɔrɔla dugubaw bɛ dolari kɛmɛ caman di akere-sen kelen na jiko la, Nafolo min bɛ bɔ nin bɛnkan ninnu na, o bɛ kɛ ka fɛnsɔrɔ yiriwali musakaw kɛ.

 Dɔ farali degun kan : Ɲininkaliw bɛ ka caya sisan sigida in sabatili janya kan, Ani ni fɛn caman b’a la ka Yɛlɛmaba kɛ jiko hakɛw n’a baaracogo la walasa ka mara in lakana ani ka se waati jumɛn ma.

=== FASARI 195 (taamasiyɛn 3068) — 2026-04-17 02:42:14 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman
Awirilikalo tile 12 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 17h31
Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔkobaw ni latigɛw bɛ sɔrɔ sɛgɛsɛgɛli kɔnɔ:

✅Jamanadenw bɛna se ka furakɛli kura sɔrɔ min taara ɲɛ kansɛri kama fu. Kɛnɛyako lasigidenw ka waleya wajibiyalen kɔnɔ, furakɛli fana sen b'o la Baara kɛli ni Funun bolociw ye ani CAR-T Kɔnɔnatumu furakɛli. Fura minnu labɛnna kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la banabagatɔ kelen kelen bɛɛ kama.

✅Kɛnɛya fɔlɔw ka ɲɛtaa bɛna t’a fɛ maraw kɔnɔ. Mara damadɔ bɛna bolomafara wɛrɛ sɔrɔ min bɛ tɛmɛ rubulu miliyari 2 kan nin baara ninnu kama.i n'a fɔ Rubulu miliyari 10 wɛrɛ bɛna bila furakɛli Jatedenya yiriwali kama mara 84 kɔnɔ.

✅Mikhail Mishustin ye fɛɛrɛbɔ lajɛ dɔ kɛ Taji baarada ni fanga yiriwali kan, ka fara jateminɛ kan Kɔrɔnfɛla cɛmancɛ ka ko la.

✅Jamanakuntigi ka cikan fɛ, gɔfɛrɛnaman ka jɛkulu dɔ sigira sen kan sanjiba dɛmɛni kama. Foroba minisiriw taara yɔrɔw la minnu falen don ji la, ka kɛnɛyako yɔrɔw n’u sigiyɔrɔw sɛgɛsɛgɛ Dagestan ni Tchechenia jamanaw na, mɔgɔ minnu tɔɔrɔla.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 196 (taamasiyɛn 3074) — 2026-04-17 02:39:51 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya 

Minisiriw ka lajɛ min kɛra arabadon awirilikalo tile 15 san 2026, o bɛ ɲɛfɔli kɛ latigɛba caman kan minnu kɛra ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ. Nin ye hakilina jɔnjɔnw ye:

1. Sariya ni sariyasunba fɛɛrɛw
Ji: Sɔnni Faso ka jiko Fɛɛrɛ ma (2026-2035). Nin Fɛɛrɛ in kun ye ka yiriwali sabatilen sɔrɔ waati yɛlɛma ɲɛkɔrɔ, ka bɛn ni "Mali Kura ɲɛtaa sira" ka yeta ye.

Fasodenw ka baarabolodacogo (Kamalenninw ni Farikoloɲanajɛ): Latigɛ minnu bɛ fanga di jamana ma foroba la ka taa dugutigibulonw, Marabolow, ani Bamakɔ mara la, k'olu ta. Nin bεnkan in bɛ tugu "Marabolo" hakɛ bɔli la marako siratigɛ la.

Baganmara : Baganmara sabatili ni lakanani fɛɛrɛw sigili sen kan (2025-2029). Faso ni marabolow ɲɛnabɔli sira dɔ bɛ ka sigi senkan walasa ka baganw lakana san 2034 kɔnɔ.

2. Lajɛba jɔnjɔnw (Mɔgɔ kelen-kelen bɛɛ ka fɛɛrɛw) .
Minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ ka ɲɛmaaso : Ɲɛmaa Balɔbɔ Cise kɛra ɲɛmɔgɔba ye nafolo marali bɛɛ baarada la.

Bolifɛnko ni fɛnsɔrɔsiraw : Ɲɛmaa Yusufu Danbɛlɛ kɛra Sɛgɛsɛgɛlikɛla kuntigi ye, ani Furumuso Mariam Ture Jalo kɛra baarakɛlaw ɲɛmɔgɔ ye.

Tilennenya : Ladilikɛla kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen fila sigili (Ɲɛmaa Guindo ni Furumuso Sanogo).

=== FASARI 210 (taamasiyɛn 3198) — 2026-04-14 20:25:15 CEST ===
Mali jamana ka tɔnba (CNT) ka awirilikalo san 2026 lajɛ gansan daminɛ kuma dafalen bɛ bɔ kɛnɛ kan, min kɛra a ɲɛmɔgɔ fɛ CICB Jeli Baba Sisɔkɔ so kɔnɔ.

Sariyasunba bolodalen bɛ bɔ kɛnɛ kan ni sɛbɛn 32 ye minnu ka kan ka sɛgɛsɛgɛ, i n’a fɔ dantigɛli minnu bɛ kɛ Faso polisiw kan, ani Fasodenw lakanani, ni damanko, ani jamana baarakɛlaw ka sariya ani tɔnw sigili i n’a fɔ daman ciyɛn tɔn.

An'a bɛ sɛmɛntiya Mali jamana sinsinnin kan, ani ka dɛmɛ don Sahel jamanaw ka jɛkuluba (AES) ma, ani Jamana wɛrɛw ka donko damantɛmɛni jalaki, ani fɔlɔfɔlɔ kow i n’a fɔ kalan, baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya, ani fanga lakanani.

=== FASARI 212 (taamasiyɛn 3223) — 2026-04-13 19:47:29 CEST ===
Fɛn koloma minu bɔra Mali baganmara ni jɛgɛko minisiri Yuba Ba ka fɔta la, ka ɲɛsin nɔnɔ bɔli yiriwali baarabolodalen ma:

bɔta cayali : kɔrɔtali kɛra nɔnɔ lajɛta hakɛ la ni hakɛ ye 10% lajɛyɔrɔ 137 kɔnɔ, k’a sababu kɛ san 2024 ladilanni ye.

Sɔrɔko nafa : Baganmara bolofara bɛ 19% don Ka bɔ Mali ka sigida bɔta bɛɛlajɛlen na, nka sisan seko tɛ baara kɛ kosɛbɛ (44% dɔrɔn de bɛ baara kɛ Ka bɔ bɔtaw seko la).

Gɛlɛyaw : Baganmara baarabolo bɛ tɔɔrɔ sigida bɔnsɔnw ka bɔta dɔgɔyali fɛ, waati yɛlɛmaw, bagan banaw, ani jigidaba nɔnɔmugumugu donta kan.

Ɲɛnabɔli ni kɛlenw: Lajɛyɔrɔ kura dɔw sigira sen kan an'u labɛnna ni minɛnw ye (Misali la, kɔlɔnw senni minnu bɛ baara kɛ ni tile fanga ye Segu ni Tinbukitu) walasa ka sigida dilanni yiriwa ani ka dontaw dɔgɔya. 

Kumasurunna : Mali mara b’a ɲini ka baarabolo in kuraya walasa ka baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔ ani ka jamana kɔnɔ ɲininiw dafa ka kɛɲɛ ni diɲɛ sariyaw ye.

=== FASARI 228 (taamasiyɛn 3346) — 2026-04-11 13:15:50 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya
Koulouba/ Awirilikalo tile 10, san 2026/ #kɔkanko

Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmaa bɛ Marɔku jamana kɔkankow minisiri, n’o ye Nasser Bourita ye, bisimila

Nin jumadon awirilikalo tile 10 san 2026, Marɔku masaya jamana kɔkankow, Farafinna jɛ-ka-baara, ani Marɔkukaw minnu sigilen bɛ jamana kɔkan, minisiri, n’o ye Naser Bourita ye, o bisimila jama la, Tɛmɛsira jamanakuntigi fɛ, n’o ye a ka bonyali ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta ye, n’o ye jamanakuntigi ye.

Lajɛ in kɔfɛ, Marɔku jamana kɔkankow minisiri y’a jira ko bonya b’a la k’a bisimila Tɛmɛsira jamanakuntigi fɛ, a ye a ka balimaya foli lase a ma, a ka masakɛ Mohamɛdi VI fɛ, ka fara jamanakuntigi ka cɛsiri kura kan, walasa ka Bamakɔ ni Rabat cɛsiraw sabati ka taa a fɛ.

Minisiri Nasser Bourita y’a jira ko a ka masaya masakɛ Mohamɛdi VI ka dɛmɛn min tɛ wuli ka bɔ a nɔ na, Mali ka marabolodacogo n’a ka jamana kelenya sabatili la. A ye Marɔku masaya ka dɛmɛ fana kura ye fɛɛrɛ bɛɛ la minnu kɛra a ka jamanakuntigiba Assimi Goïta ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ walasa ka lakana ni bɛn lasegin jamana fan bɛɛ la.

Nin lajɛ in ye sababu di Marɔku fan fɛ, u ka bilasirali ni ladilikan sɔrɔ ka bɔ Tɛmɛsira jamanakuntigi fɛ, jamana fila ka jɛkafɔ sabatili fɛɛrɛw kan, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, sɔrɔko, kalanko, ani lakana siratigɛ la, Mali ni Marɔku cɛ.

A ka masakɛ Mohamɛdi VI tɔgɔ la, Marɔku minisiri y’a ka waleɲumandɔn jira Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmaa la Mali ka tariku latigɛlen na, n’o ye k’a ka danbe bɔ Sahrawi Arabu bɛjɛfanga jamana na.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 236 (taamasiyɛn 3476) — 2026-04-09 21:28:12 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman
Awirilikalo tile 5 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 17h03
Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun in kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ sɛgɛsɛgɛli kɔnɔ:

✅Gofɛrɛnaman bɛna rubulu miliyari 1 kɛrɛnkɛrɛn walasa ka gazi lase nafa sɔrɔbagaw ma. Nin dɛmɛ in kɔnɔ denbayabaw fana sen b'o la, ani Mɔgɔ kelen-kelen minnu ka sɔrɔ man bon, ani kɛlɛden kɔrɔw. Ani waleya jaakuya kɔnɔ, ka Gazi lase sigida bɛɛ ma, ani Gazi bɛna lase sow kɔnɔ fu. Foroba ka nafolo bɛna kɛ ka gazi minɛnw san ani ka sow ni gazi cɛsiri.

✅Gundolawari  bɛna sɔrɔ sariya sira fɛ Irisi jamana na. Gofɛrɛnaman ye sariyasun dɔ lase jamana wasabulon ma. So kɔnɔ sigidaw ni warimarayɔrɔw bɛna dayɛlɛ Gundolawariw jɛnsɛnni n’a marali kama.

✅ Sigida ka lakana nafaw bɛna nɔgɔya Kɛlɛden kɔrɔw ye Soviyetiki kɛlɛdenya mara kɔnɔ Minnu ye fiɲɛ sɔrɔ.  U bɛna se ka sɛbɛn di waati o waati Ka taa sigida ɲɛmaaso la Ka ɲɛsin foroba Bolomafara ma ka bɔ foroba baaraw la. U ka baara bannen kɔ, O baara ninnu dɔw ye furaw tali ye fu, ani furakɛlisow doncogo sɛbɛnw, ni taama fu furakɛli yɔrɔ la.

Gofɛrɛnaman ye fɛɛrɛ dɔ ta walasa ka dɛn ka boli kɛlɛ min ma daga Ka ɲɛsin dilannikɛyɔrɔ bɔtaw ma fo ka se san 2030 ma.

I n'a fɔ a sɔnna cogo min sɛbɛn kura dɔ ma Sirako bolodalenenw kama, an'o min bɛ suli kɛ danni kan ani ka labɛn segi min ka ca ka tɛmɛ kilomɛtɛrɛ 2000 kan, ka bɔ foroba ka sira la ani mara ta Irisi jamana na san 2031 laban na. Ani baarabolodalenw bɛ fara ɲɔgɔn kan ka M-12 "Vostok" siraba sama  ka bɔ Yekaterinburg ka taa Tyumen, Ani dilanni sira munumunuw na Orenburg ni Orel lamini na, ani Azov sira munumunuta ( o ye sira ye min bɛ Azov kɔgɔji kɛrɛfɛ), ani ka kunkuru dɔ fɛrɛ Ka bɔ M-4 "Don" siraba la ka taa taasira wɔɔrɔ la.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 238 (taamasiyɛn 3609) — 2026-04-09 10:09:19 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Nin kunnafoni min bɔra Minisiriw ka lajɛ la arabadon awirilikalo tile 8 san 2026, n’o ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta bolo, o bɛ ŋaniyaba caman jira Mali bolo :
1. Kɛnɛya ni kalanko : ka Nafolo di min hakɛ ye Faransi ka Farafinna CFA miliyari 83,4 ye
Gofɛrɛnaman ye juruko bɛnkansɛbɛn dɔ sigi senkan ni diɲɛ yiriwali tɔnba (IDA) ye, n’o ye Faransi ka Farafinna CFA 83.437.730.400 ye.

Laɲini : Ka wari di "baarabolodalen min bɛ se ka kɛ sababu ye ka baara nafamaw sɔrɔ joona."

Waleya kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw : Kɛnɛyako fu sabatili musow ni denmisɛnw ye, boloci ni furaw sanni, kɛnɛyasow ladonni, ani dɛmɛni di kalanko siratigɛ la.

2. Baarakɛnafolo: Jamana ka fɛɛrɛbɔ daminɛ san 2026-2030
Lajɛ ye sariya in ta foroba la walasa ka Fasodenw ka baarakɛnafolo bolodacogo daminɛ.
Laɲininenw: Cɛ ni muso baarakɛla kamalenninw.

Laɲini : Ka ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo dɔ sigi senkan min bɛ bɛn nafolo sɔrɔli ma Mali sɔrɔko bolofara kunbabaw kɔnɔ san naani nataw kɔnɔ.

3. Kɔkanko ni baara: Duniya kɔnɔ
Duniya baarakɛlaw ka lajɛba: Mali bɛna a sen don diɲɛ baarakɛlaw ka lajɛba 114nan na Jenɛfu, zuwɛnkalo san 2026. Baara minisiri bɛna jɛmukan kɛ, ani minisiri caman bɛna kɛ farafinna jɛkuluw kɔnɔ minnu ɲɛsinnen bɛ sigidako ni baara ma..
Farafinna, Azi ani Kɔgɔji jamanaw ka tɔnba ka lajɛba min kɛra Malabo, Mali minisiri min ɲɛsin bɛ Maliden minnu sigilen bɛ jamana kɔkan, minisiri ye nɔnabila kɛ a ka jamana tɔgɔla Marisikalo laban na Gine Equatorial. A ye Sahel jamanaw ka hakilinaw Lase kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la lakanako koɲɛw donli ma « Malabo fɔta » kɔnɔ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 240 (taamasiyɛn 3648) — 2026-04-07 13:24:25 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamana fan bɛɛ la, mara suguya caman bɛ ka waati bɛnnen minɛ walasa ka samiya sɛnɛ bɔta sinsin cogo labɛnnen na.

Alikaama foro ɲɛnabɔli bɛ ka tɛmɛ waati gɛlɛn na sisan. Ani Anhui mara ye sɛnɛko ɲɛnabɔbagaw ni dɔnniya ni seko ni dɔnko cidenw bila baara la Walasa ka fɛɛrɛko ɲɛminɛni yiriwa ka ɲɛsin samiya sɛnɛ ma, ani mara minnu bɛ sanfɛyɔrɔ bɛɛ la, olu ye baarakɛnafolo miliyari kelen di walasa ka Alikaama banabaw ni fɛnɲɛnamaw kunbɛnni n’u kɛlɛli sabati. ani Shandong mara la, ka bɔ nɛnɛ Alikaama mu miliyɔn 60 ni kɔ donna binkɛnɛya ni Cili la, wa sigida ɲɛmɔgɔw wulila k'u jɔ ni ka sɛnɛ fɛɛrɛtigi 72.000 labɛn walasa ka don forow kɔnɔ kosɛbɛ walasa ka Alikaama binkɛnɛ sɛnɛ fɛɛrɛw yiriwa ani ka dɔ fara u bɔta kan. Ani Pekin dugu kɔnɔ, sɛnɛ dɔnnikɛlaw taara bɔ yɔrɔ 20 ɲɔgɔn na foro minnu ye misaliw ye, ka bɔ olu sa dugu fila forow Henanzhai Miyun ani Doudian Fangshan, walasa ka ladilikanw di nɔgɔko fɔlɔ la ani sɔnni ɲɛnabɔli samiya Alikaama kama. Jiashan, Zhejiang mara la, sɛnɛ ɲɛmaasow, ani fanga ani Jiko nafolo sɔrɔcogo ye jɛkulu kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw sigi senkan walasa ka sɔnni labɛn hakilitigiw jɔli yiriwa kosɛbɛ, Ni laɲini ye ka ji baarali ɲɛ ani ka bɔta wasa kɔrɔta, O bɛ kɛ sababu ye ka sɛnɛfɛnw sɔrɔli sabati ani k'a caya..

I n'a fɔ Malo sɛnɛ joona  fana yeli kɛ farifoniba la. Guangdong mara la, sɛnɛ daminɛna min ka ca mu miliyɔn 13 ni kɔ ye ka bɔ malo fɔlɔ la. ani Haifeng mara la, n’o ye maraba ye sumankisɛ bɔli la, Sigida mara ye dɔ fara a ka baara kan malo bɔli joona ɲɛnabɔli baarabolodalenw na ɲinan san na, ka labɔli kɛ sɛnɛkɛlaw ye baara dafataw la O bɛ sɛnɛkɛ masinw fara ɲɔgɔn kan ani kannikɛlanw ni dɔ wɛrɛw. Nka Guangxi dugu kɔnɔ sa, an'o malo sɛnɛkɛnɛ tɛmɛna mu miliyɔn 26 kan (hectare miliyɔn 1,7 ɲɔgɔn). Ɲinan, ani sigida mara ye dɔ fara ɲinan Ka bɔ sɛnɛkɛminɛn hakilitigiw baara la, yɔrɔla sisan a bɛ baara kɛ min ka ca ka bɔ bɔli yɛrɛma labɛn sateliti nɔɔnikɛla 3000 la Beidou ani min ka ca ka bɔ sɛnɛko doroni 7000 la.

Ani Labɛnw bɛ ka taa  samiya sɛnɛ kama ni jɔlɔkɔ ye Mara fan bɛɛ kɔnɔ. Chifeng kɔnɔ, Mongolia mara kɔnɔna na, Jalayɛlɛ bɛ ka boli ni fanga ye ɲinan san na walasa ka Jatedenya jirisunninw sɛnɛ, O bɛ kɛ sababu ye ka jirisunninw suguya ɲɛ  ka bɔ kisɛ minsɛnninw cɛma, ani sɔnni ni funfunni minɛn. Ani marali minɛn hakilitigiw Funteni hakɛ la, wa Fuxin kɔnɔ, Liaoning mara kɔnɔ, kurayali bɛ ka boli Laadalata kisɛ minsɛnw na, ka fara sɔnni siraw samali kan ni tomi bila ye an'o waati kelen na ni danni ye walasa ka ji baarali lakanani sɔrɔ ani ka dɔ fara wasali kan. ani Mianyang kɔnɔ, Sichuan mara kɔnɔ, Doncogo Kelen Bɛ Sabati sɔrɔko jɛkuluw fɛ ani feereli Duguw kɔnɔ ani tilali ka jɛkuluw yɔrɔ Doncogo ni feereli togodaw la Sɛnɛkɛminɛnw suguya ɲɛ ani Sɔngɔw sabatili n'a labɔli. Ani bɔnsɔn fila kɔnɔ, Kisɛw seli hakɛ tɛmɛna ani bagaji ka taa sow kɔnɔ kɛmɛsarada la 80% kan, wa Sɛnɛkɛlaw y’a daminɛ ka suguya ɲɛnabɔlenw sugandi, ani ka sɛnɛkɛ minɛnw sɛgɛsɛgɛ, ani ka bagajiw mara labɛnni na samiya cikɛ kama  ɲinan san na.

=== FASARI 244 (taamasiyɛn 3675) — 2026-04-03 15:19:31 CEST ===
Mali minisiriw ka tɔnba ka kunnafonisɛbɛn kɔnɔko kunbabaw lajɛlen filɛ nin ye, min kɛra arabadon awirilikalo tile fɔlɔ san 2026, ɲɛmaaba Assimi GOITA ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ

1. Sariya ni sariyasunba fɛɛrɛw
Gofɛrɛnaman ye sɛbɛn bolodalen caman ta minnu bɛ tali kɛ seko ni dɔnko siratigɛ la, ka bɔ Olu la:

Fanga lakanani : Jamana ka jigiɲɛ dɔ dayɛlɛli ka bɔ taji bɔlimafɛnw na (siyɔɔlɔmafɛw ni gaziw) A labaarali bɛ wasa kɛ tile 45. Nin taabolo in bɛ laɲini kɛ Ka sɔrɔ lakanani ma Ka bɔ diɲɛ sɔngɔw yɛlɛmaliw ma ani ka mɛɛli mara baara ɲɛnamaw na(kuran, taama, danman, lafasali).

Juguya kɛlɛli : ka Sariyasen fila banban walasa Ka sariya sigicogo sinsin hadamadenw feereli kɛlɛli kama (ani kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la musow ni denmisɛnw) ani dɛn ka boli ni taamadenya, O ye ka dɛsɛw ɲɛnabɔ San 2012 sariyaw kɔnɔ.

Dɔnko ni Hakɛli dilanlen: Sɛbɛnnikɛli hakɛ labɛnw sɛgɛsɛgɛli ni carinni walasa k'a ni fɛɛrɛbɔ yiriwalenw fara ɲɔgɔn kan, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, Fɛn min bɛ ka bɔ o la sa o ye hakili dilanlen labaarali ye hakilina bɔli baaraw la.

2. Bugunatigɛli kelen-kelenw 
Damadɔ bugunatigɛra ka bɔ ɲɛmaaw la ɲɛmaaya jɔyɔrɔ dɔw la, ka bɔ cɛma:

Sɔrɔ ni nafolo : ɲɛmaa Mohamɛdi Amara KEITA ni ɲɛmaa Mossa ASSIBIT (Ladilikɛla fila fɛɛrɛbɔla fila).

Dilanni ni Jago: ɲɛmaa Amara NIMAGA (Faso ka baarakɛyɔrɔ fitininw ni cɛmancɛw ɲɛmɔgɔba) ani ɲɛmaa Mariyamu MAIGA (Nafolosɔrɔko ɲɛnabɔli jɛkulu ɲɛmɔgɔba).

3. Tuguɲɔgɔnyaw ni diɲɛ Tuguɲɔgɔnya
Lajɛba ye kunnafoni caman lamɛn Mali ka nali kan diɲɛ seleke naani kɛnɛ kan :

Donniko: ɲɛmaa TRAORE Oumou KEITA ka sugandili ka kɛ Diɲɛ Donniko tɔnba ka nanbarako kɛlɛli jɛkulu ka lajɛ 47nan ɲɛmɔgɔ ye (min bolodara ka kɛ Bruxelles san 2027).

Hadamadenw ka josariyaw: Mali ka sendon jɛkafɔ la jenɛfu. Gofɛrɛnaman y'a fɔ k'a gɛlɛya ko a labɛnnen don ka jɛkafɔ kɛ k'a sɔrɔ a bɛ ban hadamadenw ka josariyaw koɲɛw "fɛɛrɛko" la..

Musow ka koɲɛw : Ka sen don musow ka koɲɛnabɔjɛkulu ka lajɛ 70nan na Niyɔriki, an'o min tun sinsinnen bɛ Muso seli kan tilennenya ma.

Kɛnɛya (AES): sendon kɛnɛ kan kɛnɛyako wariko kan Burukina Faso. AES minisiriw ye seginnkanni kɛ kɛnɛya ɲɛmaayaso  yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya la.

4. Faso ka baarabolodalenw ni kɛnɛyako
Kamalenninw: Fɛɛrɛ dɔ daminɛ "N tɛ N t'a la: Funankɛninw ye, Fasodenw ka karanga kogo ye" walasa ka jɛɲɔgɔnya sinsin funankɛninw ni Lakana Faamaw cɛ.

Foroba kɛnɛya: Gofɛrɛnaman y’a kɔlɔsi kɛ kɔrɔtali dɔ la dengue sumaya cogoyaw la. Ani Tɛmɛsira dakun ɲɛmaa ye jamanadenw su u k'u janto u yɛrɛ la ani ka laadilikanw labato, Ni banakunbɛn fɛɛrɛw ye.

Kɔlɔsili: Nin kuma in b’a jira ko a cɛsirilen don ka Faso ka yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sinsin, A kɛra fanga siratigɛ ye wo, sariya siratigɛ ye wo, walima kɛnɛyako siratigɛ ye wo, ka fara labɛnni ni bi fɛɛrɛtigɛ gɛlɛyaw kan.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 245 (taamasiyɛn 3681) — 2026-04-03 12:14:46 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman
Marisikalo tile 29 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 17h29
Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun in kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ sɛgɛsɛgɛli kɔnɔ:

✅Mikhail Mishustin ye kumaɲɔgɔnya kɛ ni Vietnam minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ Pham Minh Tinh ye. Jamana fila ninnu ka gɔfɛrɛnamanw b’a lajɛ ko bɛnkan minnu kɛra jamana ɲɛmɔgɔw fɛ, k'olu waleyacogo ɲuman na. O kumaɲɔgɔnyaw kɔfɛ, gɔfɛrɛnamanw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ bɛnkan dɔ bolonɔ bilala Vietnam jamana ka nukliyɛri kuran baarakɛyɔrɔ fɔlɔ jɔli kan.

✅Gofɛrɛnaman sɔnna hakilina dɔ ma walasa ka dɛmɛ fɔlɔ yiriwa, o min bɛ di kɔrɔtɔko cogoyaw la hali ka kɔn Furakɛlikɛkaw seli ɲɛ.

✅Fura minnu nafa ka bon kosɛbɛ Irisi jamana na, olu sɛbɛn bɛna labɛn. Ani nin sɛbɛn in bɛna Fura nafamaw  fara ɲɔgɔn kan, Fɛn min bɛ ka bɔ o la sa o ye bolociw ni dimimadalanw, ani Banajuguw furaw. Ani Jamana bɛna dɛmɛ di dilannikɛlaw ma, wa furaw fɔlɔw bɛna di foroba sannikɛyɔrɔw la.

✅Mikhail Mishustin wuli k'a jɔ ni baara bɔ ɲgɔnye ye ka taa Kazakstan. Ani kumaɲɔgɔnyaw bolila ni Kazakstan minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ Olzhas Bektenov ye, ni ka ɲɔgɔnye kɛ ni Kazakstan jamanatigi Kassym-Jomart Tokayev ye. Mikhail Mishustin fana y’a sen don tuguni Eurasie jamanaw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ tɔnba ka baaraw la.

✅ Eurasie jamanaw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ tɔnba kɛrɛfɛ, Mikhail Mishustin ye ɲɔgɔnye kɛ ni Kuba minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ kɔrɔsigi ye ani jamana kɔkan jago ni kuba nafolodon minisiri, Oscar Pérez-Oliva Fraga. Ani nukun fila ninnu ye baro Kɛ sisan jamana fila ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ ɲɔgɔn dɛmɛ koɲɛw kan.

✅ A ka bɔ ɲɔgɔnye in laban na ka taa Kazakstan, Mikhail Mishustin ye jɛmukan dɔ kɛ diɲɛ jatedenya forobajɛkulu la min tɔgɔ ye ko Qazaqstan Jatedenya 2026. A  kumana o la Risi dɔnniyaw fɛɛrɛbɔ baarada ka kɛlenw kan, Ani yiriwali minnu kɛra hakili dilanlen siratigi la, ani tuguɲɔgɔnyaw fɛɛrɛw yiriwali la.

Marisikalo tile 22 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 16h11
Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun in kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ sɛgɛsɛgɛli kɔnɔ:

✅Irisi jamana ka kalan bolofara kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw ni sanfɛ kalansow bɛna baarakɛlaw dege minnu bɛna kɛ Walasa jamana ka se ka seko ni dɔnko yɛrɛwasa sɔrɔ. ani Gofɛrɛnaman ye baarakɛyɔrɔ 500 ni kɔ tɔgɔ sɛbɛn, ani Kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, o de ka kan Walasa ka se ka ɲɛmɔgɔya sɔrɔ fɛɛrɛtigɛ siratigɛ la.

✅Dɔ bɛna fara sigida ɔridinatɛri belebelew seko kan, Siɲɛ tan ka se san 2030 ma. Ani walasa k’o laɲini in sɔrɔ, Gofɛrɛnaman ye siraba dɔ labɛn walasa ka ɔridinatɛriw labɛn minnu bɛ baara kɛ kosɛbɛ ani ɔridinatɛri belebelebaw minɛnw.

✅Mikhail Mishustin taara bɔ jamana ka furakɛli ɲininikɛyɔrɔ la min ɲɛsinnen bɛ jolilabanaw ma. O min bɛ jolibanaw furakɛ ka ɲɛ, bana minnu tun bɛ jate fura tɛ minnu na fɔlɔ. Furakɛli fɛɛrɛ kura minnu bɛ ka labɛn yɔrɔw fɛ, olu bolodara ka don furakɛli basigi wajibiyalen waleya kɔnɔ. I n'a fɔ Mikhail Mishustin fana ye ɲɔgɔn Sɔrɔ ni Irisi jamana ka bonya dɛmɛbagaw ye, ani ka Jirali kɛ ka bɔ rubulu miliyari 12,5 kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen ma i n'a fɔ Dilifɛn ninnu dɛmɛbaga suguw ma sàn o sàn.

Ani Gofɛrɛnaman ye sariyasun dɔ labɛn min b’a to marabolow ka dɛmɛni fɛɛrɛw ka se ka waleya  Sigida Bolomafara fɛ. An'o bɛ farali kɛ marabolo ka bangekɔlɔsi nafolokun na, Ani nafa minnu bɛ sɔrɔ kɛlɛden kɔrɔw ye, ani kalanso sisikurunw sɛbɛnw musakaw sarali.

=== FASARI 269 (taamasiyɛn 3864) — 2026-03-26 10:59:54 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Minisiriw ka tɔnba ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ lajɛba la arabadon marisikalo tile 25 san 2026, a ka hakilijagabɔso kɔnɔ Koulouba masaso kɔnɔ, ɲɛmaaba Assimi GOITA ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ, n’o ye wuliliko ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, jamanakuntigi.

Nin kunnafonisɛbɛn in, min bɔra minisiriɲɛmɔgɔso fɛ san 2026 marisikalo tile 25, n’o ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta bolo, o bɛ latigɛba caman ɲɛfɔ, minnu ɲɛsinnen bɛ wuliliko ma Mali kɔnɔ.

O yɔrɔ kolomaw ye ninnu ye:

1. Lamini ni saniyako : Gofɛrɛnaman sɔnna foroba la jamana ka saniyako fɛɛrɛ ma.(2026-2030). Nin fɛɛrɛ in kun ye ka Malidenw ka ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo ɲɛ ka kɛɲɛ ni bi saniya baara kɛli ye bɛnkan ni sabatili siratigɛ la, ka bɛn "Mali Kura" yeli ma.

2. Lakanali : Kɔrɔtɔko nimɔrɔ kura : Walasa ka jaabi nɔgɔya ani ka ko kɛlenw lakali, nimɔrɔ saba minnu tɛ sara (minnu ka nɔgɔn hakili la) olu bilala baara la :

101: Faso polisiw

111: Faso kojugukɛlaw nɔfɛbɔbaaw

122: Fasodenw lakanani

145: Faso kɔlɔsilikɛlaw

(Dɔ wɛrɛw : 133 ye marifa misɛnninw ye, 135 ye dɔrɔguta ye, 144 ye gɛlɛyaw kunbɛnni ye).

3. Sɔrɔko ni dugukoloko Fɛɛrɛ 
Ka kɛɲɛ ni gɛlɛyaw ye minnu bɛ Kɔrɔnfɛla cɛmancɛ (Babɔlɔn koronyɔrɔ) la, o min bɛ diɲɛ jagokow bagabaga, gɔfɛrɛnaman ye jaabi tan labɛn. Nin laɲini in kun ye ka jamana ka fɛn nafamaw sɔrɔli lakana ani ka seko ni dɔnko siratigɛw lakana ka bɔ nafolosɔrɔbaliya ni dɛsɛw la.

4. Sigida ka ɲɛnamaya ni kɛnɛya
Sunkalo 2026 seginnkanni : Baganmara yiriwali wuli ka jɔ kɛra ɲɛtaa ye kosɛbɛ, bagan 99% ni kɔ (kun 4074) feerela, o kɛra sababu ye ka sogo sɔrɔli nɔgɔya jamanadenw bolo.

Kɛnɛya : Dengue banakisɛw dɔgɔyali kɔlɔsira, jamanakuntigi y’a ɲini ka kɔlɔsili kɛ ka taa a fɛ banakunbɛn fɛɛrɛw kan.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 280 (taamasiyɛn 3969) — 2026-03-21 22:42:01 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Minisiriw ka tɔnba ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ lajɛ gansan na arabadon marisikalo tile 18 san 2026, a ka hakilijagabɔso kɔnɔ Kuluba masaso kɔnɔ, kɛlɛbolo ɲɛmaaba Assimi GOITA ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ, n’o ye wuliliko ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, jamanakunti

Nin ye hakilina jɔnjɔnw ye minnu ka kan ka jateminɛ ka bɔ minisiriw ka lajɛ la:

1. Kɔkanko: Jɛkulu sigicogo kura
Gofɛrɛnaman ye sariya caman bɔ walasa k’a ka kɔkanko cidenyabaarakɛlaw ka jɛkulu sigicogo (baarakɛlaw ni jɛkulu) sɛmɛntiya jamana kɔkan. O sariyasenw bɛ lasigidenso minnu bɛ Pekin, Bɛrɛlin, Kigali, Romɛ, Kayira ani Moskow, olu fana kofɔ fɛ, ka fara lasigidensoba kan Pari. O laɲini ye ka dɛsɛw ɲɛnabɔ san 2018 sariyasunba kɔnɔ, ka mago kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw ɲɛfɔ san duuru nataw kɔnɔ.

2. Damanko ni sɔrɔko: Sanu bɛ latigɛw dusukun na
Yamaruya janya: Roscan Gold Mali SARL bɛ k'a ka ɲininikɛyɔrɔ ka dɔ fara a ka kɛnɛ min b'a tɔgɔ la Kandeoli kɔgɔduguyanfan fɛ (Kɛɲɛba) ka bɔ kilomɛtɛrɛ seleke naani 28 na ka se kilomɛtɛrɛ seleke naani 63,4 ma.

Nafolo ɲɛmaaso sigili ka ɲɛsin fɛn nafamaw ma : Nin ye fɛɛrɛ nafamaba ye. N'an y’a jateminɛ ko danfara bɛ jatebɔ foroba ni jagokɛcogo lakikaw cɛ (kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, Alikaama tolenw, minnu bɛ tɔni 6 jira ka bɔ tɔni 72 jujɔnna minnu bɔra san 2022), O ɲɛmaaso in wajibiyalen don ka feereli labɛn, k’a kɛ sariya ye, k’a lakana walasa ka jamana ka sɔrɔko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya yiriwa..

3. Fɛnsɔrɔsiraw ni togodala yiriwali

Sɛnɛ: Foroba nafa jirali bajida kɛnɛw labɛnni kama Kuriomi, Daye ani Hamaja (Timbuktu). Ani nin bɛ kɛ sababu ye ka tigiya minɛni baara wajibiyalenw kɛ. walasa ka forow kɛnɛ fɛrɛ.

Taama: ka Baaraw kɛ Bamakɔ-Dakari tɛmɛsira kɔgɔdugu fɛ, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la dakun fila Dédéni-Déma-Sandari (km 313) ani Kayes-Debouly (km 96,5).

4. Baarakɛlaw ka baarakɛcogo ani siniɲɛsigi jigiyaw

Gofɛrɛnaman bɛ ka jamana ka baarakɛnafolo bolodalen daminɛ san 2026-2030. Nin waleya in kun ye ka jɔli balilanw bɔ yen. (nafolo ni kalan) Kamalenninw ni musow ɲɛkɔrɔ, an'o bɛ kuntaalajan yeta "Mali Koura" hukumu kɔnɔ.

5. Duniya bɛɛ ka baarakɛnafolo ni kɛnɛyako

Diɲɛ jago tɔnba: Mali bɛna a sen don minisiriw ka lajɛba 14nan na Yaoundé (san 2026 marisikalo laban na). Minisiri min ɲɛsinnen bɛ dilannikɛyɔrɔw ma, o bɛna kuma yen C4+ Kodiwuari kulu tɔgɔ la, Ka ɲɛsin kɔɔriko fɛɛrɛ tigɛlenw ma.

Taamako: Bamakɔ jamanaw ka taamako ɲɛjirali kɛnɛba 5nan bɛna kɛ Ɔkutɔburukalo la.

Kɛnɛya: labɛn ma wuli a jɔlen don Dengue sumaya ko la, ka fara a cogoya caya jateda kan.
