=== FASARI 281 (taamasiyɛn 3996) — 2026-03-20 17:55:37 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman
Marisikalo tile tan ni duuru nɛgɛ kanɲɛ tan ni wɔɔrɔ tɛmɛnen ye ni sagan mugan ni wɔɔrɔ ye
Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun in kɔnɔko jɔnjɔnw ni latigɛw bɛ sɛgɛsɛgɛli kɔnɔ:

✅Farikoloɲɛnajɛ bɛna kɛ fɛn ye min bɛ se ka kɛ jamanadenw bolo minnu bɛ Baara kɛ cogoyaba la, yɔrɔla Jamana ka wariko tɔnw ni Cakɛdaw bɛna farikoloɲɛnajɛ kuluw sigi senkan minnu bɛ ni minɛn kuraw ye, ani ntolatanw labɛnni ni ɲɔgɔndanw  baarakɛlaw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ.

✅Irisi jamana ka woroduguyanfan munumunuyɔrɔ ɲininikɛlaw bɛna t’a fɛ u ka ɲininiw na woroduguyanfan munumunuyɔrɔ yɔrɔw la minnu ma kalan kosɛbɛ. Ani Gofɛrɛnaman Sɔnna sɛbɛnw na minnu ka kan walasa Irisi jamana ka taama in ka Taa woroduguyanfan munumunuyɔrɔ la mun kɔsɔn ka baara Kɛ sàn sàba nataw kɔnɔ.

✅Taamakɛlaw bɛna wari segin sɔrɔ taamaw la minnu tiɲɛna ka taa kɔrɔnfɛla cɛmancɛ la. Nin ŋaniya in bɛ tali kɛ taamaw la minnu bolodara ka bɔ feburuyekalo tile mugan ni seegin ni marisikalo tile bisaba ni kelen cɛ, ani taama kolokolobaaw bɛna se ka baara kɛ ni mɔgɔ yɛrɛw ka jalaki nafolo ye, min bɛ sɔrɔ suguba kɔnɔmɔgɔw ka wari sarali la tuma bɛɛ, walasa ka wari segin u ma.

✅Gofɛrɛnaman ka laadalata minnu bɛna di Taamako siratigɛ la san bafila ani mugan ni duuru kɔnɔ, olu laseli kɛra, O bɛ labɔli kɛ baarabolodalen minnu ye se sɔrɔ, Olu ye taamako baarabolodalen dɔw ye Dagestan, ani taama teliyakow Kɛlɛden kɔrɔw ye Soviyetiki kɛlɛdaga yɔrɔ la an'u ka denbayaw, ani Mɔgɔ minnu ye fiyentɔw ye, ani baarabolodalen ka taama yiriwa min ye dilanniko, ani kokurabɔko teliyaw ni dɔnniko.

✅San saba nataw kɔnɔ, kurayali bɛna kɛ min ka surun bataki ciyɔrɔ ɲɛmaaso bakelen ɲɔgɔnna  Togoda maraw ni yɔrɔ janw na. Ani Gofɛrɛnaman ye kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen kɛ Rurulu  miliyari duuru la nin mago in kama.

Emanuɛl Makɔrɔn

N ye kuma ta ni Iran jamanakuntigi Masoud Pezeshkian ye sisan dɔrɔn. 

N y’a ɲini a fɛ a ka teliya ka binkanniw ban joona, Iran bɛ ka binkanni minnu kɛ jamanaw kan o mara kɔnɔ, a kɛra tilennen ye wo, a kɛra proxy fɛ wo, i n’a fɔ Liban ni Irak. N y’a hakili jigin ko Faransi bɛ don a sen na lafasali hukumu kɔnɔ kosɛbɛ walasa k’a nafaw, a ka marabolo jɛɲɔgɔnw, ani kurunbokari hɔrɔnya lakana, wa ko a tɛ sɔn an ka jamana ka laɲini. 

An bɛ ka fanga bonya min tɛ se ka kunbɛn, o bɛ ka mara bɛɛ bila ɲagami na ni kɔlɔlɔbaw ye bi ani san nataw kɔnɔ. Jamanadenw, Iran jamana na i n’a fɔ mara bɛɛ kɔnɔ, olu de ye bɔnɛ ye. 

Fɛɛrɛ ni lakana sira kura dɔrɔn de bɛna hɛrɛ ni lakana sabati bɛɛ ye. O hukumu in ka kan k’a Jira ko Iran tɛ nukliyɛri marifaw Sɔrɔ abada k’a sɔrɔ a bɛ bagabagali minnu Lase a ka misili balili waleya n’a ka mara ni diɲɛ basigibaliya baaraw ma, olu ɲɛnabɔ.

Hɔrɔnya min bɛ kɛ ka kurunbokari kɛ Babɔlɔn kɔgɔjida la, o ka kan ka segin a cogo kɔrɔ la joona. 

N ye Iran jamanakuntigi fana Laɲini a k’a To Cécile Kohler ni Jacques Paris ka Segin Faransi lafiya la joona. U ka tɔɔrɔ tɛmɛna ka mɛn kojugu, wa u ni u ka denbayaw ta don.

Bɛɛnkansɛbɛn ni Baarabolodalen in kunnafoni

Nafolodon hakɛ: Nafolo min donna o baara in na, o bɛɛ lajɛlen tɛmɛna dolari miliyari 3,5 kan.

Yɔrɔ ni kɛnɛ: baarabolodalen in bɛ banban kɛnɛ kan min hakɛ bɛɛ lajɛlen ye hectare 117.000 ye Timokten dugu kɔnɔ, Adrar mara la.

Bɔliko laɲiniw : Baarabolodalen in laɲini ye ka datuguli kɛ 50% ka bɔ Alizeri ka nɔnɔ mugu magow la, San kɔnɔ bɔta jigiyalen ye ka se tɔni 194.000 ye .

A kɛfɛnw : Baarabolodalen in bɛ forow farali ɲɔgɔn kan Bagan balo bɔli kama, ani forow baganmara kama (ni Bagankulu ye min bɛɛ lajɛlen ye 272.000 ye), ani dilannikɛyɔrɔ yiriwalen dɔ  nɔnɔmugu bɔli kama.

Baara sɔrɔdaw: A bɛ ka bɔ jigiyaw la baarabolodalen in bɛna dilanni kɛ Baara jɔyɔrɔ tilennen 5000 lamini na.

Bɛɛnkansɛbɛnw faralen ɲɔgɔn kan ani yiriwali minnu bɛ tugu o la 

Bɔtaw sanni : Bɛnkansɛbɛn dɔ fana bolonɔ bilala Baladna Alzeri ni jamana ka nɔnɔko baarada (ONIL) cɛ, ka ɲɛsin nɔnɔ mugu sanni ma min bɛ bɔ sigida la. bɛnkansɛbɛnw waleyali (zuluyekalo san 2025): tuma min tugura o la (zuluyekalo tile 28, san 2025), bolonɔ bilala kɛra tuma fɔlɔ kɔnɔ ka bɔ bɛnkansɛbɛn waleyali min nafa ye dolari miliyɔn 500 ye ni diɲɛ n' Alizeri jɛɲɔgɔnw ye, ka bɔ Olu cɛma n’o ye Alemaɲi ka tɔnba GEA ye, walasa ka dilannikɛyɔrɔ ja dilan ani ka forow dilan.

Waati bolodalen : A bɛ ka bɔ latigɛw la jɔli baaraw ka daminɛ san 2026 daminɛ na, bɔli waleyali yɛrɛ de ka kan ka daminɛ nɔnɔmugu kama san 2027 laban na.

Kuluba/ Marisikalo tile 12, san 2026/ #Damannafoloko

Sigida Damanko yiriwali nafolo : Sɛbɛn belebelebaw dili sigida ɲɛmɔgɔw ma

Jamanakuntigi Assimi Goïta, n’o ye tɛmɛsira ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, ale de ye lajɛ in ɲɛmɔgɔya kɛ, n’o ye jamanakuntigi ye, ale de ye Sɛbɛn belebelew dili taamasiyɛn kɛ ka bɔ sigida Damanko yiriwali nafolo la, ka taa minisiriso sugandilen kɔnɔntɔn (9) ma. Nin lajɛ in kɛra, O min minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ nana a la, ani jamana yɛlɛmani tɔnba ɲɛmɔgɔba, ani Minisiri min ɲɛsinnen bɛ marabolow ka marako ni marabolow ma, ani damanko minisiri, ani sɔrɔko ni nafolo minisiri, ka fara gɔfɛrɛnaman ni marako ni yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya fangatigiw ka mɔgɔ dɔw kan, ani minisiri-sɛgɛsɛgɛlikɛla min bɛ daman koɲɛw ɲɛnabɔ, o kɛra Kuluba masaso la alamisadon marisikalo tile 12 san 2026.

Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmɔgɔba ye sababu ta ka foli lase gɔfɛrɛnaman ma, a kɛlen ka nin ladilikan in waleya ka bɔ jamana sigicogo kura lajɛba la. “An nisɔndiyara kosɛbɛ, wa an wasalen don kosɛbɛ, k’a sɔrɔ an ye Malidenw ka nin ŋaniya in dafa,” a y’o jira kunnafonidilaw ka ɲininkali dɔ senfɛ lajɛ in kɔfɛ.

Cɛba Alusini Sanu, n’o ye jamana minisiri ani sɔrɔko ni nafolo minisiri ye, o y’a ɲɛfɔ, k'i k'a dɔn k'a fo ko Sigida damanko yiriwali nafolo bɛɛ lajɛlen san 2025 nafolosɔrɔta la, o tɛmɛna fransi CFA 18.400.000.000 kan. Minisiri Sano y’a ɲɛfɔ ko nin nafolo in tila-tila in kɛra kokura damanko nafolo kɔlɔsili jɛkulu ka lajɛ fɔlɔ kɔfɛ marisikalo tile 2, Ka kɛɲɛ ni minisiriso jɛkulu ka sariyasen jateden 2025-0292/MEF-MM-MATD-SG ye min bɔra san 2026 feburuyekalo tile 27, O min b’a jira ko sigida Damanko yiriwali nafolosɔrɔsiraw bɛ bɛn sigida marabolo kelen-kelen bɛɛ ma. I n’a fɔ a ɲɛfɔra cogo min na nin nafolo in bɔyɔrɔ fɛ, O min ye bolomafaraw ye minnu bɛ bɔ damanko laseginbagaw yɔrɔ ani Nafa sɔrɔbagaw minnu bɛ sɔrɔ fɛnw dilanyɔrɔ bɔli lase la.   

Ka kɛɲɛ ni nin tilali seginni fɛɛrɛ in ye, 50% bɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛn ka di duguw ma minnu tɔɔrɔla damanko yɔrɔ la, ani 25% bɛ di sigida ɲɛmɔgɔ wɛrɛw ma daman yɔrɔw la ; ani 25% min bɛ tɔ o bɛ di sigida mara ɲɛmɔgɔw ma Mali marabolo wɛrɛw la . Nin jatebɔ cogo in, jamana minisiri Sanou y’a sinsin nin kan, “k'o bɛ marabolow ka tilennenya sariyakolo bonya.” Sɛbɛnw dili ye dalilu jɛlen ye min b’a jira ko jamana b’a cɛsiri ka sigidamɔgɔw dɛmɛ. A y’u su u ka baara kɛ n'o nafolo in ye ni hakilitigiya ye baarabolodalenw labɛnni na, O min bɛna kɔlɔsili tuma ni tuma.

O taamasyɛn bolofara dili bɛ fara ɲɔgɔn kan kɛ Kaye maraw la, ani Koulikoro, ni Sikaso, Buguni, Kita, Timbuktu, San, Gao, ani Kidali. Nka, a ka kan k’a dɔn ko nin nafolo dalajɛlen in bɛ tila Mali duguw bɛɛ la O min jateda ye 819 ye.

Sɛbɛn belebelebaw dilen kɔfɛ, Nafa sɔrɔlaw ka kumalasela y’a jira, kuntigi Yehiya HA KƆNTA, Alafiya duguba dugutigi Timbukitu mara la, A y’a ka waleɲumandɔnba jira tɛmɛsira ɲɛmɔgɔba la Ka da a ka teliyako kan ka damanko sariya lajɛ ani ka sigida kɔnɔkow sigicogo dɔ labɛn san 2023 kɔnɔ, k’a lajɛ ko maliden bɛɛ ka nafa sɔrɔ damanko la. Nin daman nafolo tila-tilacogo kura in ye dalilu jɛlen ye min b’a jira ko Mali ka tɛmɛnkan ɲɛmɔgɔw cɛsirilen don duguw bɛɛ yiriwali la jamana kɔnɔ. O min bɛ jigiya di sigida bɛɛ ma. mɔgɔ sugandilen y’a ɲɛfɔ ko nin fanga in bɛ ɲɔgɔndan kɛnɛman sinsin sigidaw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ. A ye layidu ta kosɛbɛ k'a bɛna baara kɛ ni nafolo sɔrɔlen ye ni kodɔn ye.

Ani Jamanakuntigi b'a nata, nin nafolo ninnu sababu fɛ,Ka ɲɛtaa tigitigiya sɔrɔ ni ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo ɲɛli ye, Jamanadenw ta fan fɛ, k’a sababu kɛ baarabolodalenw kɛcogoya ye minnu bɛna waleya ka dafa. Ka nin wariko in na a ka dɛmɛ kɛ nɔ juguw dɔgɔyali la Ka ɲɛsin damanko baara ma sigidamɔgɔw kan. Jamanakuntigi Guyita ye ɲɛfɔli kɛ labɛn dulonman ni kolatɛmɛ kama ani baarabolodalenw  fɛnsɔrɔko kɔlɔsili kama, an'o bɛ Kɛ k’a sababu Kɛ dakun saba ɲɛfɔlenw ye: dugu jɛkulu, marabolo jɛkulu, ani jamana jɛkulu. Ani Jamanakuntigi y'a jira ka bɔ a ka jigiya la O la, duguw taabolo bɛna yeli kɛ yɛlɛmalibaw la min bɛ se ka ye, jiralila ka taa k'a fɔ ko daman nafolo fana bɛna kɛ sababu ye tun ka duguw cogoyaw ɲɛ.    

Tɛmɛsira dakun ɲɛmɔgɔ ye jama hakili sigi ko fɛɛrɛ dɔ bɛ yen nafolow baaracogo kɔlɔsi kama O minnu bɛ Sɔrɔ duguw fɛ. A y’a sinsin a kan, k'a fɔ ko "Baara bɛna kɛ ni nin nafolo in ye ni kodɔn ye.", a y'o sinsin, bawo "a ka laɲini de ye jamanadenw ka ɲɛtaa ye".dugu, mara ani jamana jɛkuluw bɛna se ka nafolo in baaracogo kɔlɔsili kɛ tuma ni tuma. Ni ka dɔ Fàra a kan "Sariya siratigɛ la, fɛɛrɛ minnu ka kan, olu bɛna tigɛ fɔkoɲɔgɔya bɛɛ kama", n'o bɛ bɛn wariko ɲɛmɔgɔw ka cɛsiri ma, n’o ye ka tiɲɛni kɛlɛ.

----------------------------------------

Nin ye Azikaw bɛ min wele ko Tulɔlenya?  Yala nin tɛ muso ye min bonya b'a cogo la Farafinna kɔnɔ wa?  😳

=== FASARI 313 (taamasiyɛn 4267) — 2026-03-13 05:41:43 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya

Minisiriw ka tɔnba ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ lajɛ gansan na arabadon marisikalo tile 11 san 2026, a ka hakilijagabɔso kɔnɔ Kuluba masaso kɔnɔ, Kɛlɛbolo ɲɛmaaba Assimi GOITA ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ, n’o ye wuliliko ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, jamanakuntigi. 

Nin ye kunnafoni ye min bɛ fɛn bɛɛ lajɛlen ɲɛfɔ kosɛbɛ. Minisiriw ka lajɛ min kɛra san 2026 marisikalo tile 11 san 2026, n’o ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ Ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta bolo, o b’a jira ko cɛsiriw bɛ Mali ka yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sinsin na, ka bɔ jɔyɔrɔ caman sababu fɛ: nafolo lakanani, waatibolodacogo, ani laadalakow nɔfɛta.

Kuma surun dɔ filɛ nin ye tomi kolomaw walasa k’aw ka kalan nɔgɔya:

1. Lakanali ni sɔrɔko: "Daman kɔlɔsilikɛlaw" dilanni.
Nin bɛ jate k'a sɔrɔ Siga t’a la, o de ye taabolo kunba ye. Walasa ka sanu bɔli ni damanw feereli kɛlɛ min tɛ sariya kɔnɔ, tuma caman na minnu bɛ lakana ntanya lawuli, gɔfɛrɛnaman bɛ ka sanubɔjɛkulu kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen dɔ sigi senkan.

Cidenyabaara : Kiiritigɛlaw ka kɔlɔsilikɛlaw ka fanga ka se ka damanko min tɛ sariya ye k'o jalaki ani ka sigida lakana ( ka Baara kɛli ni kemikɛlifɛnw ye minnu balilen don).

A kun: Jamana ka dontaw  fara ɲɔgɔn kan taabolo ɲuman dɔ kɔnɔ ka ɲɛsin Faso sɔrɔko ma. 

2. kuntigiya ni waati cogoya
Lajɛba in sɔnna yiriwali fɛɛrɛbɔ bolodalen ma Ka ɲɛsin sanfɛla kɔlɔsilikɛlanw ma (2026-2030). O hakilina de ye yɛrɛ majigin ntanya ye waati yɛlɛma yɛlɛmaw kɔrɔ, O to yen k'a kunbɛn ni sɛnɛfɛw lakana ye ani fɛnsɔrɔsiraw nafamaw.

3. Mɔgɔw ka jɛnɲɔgɔnya Kucala
Daŋaniya jɛlen dɔ Kunbikaw bolo : ka kɛrɛnkɛrɛnni kɛ min ka ca ni hectare 92 ye ka bɔ Dugukolo la k'o tila-tila Walasa ka sigida maaw ka sigi kokura minnu tɔɔrɔla sanjiba ka taa ni ka segin-segin fɛ. Nin bɛ jate taabolo tilennen dɔ ye duguw labɛnni kunbɛnni kama.

4. Sigidaw ka dɛmɛ ani tiɲɛni kɛlɛli
Hali n’a y’a sɔrɔ Mali bɛ ka bɔ Saheli kelenyatɔnba kunkurun dɔ kɔnɔ, a bɛ to a tɛ wuli a ye bolo dɔ ye diɲɛ tileyako kɔnɔ Walasa ka nafolow koli kɛlɛ Farafinna tilebinyanfan kɔnɔ  (GIABA).

Faso ka faratiw jateminɛ bɛna daminɛ san 2026 marisikalo tile 20, Ka labɛn kɛ san 2027 diɲɛ seleke naani seginnkanni kama.

5. Cɛsiri ni lafaamuyali
Jabɔw ye: Ja taama in laɲini ye ka bɔ Sɛbɛnnikɛla Yambo Ologim ka ko la (san 1968 Renaudot Jara sɔrɔbaga) kan, Walasa ka nin mɔgɔba in tɔgɔ segin a cogo kɔrɔ la Mali danbe kɔnɔ.

Bololabaara: Bamakɔ bɛ ka labɛn kɛ walasa ka farafinna bololabaara minisiriw ka lajɛba 12nan kɛ, O bɛ sinsin kɛ jamana ka ɲɛmaaya la nin baara in na.

Kɛnɛyako kunnafoni kura : Kunnafonisɛbɛn b’a jira ko dengue bana ka kow bɛ ka fisaya, k’a sɔrɔ a bɛ sinsin a kan ko a ka kan ka to kɔlɔsili la, ka kɛɲɛ ni jamanakuntigi ka cikanw ye.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 314 (taamasiyɛn 4288) — 2026-03-11 21:51:58 CEST ===
Laayuni dugu y'a daminɛ Moritani, Kabini san 2023 feburuyekalo la, A ka sɛnɛko baaraw sinsinni kan k'o kɛ i n'a fɔ kunkurun ka bɔ jamana ka fɛɛrɛ kɔnɔ, min laɲini ye ka taa tigitigiyali kɛ yɛrɛwasa la ka bɔ nakɔfɛnw ni bagan balo la, walasa ka diɲɛ sɔrɔko cakɛdaw ka wulibaliya kɛlɛ ani ka sigida baloko lakana sabati.

Sɛnɛ baara minnu kɛra Laayuni (2023) .
Foro cɛsiri minnu kɛra Laayuni, olu sinsinnen tun bɛ yɔrɔ koloma caman kan:

Nakɔfɛnw sɛnɛ : Sigida teliyakow bɔra kɛnɛ kan walasa ka sugu magow labɔ ka bɔ fɛn nafamaw na, ka fara cɛsiri kan walasa k'u ka kɔrɔbɔliw fɛrɛ Walasa ka suguya kuraw fara ɲɔgɔn kan ka bɔ Nakɔfɛnw na ni ka ɲininiw minnu bɛ ka caya k'o datugu.

Bagan balo bɔli : Sinsinni dafara bagan balo sɛnɛni kan walasa ka baganmara dɛmɛ mara kɔnɔ, ale de ye baarabolo ye min bɛ ɲɛnamayali kɛ sigida sɔrɔko la.

Kɔrɔbɔliw yiriwali : Sɛnɛ baara bɛ banban Laayuni kɔnɔ, dɔnniyaw tali kan ani kɔrɔbɔli Sɛnɛkɛlaw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ. Fɛn min b'o la o ye nafa sɔrɔli ye ka bɔ laadala Sɛnɛkɛlaw fɛ Minnu ye kɔrɔbɔli jan tigiw ye.

Yɛrɛwasa tigitigiyali cɛsiriw
Foroba ɲɛmɔgɔw b’o cɛsiri Moritani, ni dɛmɛ ye ka fara sigida bɔlikɛlaw kan, ka taa tigitigiyali panniw suguya ma bɔtaw la ka bɔ:

Sigida teliyakow dɛmɛni : Faamaw bɛ sɛnɛko kɔrɔbɔli ɲɛnabɔlenw ta, ani ka Fɛɛrɛko dɛmɛni di ani minɛnw sɛnɛkɛlaw ma.  Jɛkuluw ka jɛɲɔgɔnyaw : O bɛ Baarakɛda 15 fara ɲɔgɔn kan "Sɛnɛ waribon ani yiriwali" O bɛ laɲini kɛ ka taa sɛnɛ sigida yiriwa ma, fɛn min b'o la o ye dugukolo labɛnni ani kisɛ ɲuman kɔrɔtalenw donni walasa ka dɔ fara ɲɔgɔndanw kan.

Dɔ farali Datuguli kan : Sɛnɛ minisiriso ka jateminɛ y'a jira ka taa seli ma yɛrɛwasa hakɛ la ka bɔ nakɔfɛnw na O bɛ 35% ni 40% furancɛ la san 2023, ni laɲinibaw ye ka se 84% ma san 2026. Gɛlɛyaw kunbɛnni : Jamana bɛ ka baara kɛ walasa ka se sɔrɔ jiko dɔgɔya gɛlɛyaw ani waati gɛlɛnw gɛlɛyaw kan ka bɔ jitɔnyɔrɔw jɔli fɛ ani jiko ani sɔnni yɔrɔ kuraw labɛnni.

Aw bɛ se ka yeenke dɔ wɛrɛw kan ka bɔ walawalaliw la Moritani sɛnɛko minisiriso ka Foroba yɔrɔ sababuw fɛ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 315 (taamasiyɛn 4292) — 2026-03-11 17:34:27 CEST ===
Kibaru laban minnu bɔra Moritani nɔnɔko siratigɛ la (fo ka se san 2026 marisikalo ma), olu sinsinnen bɛ bɔtaw kurayali kan Moritani nɔnɔ bɔtaw tɔnba (SMPL) la Néma, ani gɔfɛrɛnaman ka cɛsiriw walasa ka yɛrɛwasa sinsin ani ka bɔ bɔnsɔnw ɲɛni ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ ani kɛnɛya kɔlɔsili.

Nɔnɔ bɛɛ lajɛlen min lajɛra Cakɛda ka yɔrɔw la alamisadon marisikalo tile 5 san 2026, o sera litiri 4434,1 ma. Hakɛ minnu lajɛra yɔrɔ kelen-kelen bɛɛ la, olu tilatilali bɛ nin cogo in na:

- Ouarken yɔrɔ : litiri 1 818,3

- Chamia Yɔrɔ : litiri 1254, ani litiri 10 seginna

- Néma Yɔrɔ : litiri 539,7, ani litiri 38,6 seginna

- Timbédra Yɔrɔ : litiri 550

- Benku Yɔrɔ : litiri 272,1, ani litiri 16 seginna

Cakɛda in bɛ ka taa a fɛ n'a jɛɲɔgɔn baganmaralaw ka dɛmɛ ye baara in na.walasa ka nɔnɔ suguya ɲɛ ani ka dɔ fara nɔnɔ lajɛ hakɛ kan mun kɔsɔn k’a ka sannikɛlabaw magow laminɛ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 316 (taamasiyɛn 4296) — 2026-03-11 13:12:44 CEST ===
Mali sɛnɛko minisiriso
Dɔgɔtɔrɔ Ibrahima SAMAKE ka forobaciyɛn taama fɔlɔ la OHVN (Nizɛri wolon kɔnɔna sanfɛla ka ɲɛmaasoba) la, fɛn minnu taara ni a ye, olu jɔnjɔnw filɛ nin ye:
Yɔrɔ & Don: Alamisadon, Marisikalo tile 5, san 2026, Nizɛri wolon kɔnɔna sanfɛla ka ɲɛmaasoba la (Badalabugou, Bamakɔ).
Laɲini : Ka jɛkafɔ sigi sen kan ni baarakɛlaw ye, ka baarakɛyɔrɔw jateminɛ, k’a ka yelifɛn tila ɲɔgɔn na Mali ka baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya kama.
OHVN ka cidenyabaara : Ka dɔ fara bɔta kan, ka balo sɔrɔtaw sinsin ani dilantaw, ani ka dan sigi faantanya la ka bɔ togodala maraw la Zan BOUARE ka ɲɛmɔgɔya kɔnɔ.
Minisiriw ka ɲɛtaa kunbabaw : Sumankan kɔfɛ ɲɛnabɔli, Ani ka sɛnɛ dilanni jɛkulu sigi sigida ka fɛn dilanni kama, ani Ka sɛnɛtɔnw sinsin minnu bɛ baara kɛ ni kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen caaman ye .
Fɛɛrɛ hukumu : ka Waleya kɛ ni fɛn ye min bɛn "Malikura 2063" yeta ma ani ka bɛn Faso ka tarabasoli ma (SNEDD 2024-2033).

=== FASARI 317 (taamasiyɛn 4301) — 2026-03-11 13:10:10 CEST ===
Mali Minisiri min ɲɛsinnen bɛ baganmara ni jɛgɛko ma
Bamakɔ, Marisikalo tile 5, san 2026 Nin baro kunbaba minnu bɔra nin jɛkafɔ fɛɛrɛbɔ in na sigida nɔnɔko kan Mali kɔnɔ, olu filɛ nin ye :
Kɔlɔsili : Hali n’a y’a sɔrɔ a bɛ se ka tɔni miliyɔn 2,55 sɔrɔ, Mali bɛ nɔnɔko seko 44% dɔrɔn de ta, a tora a jɔcogo la a bɛ dulon nɔnɔ mugu donta la.
Nin wale in : Dɔnnibaaw kulu 17 (fɛɛrɛ ni wariko jɛɲɔgɔnw, Jɛkulu minnu tɛ gɔfɛrɛnaman ka tɔnw ye, baarakɛjɛkuluw) ye baara kɛ kalo 6 kɔnɔ walasa ka fɛɛrɛbɔ jateminɛ ani ka fɛɛrɛbɔ ladilikanw dilan.
Baarakɛlaw kunbabaw : Lajɛ in ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ minisiri Youba BA bolo, Bɛliziki jamana lasigiden, Arnaud DUSAUCY (fɛɛrɛ ni wariko jɛkulu ɲɛmɔgɔ) ɲɛkɔrɔ.
Laɲini : Ka sigida nɔnɔ labɔli n’a Suguya ɲɛ ka da jamana ka yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sariya saba kan.

=== FASARI 321 (taamasiyɛn 4387) — 2026-03-09 21:50:20 CEST ===

Jateminɛw b’a jira ko sɔrɔko bɛ se ka muɲu, ni diɲɛ jamanaw ka jateminɛw (Diɲɛ Waribon ni Duniya wariko tɔnba) b’a jira ko a mɔnni bɛ se 4,4% ni 4,6% cɛ, o min bɛ tali kɛ baara minnu tɛ sɛnɛko ye, olu fanga bonya.

=== FASARI 329 (taamasiyɛn 4445) — 2026-03-08 04:41:31 CEST ===
Sɔrɔko kibaruya labanw: Moritani, Alzeri, Marɔku, Arabie Saoudite, Faransi, Ameriki, Turquie, Ukraine, Irisi ani Sinuwa. Kuma surun na

Sɔrɔko yiriwaliba minnu kɛra jamana kofɔlenw na (kabini san 2026 marisikalo daminɛna ) olu kuncɛlen filɛ nin ye :

Arabu mara tilebinyanfanw

Moritani: Ka jamana wɛrɛw ka nafolodonni caya, o min panna ni 68% ye. Sɔrɔko minisiri b’a jira ko a bɛna mɔn ka se 5,1% ma san 2026 kɔnɔ, jamana bɛna Erɔpu nafolo miliyɔn 269 sɔrɔ walasa k’a ka jɛɲɔgɔnya siratigɛ dɛmɛ.

Alzeri: A sigilen don Arabu sɔrɔkoba naaninan na, ni jamana kɔnɔ sɔrɔ mumɛ ye min bɛna kɛ dolari miliyari 285 ye san 2026. Taamaʃyɛnw b’a jira ko sɔrɔko bɛ don "kɛcogo mɔnni na" dakun dɔ la, n'a mɔn bɛna se 4,1% ma.

Marɔku: San 2026 nafolo sariya b’a fɛ a mɔn hakɛ ka wuli ka se 4,5% ma. Jateminɛw b’a jira ko sɔrɔko bɛ se ka muɲu, ni diɲɛ jamanaw ka jateminɛw (Diɲɛ Waribon ni Duniya wariko tɔnba) b’a jira ko a mɔnni bɛ se 4,4% ni 4,6% cɛ, o min bɛ tali kɛ baara minnu tɛ sɛnɛko ye, olu fanga bonya.

Arabie Saoudite ani Babɔlɔn 

Arabie Saoudite: A b'a jigiya jamana kɔnɔ sɔrɔ mumɛ ka kɛ 4,5% ye san 2026, ka tɛmɛ diɲɛ hakɛ tilancɛ kan. Nafolo minisiri sɔnna jurudon bolodalen dɔ ma min ye Riyali miliyari 217 ye walasa ka wariko magow ɲɛ ani dɛsɛ jateminɛlen datugu min ye Riyali miliyari 165 ye.

Fanga sɔngɔ : Taji sɔngɔ  (Taji gɛrɛgɛrɛ Brent) panna ka se dolari 90 ma bariko kelen na san 2026 marisikalo daminɛ na.

Duniya fanga ni kɛlɛw

Ameriki: A b'a jigiya ko sigida bɔta lakika bɛna mɔn ka kɛ  2,8% ye san 2026 kɔnɔ, o bɛ sanfɛ ka tɛmɛ ni jateminɛ tɛmɛnenw hakɛw ye.

Sinuwa ni Erɔpu: Erɔpu jamanaw ka jago dɛsɛ ni Siniwa jamana ye, o sera a dan na, n’o tɛmɛna ɛrowari miliyari 400 ni kɔ kan san 2026 feburuyekalo la, o kɛra sababu ye ka sirankow wuli donni tugu tuguli ani ka dulon Sinuwa jamana ka fɛɛrɛbɔw la.

Irisi ni Ukraine: Sɔrɔko degun minnu bɛ bɔ kɛlɛ la, o bɛ ka taa a fɛ, ka fara duniya Kelenyatɔnba ka lasɔmini kan, ka bɔ fanga baarayɔrɔw laɲininina babɔlɔn jamanaw kɔnɔ, o bɛ se ka nɔ bila diɲɛ sɔrɔko basigili la.

Faransi: Faransi kɔgɔjida jago ye bɔnɛ sɔrɔ kɔsa in na kɔgɔjida la kurunboli basigibaliya gɛlɛyaw fɛ babɔlɔn kɔnɔ, kunnafoni dɔw y’a carin ko Faransi kurun 50 ɲɔgɔn bɛ ka jɔ.

Türkiye ani diɲɛ suguw
Nɛgɛ nafamaw : Sanu ye tariku hakɛ caman sɔrɔ, a tɛmɛna dolari 5300 kan onsi kelen na san 2026 marisikalo la, o kɛra sababu ye ka waridonnaw sɔɔri ka taa yɔrɔ lakananenw na, dugukoloko fɛɛrɛ daŋaniya ntanya cɛma.

✅Mikhail Mishustin y'a ka san o san gofɛrɛnaman ka baara jateminɛ di jamana Duma ma. A ye jamana ka ɲɛtaa kunbabaw jira san 2025 kɔnɔ, seko ni dɔnko siratigɛ la i n’a fɔ :

• sɔrɔ yiriwali , .
• kɛnɛyako , .
• dɛmɛ don denbayaw ma minnu kɔnɔ denw bɛ yen , .
• jɔli ni fɛnsɔrɔsiraw yiriwali , .
• dɛmɛ don kɛlɛbolo ni sɔrɔko jɛ-ka-baara sendonbagaw ma ,
• kalanko ni dɔnko , .
• bolifɛnko,
• diɲɛ jamanaw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ jɛɲɔgɔnyaw ni bɔtaw.

=== FASARI 332 (taamasiyɛn 4599) — 2026-03-04 23:14:55 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamana na San 2026, Shaanxi mara b’a fɛ ka yuan miliyari 50 nafolo don bolifɛnko musaka bɛɛ lajɛlen na, k’a sinsin nɛgɛsira, siraba, ani jamanaden Gansanw ka Pankurunboonda jɔli kan, ka ɲɛtaa caman sɔrɔ nɛgɛsira la min ka teli kosobɛ la, ani siraba, ni pankurunbonda la.

Laɲini jɔnjɔnw ye ninnu ye:

Nɛgɛsira jɔli

Banni kafoli ani Xian-Ankang nɛgɛsira baara min ka teli kosobɛ, ni Xian-Shiyan nɛgɛsira min ka teli kosobɛ , ani Xi’an-xi'yan nɛgɛsira sira min ka teli kosobɛ, ani xi'an kɔrɔnyanfan jɔyɔrɔ, O bɛ kɛ sababu ye ka dɔ fara a kan min ca ka tɛmɛ kilomɛtɛrɛ 300 ni kɔ kan, ka bɔ nɛgɛsiraw janya la min ka teli kosobɛ n'a bɛ ka baara, walasa nɛgɛsira bɛɛ janya ka se min ka teli kosobɛ n'a bɛ ka baara mara kɔnɔ ka taa a ka ca tɛmɛ kilomɛtɛrɛ 1600 kan.

Ka baara kɛ kosɛbɛ walasa ka Yan’an-Yulin nɛgɛsira min ka teli kosobɛ ani Kangding-Chongqing nɛgɛsira min ka teli kosobɛ k' Olu jɔli yiriwa, ani ka doni nɛgɛsira jɔli teliya ka taa Xi’an Kaotang dilannikɛyɔrɔ la.

Siraba yiriwali

Sira teliman minnu bɛ baara la, olu bɛɛ janya bɛna tɛmɛ kilomɛtɛrɛ 7000 kan.

ka sira koori dafali ban min bɛ Yan’an kɔrɔnfɛla la ani  Luonan-Lushi siraba, ani ka tɛmɛ baarabolodalen labɛnni kɔnɔ ani ka Heyang-Pucheng yɔrɔ fɛrɛ ka bɔ Pekin-Kunming siraba la ani Xian-Yongshu yɔrɔ ka bɔ Fuzhou-Yinchuan siraba la.

Jamanaden Gansanw ka Pankurunboonda jɔli

Ka Pankurunboonda kura fila dafa Dingbian ni Fugu, ka jamanaden Gansanw ka pankurun labɛncogo ɲuman kɛ Shaanxi kɔgɔduguyanfan fɛ. Baoji Pankurunboonda jɔyɔrɔba jɔli daminɛna ni fanga ye, wa Baara fɔlɔ bɛ senna sisan ka ɲɛsin sanfɛtaama ma mara fila la Shunyang ni Shuhe.

Baarakɛcogo bɛɛ lajɛlen ɲɛtaa

Waleya in kɛli bɛ ka kɛ teliya la"ka fila fara a kan San saba kɔnɔ" ka ɲɛsin siraba baarakɛyɔrɔw ma, yɔrɔla fanga kura sɔsɔyɔrɔ kɔrɔtalen 99 farala a kan ka ban, ani ka "sofaw ka so" 8 jɔ, o bɛɛ bɛ taama kɔrɔbɔli sinsin.

Togodala siraw dali bɛ ka taa a fɛ, yɔrɔla kilomɛtɛrɛ 6000 labɛnna ka ɲɛ ka ban ka bɔ siba jɔw la marabolo ni duguba kɔnɔ,kilomɛtɛrɛ 4000 latɛmɛna ka bɔ tɛmɛsira lakana baarabolodalen na duguw kɔnɔ.

=== FASARI 333 (taamasiyɛn 4653) — 2026-03-04 19:40:23 CEST ===
Ameriki ka bolifɛnko minisiriso ye dɔ fara dugu sirako dɛmɛni kan a siɲɛ fɔlɔ ye nin san 10 ni kɔ kɔnɔ
 Sɛtanburukalo tile 10, san 2025

 Laseli in bɛ tali kɛ Kɔnɛkɛti bolifɛnko minisiriso (CTDOT) ka dɔ fara nafolo kan dugu dɛmɛ sira (TAR) ka dɛmɛ waleyali kama.Sɛtanburukalo tile 10 san 2025, jamanakuntigi Ned Lamont ni CTDOT ye laseli kɛ ko dɔ farala TAR ka dɛmɛ kan ni 33% ye, o kɛra o ɲɔgɔnna farali fɔlɔ ye san tan ni kɔ kɔnɔ.  Nafolo bɛ wuli ka bɔ dolari miliyɔn 60 la ka se dolari miliyɔn 80 ma san o san nafolo san 2026 ni 2027. O bɛ sigida siraw dilanni, ladonni, ani ɲɛtaa dɛmɛ duguba 169 bɛɛ kɔnɔ ani mara 4 bɛɛ kɔnɔ Kɔnɛkɛti, Sigida bolomafara si tɛ wajibiya. O nafolo ninnu bɛ bɔ mɔgɔ kelen-kelen ni foroba sariyaw labatoli juruw faralen ɲɔgɔn kan., minnu kun ye ka sigida siraw kɛ a lakana ka ca an' a danaya. O kuma fɔra sigida kibaruyaw kɔnɔ (misali la, WFSB ka kunnafoniw) ani CTDOT ka kunnafonidisɛbɛn foroba.  Dugu minnu bɛ i n’a fɔ Newington, olu y’a jira ko labɛnw kɛra walasa ka baara kɛ ni nafolo wɛrɛ ye walasa k’u ka sirako siraw labɛn.Ni aw b’a fɛ ka kunnafoni wɛrɛw sɔrɔ, aw ye laseli foroba lajɛ: CTDOT ka kunnafonidisɛbɛn walima jamanakuntigi Lamont ka fɔta.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 334 (taamasiyɛn 4658) — 2026-03-04 14:34:45 CEST ===
San o san, jɔli ni tiɲɛni nɔgɔ tɔni miliyɔn 22 bɛ se ka wuli ka kɛ sababu ye ka sabatili ni musaka kɛcogo ɲuman caya, n’a kɛra nɔgɔ ɲagaminenw ye siraw kama.

Arabu Sawudi jamana ka siraba ɲɛmɔgɔba bɛ ka baarabolodalen kura dɔ labɛn walasa ka jɔli ni tiɲɛni nɔgɔ tɔni miliyɔn 22 lasegin u cogo kɔrɔ la san o san, k’u yɛlɛma kɛ nɔgɔ ɲagaminen ye siraw kama, o cogo la, ka sigida gɛlɛya dɔ yɛlɛma kɛ sabatili sababu ye.

Sawudi siraba ɲininikɛla ka ɲininiw dɛmɛni fɛ ani sɛgɛsɛgɛliw kɛra kɔrɔbɔliyɔrɔ kuraw la, nin fɛɛrɛ in min bɛ diɲɛ kɔnɔ, o daminɛna ka waleya fɔlɔ san 2025 maraw kɔnɔ i n’a fɔ Al-Ahsa ani Riyad, jɛkafɔ kɔnɔ ni dugubaw ni nɔgɔko ɲɛmɔgɔw ye.

Nin teliyako in bɛ jate kunkurun dɔ ka bɔ sɔrɔko koori kɔnɔ Arabu Sawudi masaya kɔnɔ, n’a laɲini ye ka o nɔgɔ in 60% lasegin u cogo kɔrɔ la san 2035. O bɛna kɛ sababu ye ka foroba musakaw ni gaziw bɔli dɔgɔya, ka kɛɲɛ ni sɛgɛsɛgɛliw ye minnu b’a jira ko a muɲuli bɛ se ka suma ni laadalafɛnw ye.

=== FASARI 335 (taamasiyɛn 4694) — 2026-03-04 09:01:15 CEST ===
Mali ka bolifɛnko ni fɛnsɔrɔko minisiri, ye kuma nafamaw fɔ a ka sɛgɛsɛgɛli taama senfɛ Bamakɔ marisikalo tile 2 san 2026.

A ka kuma fɔlenw yɔrɔ kolomaw filɛ nin ye:

Cikan barikama don baarakɛlaw ma:

A y’a jira k’a jɛya ko gɔfɛrɛnaman tɛna muɲu ni waati latɛmɛni ye min tɛ jo sɔrɔ. A y’a sinsin a kan ko tɔn minnu ye bɛnkansɛbɛnw sɔrɔ, olu ka kan k’u ka bɛnkansɛbɛnw ka wajibiw bonya kosɛbɛ, ɲɛni ni waatibolodacogo siratigɛ la.

Fɛɛrɛbɔcogo ɲuman:

Minisiri y’a jira ko laɲini tɛ siraw dali dɔrɔn ye, nka ka fɛnsɔrɔsiraw dilan minnu bɛ mɛ, minnu bɛ se ka waati cogoyaw ni sirako gɛlɛnw muɲu. A ko, a kunba la, "An bɛ yan walasa Faransi ka Farafinna CFA warijɛ kelen-kelen bɛɛ min bɛ don nin sira ninnu na, o ka segi ni nafa lakika ye ani kuntaalajan lase jamanadenw ma."

Sanji waati labɛncogo:

A y’a jira ko taama in bɛ na waati gɛlɛn na sanni sanjiko ka daminɛ, k’a jira ko « ɲɔgɔndan ni waati ye» dɔ daminɛna walasa ka baarabaw dafa (i n’a fɔ dingɛw ni jibolisiraw) walasa ka baara jɔli bali walima sira tiɲɛni sanji waati.

Yafa ɲini jamanadenw fɛ ani ka muɲuli ɲini :

A ye kuma ɲɛsin yɔrɔw sigibagaw ma baarabolodalen in kama (i n’a fɔ Kulikɔrɔ sira), k’a ka faamuyali jira gɛlɛya min bɛ sɔrɔ dingɛw ni buguri fɛ, nka a y’a sinsin a kan ko « nin gɛlɛya in ye waati dɔɔninko ye o bɛna kɛ sababu ye ka dususalo banbali sɔrɔ ani ka nɔgɔya don taama la».

Kɔlɔsili dafalen:

A y’a jira k’a ka ɲɛmaayaso bɛna ɲɛtaa jateminɛw kɛ dɔgɔkun o dɔgɔkun, wa a tɛna a jigi da sɛbɛnw dɔrɔn kan, nka a bɛna taa a fɛ ka foro lajɛw kɛ cunkanna walasa k'a dɔn ni baarakɛlaw bɛ yen ani ni minɛnw bɛ sɔrɔ yɔrɔw la.

Kuma surun na, a ka kumasen ye "marako gɛlɛya" ni "jama hakili sigili" ye ani k'a fɔ ko baarabolodalenw bɛ ka taa a ka sira fɛ taacogo ɲumanna.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 336 (taamasiyɛn 4732) — 2026-03-03 15:45:24 CEST ===

=== FASARI 337 (taamasiyɛn 4741) — 2026-03-03 13:09:03 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya
Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmɔgɔba, a ka bon ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta, ye kunbɛnni kɛ ntɛnɛdon marisikalo tile 2 san 2026, sariyatigiw ka kulu la, minnu bɛ Mali jamana nɔna bila kiritigɛlaw ɲɛkɔrɔ,  Farafinna tilebinyanfan jamanaw ka sɔrɔko jɛkulu (CEDEAO) ani Farafinna tilebinyanfan sɔrɔko ni wariko tɔnba (UEMOA).

Nin lajɛ in bɛ tugu kiritigɛ la min bɔra san 2026 zanwuyekalo tile 28, Farafinna tilebinyanfan sɔrɔko ni wariko tɔnba ka kiritigɛso fɛ, ka Mali dɛmɛ, k’a jira ko jalaki minnu kɛra an ka jamana kan san 2022 daminɛ na, olu ye sariya sɔsɔli ye, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, marabolo fitininw kɔnɔ.

Minisiri min ɲɛsinnen bɛ kiritigɛlaw ni hadamadenw ka josariyaw ma, taamasiyɛnw marabaga, Mahamadou KASSOGUÉ, ani sariya jɛkulu mɔgɔ saba—Cɛ. Ousmane Mama TRAORÉ, Fatoumata SIDIBÉ DIARRA, ani Amadou Tiéoulé DIARRA—u y'u sen donna ɲɔgɔnye in na. Ka fara nin sariyatigi saba ninnu kan, o jɛkulu kɔnɔ, Moustaphe CISSÉ, Abdrahamane Ben Mamata TOURÉ, ani Cheick Oumar KONARÉ fana tun bɛ yen.

A ka kuma kɔnɔ, kiritigɛlaw ka minisiri ye lafasalitɔn tanu u ka cɛsiri, u ka gɛlɛya, ani u ka baarakɛcogo ɲuman na. A y’a jira ko ko minnu kɛra sigida kiritigɛso ɲɛkɔrɔ, u daminɛ na, o kɛra sababu ye ka jalaki in waleyali jɔ, sanni kiritigɛ laban ka bɔ o ko in na, o min y’a jira k’a jɛya ko taabolo minnu sɔsɔra, olu tɛ sariya kɔnɔ.

"Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmɔgɔba y'a laɲini ka foli lase sariyatigiw ka jɛkulu ma, minnu ye Mali nafaw lafasa ni bonya ni danbe ye." Kiiritigɛ minisiri y’a jira ko: “Nin dugukoloko fɛɛrɛ  makaritɔko in kɔnɔ, O ŋaniya sugu sɔrɔli tun tɛ ko ye k'i bɛ se k'i yɛrɛ bɔ..”

A y’a sinsin a kan fana ko jalaki minnu jaakuyara Mali kan, jamana ɲɛmɔgɔw y’a jate ko kiiri tɛ, wa sariya tɛ, wa hadamadenya tɛ. O cogo la, zanwuyekalo tile 28 kiritigɛ in bɛ kɛ kiritigɛlaw ka dɛmɛ ye, ka fanga di Mali jamana ka jɔyɔrɔ lafasali ma marabolo ni diɲɛ seleke naani na.

Faamaw ye wasa sariyalen min jira, o kɔfɛ, Minisiri ye sigida kiritigɛso kiritigɛlaw ka jagɛlɛya n’u ka yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya tanu. A y’a jira kokura ko sariya in waleyali gɛlɛn, fɛɛrɛ sigida la, gɛlɛyabaw bɛ min kɔnɔ, o bɛ kɛwale barikama jira min bɛ sariyasunba dɛmɛ ani ka marabolow ka jɛɲɔgɔnya jɛkuluw sigicogo sariyakolow bonya.

Ousmane Mama Traoré ka kuma la, ɲɔgɔnye kɔfɛ, a y’a jira ko jɛkulu in ka waso don, k’a sababu kɛ jamana ɲɛmɔgɔba ye u bisimila. A y’a jira ko: “An y’an ka baara kɛ dɔrɔn i n’a fɔ lafasabaa, k’an jigi da sigida sariyasun min bɛ yen dɔrɔn.”

A y’a sinsin a kan ko Mali lafasali bɛ da sɔsɔli barikamaw kan, minnu sinsinnen don kosɛbɛ sɛbɛnw kan minnu bɛ Farafinna tilebinyanfan sɔrɔko ni wariko tɔnba ɲɛminɛ ani jɛkulu ka sariyasunw daminɛw. A y'a Jira ko "Mali tun tɛ ni fɛɛrɛ wɛrɛ ye n'a ma Kɛ k'a jigi Da Erɔpu kelenya tɔba jamanaw ka sariya kan walasa k'a ka fɔtaw Dɛmɛ. O sariyako in kɔnɔ, a jɛlen don ko Jalaki minnu kɛra, olu tun ye fili ye."

Hakililajigin na, fɛɛrɛ minnu sɔsɔra, olu tun kɛra sababu ye ka Mali ka lasigidenw bila ka bɔ sigida jɛkulu caman na.Tɛmɛsira fangatigiw ye sɔsɔli kɛ taabolo ninnu tiɲɛni n’o latigɛw sariya siratigɛ la kiritigɛso min bɛ ka baara kɛ, k’a fɔ k’u ma tugu sɛbɛnw sigicogo sariyaw kɔ.

Kiiritigɛso y’a ka kiritigɛ-sɛbɛn kɔnɔ, sariya tiɲɛni jɔnjɔnw kɔlɔsi jalaki taabolo la, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, fanga minnu dira latigɛjɛkuluw ma Erɔpu kelenya tɔba ka bɛnkanw kɔnɔ. A y’a jira kokura ko hali fɛɛrɛ gɛlɛya waatiw la, jamanadenw ni marabolow ka tɔnw bɛ to u cɛsirilen don kosɛbɛ sariyasun jɛlen na min bɛ u ka walew ɲɛnabɔ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 338 (taamasiyɛn 4826) — 2026-03-03 04:26:43 CEST ===
Kɔɔri Geseko dilanyɔrɔw ye yeli kɛ Sinuwa jamana na  tugu tuguli la ka bɔ bɛrɛbɛnw na ani yɛlɛmaliw min ka dun kosɛbɛ san 2025 ani 2026, yiriwali taabolo Bɛɛjɛfanga jirali la, ka ɲɛsin “Degunw faamuyali, cɛsiriw faralen ɲɔgɔn kan, ani sigicogo ɲɛnabɔli, an' a ɲɛ hakɛ kɔrɔtali.”. Hali n’a y’a sɔrɔ gɛlɛyaw bɛ a kan i n’a fɔ kɔkanna ɲinini dɔgɔyali ani suguw ka ɲɔgɔndan gɛlɛyaw, i k'a dɔn dilanni a bɛ k'a ɲɛsin kolonkolonli la dɔɔnin dɔɔnin ka taa cogoya kura fan fɛ ka bɔ ɲɛ yiriwali kɔrɔtali la, ka bɔ ɲɛsinfanw fɛɛrɛw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ, ani kokurabɔw minu ye fɛɛrɛbɔw ye ani ka binkɛnɛ yɛlɛma.

Fɔlɔ. Bɔli sabatilen ani dɔɔnin farali; Xinjiang Tora bɔli yɔrɔ koloma ye
San 2025, Siniwa jamana ka kɔɔri bɔli bɛɛ lajɛlen sera tɔni miliyɔn 6,641 ma, o ye san o san caya ye ni 7,7% ye, yɔrɔla Sinjiang ka kɔɔri bɔli tɛmɛna tɔni miliyɔn 6,16 kan, o kɛra 92,8% ye jamana ta bɛɛ lajɛlen na, o ye a jɔyɔrɔ sinsin ka t’a fɛ i n’a fɔ jamana ka ɲɛmaa kɔɔri kama minu ɲɛ kɔrɔtalen don ani kɔɔri falen.

San 2026, Sinjiang ye taama kura daminɛ, ka bɔ kɔɔri dilanyɔrɔw labɛnni na, ni laɲini ye ka kɔɔri sɛnɛyɔrɔ dɔgɔya ni 10% ni kɔ ye, k’a laɲini k’a sabati ka se mu miliyɔn 36 ɲɔgɔn ma, walasa ka degun tɛmɛnenw dɔgɔya ani ka sigida mɛli kafo.

Sɛgɛsɛgɛli fɔlɔ minnu kɛra Sinuwa jamana ka kɔɔri tɔnw fɛ, olu y’a jira ko kɛnɛ min laɲininen don ka sɛnɛ jamana fan bɛɛ la san 2026, o ye mu miliyɔn 44,583 ye, o ye san o san dɔgɔyali ye ni 0,5% ye, nin dɔgɔyali in bɛ dɔgɔya kosɛbɛ, o b’a jira ko suguw ka dannaya bɛ ka segin dɔɔnin dɔɔnin.

Filanan. tugu tuguli bɛ ka waleya ka bɔ Fɛɛrɛw la walasa ka dilannikɛyɔrɔw ka bɔli tugu tuguli yiriwa.

Wa a lajɛra kokura"Xinjiang ka yiriwali bolodalen cɛmancɛ ni waati jan kɔnɔ Gese ni finiw dilanni kama (2026-2035)" ani k'a banban san 2026 daminɛ 
na,o min ye a ɲɛfɔ k'a jɛya  ɲisinna ka taa hakilidiya fan fɛ, ani mɛli, ni dafali, n’a laɲini ye ka ɲɛkun kura dɔ jɔ Gese dilanni kama min dayɛlɛlen don tilebin jamanaw kan.

Xinjiang ye'' dabali fɛɛrɛ kɔnɔntɔn" bɔ kɛnɛ kan, O bɛ laɲini kɛ ka dɔ fara wariko dɛmɛ kan ka ɲɛsin bɔtaw labanw dilannikɛlaw ma, ani ka fɛɛrɛko baarabolodalenw yɛlɛma, ani ka Gese minu ɲɛ kɔrɔtalen don k'o feere, ka dusu don cakɛda, kɔnɔ u ka wuli ka bɔ kɔɔri falen na ka taa bɔtaw la minnu ni dɔ faralen don u sanga kan an'a kɔrɔtalen don.

daminɛni na ka bɔ san 2026 zuluyekalo la, jamana ka suguw ɲɛnabɔli jɛkulu bɛna a daminɛ ni bɔko kura jaakuyali ka bɔ " Kɔlɔsili Fɛɛrɛw fuw bɔta ɲɛ kan ani k'a ɲɛnabɔ" ani sariya wajibiyalen GB 18383-2025, ani ka sinsin kɔlɔsili kan bɔtaw la i n’a fɔ denmisɛnninw ka finiw, kalanso masiriw, ani fonɛnɛ na suturaw, ani fuw labaarali bali minnu bɛ segin ka kɛ fɛn wɛrɛw ye ni cogoya gɛlɛn ye , ani ka sinsin dilanni kɛcogo kura kan ani dɔ faralali ye a banbanni kan.

Sabanan. Sugu baara masinw: sigida sugu kɔgɔlen don, jamana ka sugu fanga ka dɔgɔ, Sɔngɔw furancɛ fɛrɛlen 

Kabini san 2025, sigida ka kɔɔri sɔngɔw taamasiyɛnnen don ni "yɛlɛmaliw ye Minnu fɛrɛlen don ani danfaraw sugu fila ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ sigida ta ani diɲɛ ta." Ni Gofɛrɛnɛman ka dɛmɛ sababu ye ani Gese tɔnw ka fɛnw falenfalenni sababu ye, jamana kɔnɔ kɔɔri sɔngɔw tora ka wuli, k’a sɔrɔ diɲɛ kɔnɔ kɔɔri sɔngɔw tora ka dɔgɔya k’a sababu kɛ diɲɛ kɔnɔ kɔɔri sɔrɔli caya ye, O kɛra sababu ye ka sɔngɔ danfara don Sigida ni diɲɛ kɔɔri cɛ O tɛmɛna waati dɔ la ka bɔ waatiw la yuan 3000 kan tɔni kelen na.

Ka kɛɲɛ ni Sinuwa kɔɔritɔn ka fɔta ye, a bɛ ka bɔ jigiyaw la k'a fɔ ko jamana ka kɔɔri baarata 2025/26 waati kɔnɔ,  ko a bɛna se tɔni miliyɔn 8,1 ma, o ye san o san caya ye ni 3,8% ye. Gese ɲinini ɲɛnamayali kɛra sababu ye ka baarakɛcogo hakɛ ɲɛnamaya a cogo kɔrɔ la, wa falen marataw dɔgɔyara ani Gese ni finimugu gɛrɛgɛrɛ ka taa se hakɛw la min bɛnnen don.

Naaninan. Baarakɛda caman yɛlɛmara: jatedenya  ani binkɛnɛ dilanni O fila y'u dugujɛ ka ka bɔ sinsinna jɔnjɔnw na.

 Ɲɔgɔndan gɛlɛn suma na ani musaka degunw na, baarakɛdabaw bɛ ka u ka ɲɛtaa teliya ka bɔ a ka fɛn kuraw teliya la suguw la"juruw minnu ɲɛ kɔrɔtalen don, an' a baarakɛcogo, ani teri don sigida ye":

Jiangsu Dasheng kulu ye jɔli kɛ dilannikɛyɔrɔ dɔ la min hakili ka di Falen dali la min bɛ karɔbɔni bɛrɛ bɛn, O kɛra sababu ye ka baarakɛlaw jateda dɔgɔya falen dalan 10000 O  10000   la ka se min ka mɔgɔ ni 10 ye, ka fanga tacogo dɔgɔya tɔni kelen na ka bɔ juruw la ni 12% hakɛ ye, ani ka lakikayali kɛ juruw ɲɛ na min ye ɲɛtaa sɔrɔ diɲɛ kɔnɔ.

Donni tugu tuguli nɔ kan: Hali n’a yɛrɛ ka bɔtaw bɛɛ bɛ dilan 100% Ameriki, a bɛ siran  ko Saalenw bɛna bɔli baaraw Garan. Ka fara o bɛɛ kan, a b'a nata ko ka dusu nin saalenw na sigida ka bɔta la, ani ka dɔ fara dusudon kan Ameriki ka kɔɔri dilannikɛyɔrɔw kɔnɔ walasa u ka kɔɔri sɛnɛlenw baara min ye sigidata ye. Sɔrɔko siratigɛw min ka gɛlɛn : Nin ɲininkali in kɛra waati min na sɛnɛko siratigɛ tun bɛ k'a ɲɛsin tiɲɛ na kɔrɔtaba la bɔli musakabaw ni tɔnɔ ntanya sɔrɔta kunba la kabini san 2022.

Yiriwaw minu gunna o la: (2025-2026):
Kɔrɔtɔko dɛmɛw:
Jagokɛlaw ka waleɲumandɔn tajurusara jaabi la, gɔfɛrɛnaman ye kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen kɛ fɛn na min ka surun Ameriki dolari miliyari 12 ɲɔgɔn na i n'a fɔ yɛlɛmali dilifɛn (FBA ani Bridge waleyaw) walasa ka sɛnɛkɛlaw dɛmɛ minnu tɔɔrɔla.
Bɛnkanw ni filitaw : San 2025 nowanburukalo la, bɛnkan min kɛra ni Sinuwa ye, o kɛra sababu ye ka tajurusara donko Saalenw caman jɔ, ka fara Pekin ka jija kan ni hakɛba sanni ye sunbala siyɔ la.

=== FASARI 341 (taamasiyɛn 5001) — 2026-03-01 14:31:44 CEST ===
San 2025 zuluyekalo la, Faransi Gese suguw bɛna fɛnba dɔ yɛlɛmali ye fɛɛrɛko siratigɛ la walasa ka finidoncogo teliya fanga kɛlɛ (Sheen, Timo). O fɛn caman yɛlɛmali in sinsinnen bɛ sariyaw kan minnu ɲɔgɔn ma deli ka kɛ fɔlɔ ani sigida ɲɔgɔndan labɛnni kokura kɛ.

1. Sariyasunba ka jaabi min bɛ sanfɛ 
Faransi ye tariku  senna-taama ta ni wasabulon maakɔrɔw ka sariya ye  ka "Suguya teliman kɛlɛ"" zuwɛnkalo tile 10 san 2025. Nin sariya in laɲini bɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la Suguya teliya ka bɔ nata saba fɛ:

Nafolo jalakiw : Sigidako bolomafara min bɛ taa ɲɛ, o bɛ daminɛ ni ɛrowari 5 ye fɛn kelen na, wa a bɛ se ɛrowari 10 ma san 2030. Jirali balili : Baarakɛda belebele ma se ka jirali tigɛ walima ka kɔlɔsili kɛ.

Lamini taamasiɲɛw kɛli  : A bɛna ɲini tɔnw fɛ u ka magojɔbagaw ladɔnniya u ka fɛn dilannenw nɔfɛkow la sigida nɔfɛtɔkow la u ka bɔtaw la, jalaki banbanli kɔrɔ.

2. bɛɛ Ka filɛli Sugu la ani ɲɛsinfan lamaga
O fɛɛrɛw bɛɛ n’a ta, bɔlɔlɔ yɔrɔw nɔ bɛ to ka kɔgɔ. San 2025, Faransi sannikɛlaw tilancɛ ni kɔ ye sanni kɛ o yɔrɔw fɛ. Nka, fɛn dɔw b’a jira ko fɛn dɔw bɛ se ka yɛlɛma:

Sugu dɔw ka sɔrɔta dɔgɔyali : San 2025 laban na, SHEIN ka sugu sɔrɔta jigira ka bɔ 14,9% na ka se 10,7% ma, kɔrɔfɔba dɔ kɔfɛ.

Bɔta Labaaralenw Sugu yɛlɛli : nin seko in b'a poron 18% kan fɛn minu bɛ san ka bɔ bɔlɔlɔ sira fɛ, ka bɔ fɛnna o bɛ sugu kɛcogo labɛn kokura kudayi.

Tɛmɛni ni kokurabɔ : dilanni cakɛdaw bɛ jate i n’a fɔ Limaheu min b'a yɛrɛ ka fɛnkuraw bɔ a yɛrɛ ye, ni sinsin ye Faransi ka fɛn bɔtaw la ni 100% ninyɔrɔ ye walasa ka sannikɛlaw sama minnu b'u ɲɛ sin yɔrɔjanw fɛ ka bɔ  laadalabɔtaw ma, minnu ɲɛ bɛ cɛmancɛ la.

3. Erɔpu sariyasunba siratigɛ la : Faransi bɛ ka kɛ Erɔpu tɔnba ka kɔrɔbɔli Foro ye, min sɔnna ka nɔgɔw ɲɛsinfan sariya lajɛ kokura san 2025 sɛtanburukalo la, ka kunkanbaaraba jaakuya min fɛrɛlen don bɔlikɛlaw kan Erɔpu fan bɛɛ la.

Yala aw b’a fɛ ka kunnafoni caman sɔrɔ saalenw jalaki kan walima ka Faransi ka jago taamasiyɛnw dɔn o min bɛ nafa sɔrɔ nin sariya kura in na wa?

=== FASARI 343 (taamasiyɛn 5034) — 2026-03-01 00:52:20 CEST ===
Moritani teleso sɛbɛn min carinnen: Fini dilanni Sigida, cɛsiri minnu bɛ senna walasa ka yɛrɛwasa sɔrɔ
Nowanburukalo tile 23, san 2025
"Kibaruya: Fini dilanni sigida, cɛsiri minnu kɛra walasa ka yɛrɛwasa sɔrɔ." Nin kunnafoni in sinsinnen bɛ finiko tɔnba kan (o min ni Moritani faso kɛlɛcɛw cɛsirilen don) wa yeelen bɛ jɔ cɛsiriw kan min bɛ ka taa fɛ walasa ka sigida in yiriwa n’a kun ye ka yɛrɛwasa kɛ lakika ye finiw bɔli la, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la fanga maramanfɛntigiw ni kɛlɛ magow kama ani dugubakɔnɔmaaya cɛrinnen don ɲɔgɔnna. Sigida in kɔkankow : A daminɛna ni "Finiko tɔn" ye san 1993,o kɔfɛ a yiriwara ka taa sigida kura la ani ka kɛ bi tile ta ye fini dilanni tɔgɔ sigida jukɔrɔ.

A tugulen don Moritani faso kɛlɛbolokulu la, wa a laɲini ye ka taa kɛlɛ masiriw bɔ, finiw ani minɛn wɛrɛw min ye sigida ta ye.walasa ka jigida dɔgɔya fɛn dontaw kan.

A ye yiriwaliba ye nin san laban ninnu na, ni minɛnw kurayali ni baaraw ye mun kɔsɔn a k'a dugujɛ a ka wasa ka caya an'a ka bɔta.

San 2025 nowanburukalo la, kunnafoni in kɔnɔko : Nin kunnafoni in ye wuli ka jɔba dɔ ye min kɛra Moritani jamana na, walasa ka sigida ka fɛn dilanni n’a yɛrɛwasa yiriwa siratigɛ caman na  (i n’a fɔ sɛnɛ, Malo, ani fɛn wɛrɛw dilanni).

=== FASARI 344 (taamasiyɛn 5036) — 2026-02-28 14:48:44 CEST ===
Kunnafoni minnu bɔra kɔsa in na, minnu ɲɛsinnen bɛ Mali Gesemanfɛnw yiriwali tɔnba (CMDT) ma san 2026 feburuyekalo la, olu b’a jira ko baarakɛnafolo ŋaniyaba dɔ daminɛna ka nafolosɔrɔ san nata kama, ani laɲiniw kan, minnu bɛna kɛ sababu ye ka kɔɔri sɛnɛni lakunu.

1. Wariko kibaruyaw ni baarakɛnafolo (san 2026 feburuyekalo) .
San 2026 feburuyekalo tile 26, CMDT ka ɲɛmɔgɔyaso ye baarakɛnafolo belebeleba dɔ ta san 2026 nafolosɔrɔta kama :

Baarakɛnafolo bɛɛ lajɛlen : A ka ca ka tɛmɛ Faransi ka Farafinna CFA miliyari 418 ni kɔ kan min kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen na walasa ka kɔɔri seko dɛmɛ.

Tɔnɔ laɲini : cakɛda in b’a ɲini ka tɔnɔ sɔrɔ min ka surun Faransi ka Farafinna CFA miliyari 10 ɲɔgɔn na.

Nafolodonni : Nin laɲini in kun ye ka dilannikɛyɔrɔw kɛ kuraya ani ka sɔrɔ dontaw banbanni lakana kɔɔri sɛnɛkɛlaw ye.
2. Sɛnɛko wuli ka jɔ san 2025-2026
CMDT cakɛda ye yiriwali laɲiniw sigi senkan walasa k’a jɔyɔrɔ sɔrɔ kokura ka kɛ mara ɲɛmɔgɔ ye, gɛlɛyaw kɔfɛ a ye minnu kunbɛn san tɛmɛnnenw na, k’a sababu kɛ kɔɔri fɛnɲɛnamaw ye (fɛnɲɛnamaw) ye :

Sanni sɔngɔ min bɛ kɛ fɛn bɔlikɛlaw ye : kɔɔri gɛrɛgɛrɛ sɔngɔ latigɛra ka kɛ Faransi ka Farafinna CFA 300 ye kilo kelen na walasa ka bɔli dusu don.

Kɛnɛ min labɛnna sɛnɛ kama: Mali bɛ ka labɛn kɛ min ka surun hectare 672.000 ɲɔgɔn na k'o sɛnɛ nin wuli ka jɔ in na.

bɔta makɔnɔnen : Jamana ka waleyaw laɲinitaw ye ka taa basigi lakika sɔrɔ kɔɔri gɛrɛgɛrɛ bɔli la ani k'a tɛmɛ, n’o ye tɔni 650.000 ye. A dɔn k'a fɔ tiɲɛna ko Mali a ye bɔli kɛ Faransi ka Farafinna CFA mara kɔnɔ bɔlila min sera  tɔni 656.751 ma salon.

Ɲɛmɔgɔya : cakɛda in ɲɛmɔgɔya bɛ sisan dɔgɔtɔrɔ Kolomégui Dembélé bolo, ale min kɛra ɲɛmɔgɔba ye san 2025 zuwɛnkalo la.

Baarakɛcogo : Fɛɛrɛko cidenyabaara dɔ bɛ baara kɛ (o bɛ Alemaɲi jɛɲɔgɔnw fara ɲɔgɔn kan) walasa ka gese dilanyɔrɔ bɛɛ sigi senkan sigida la kɔɔri fu baara kama.

Gɛlɛyaw : ɲɛmɔgɔya in ma wali a bɛ fɛnɲɛnamaw kɛlɛli (fɛnɲɛnama minnu bɛ pan) ani lakana bɔliyɔrɔw la, o tora haminankoba ye o min bɛ jɔrɔnanko wuli ɲɛmɔgɔyaso sigiyɔrɔ la.

=== FASARI 348 (taamasiyɛn 5054) — 2026-02-27 11:16:32 CEST ===
Mali Jamanakuntigiya
Kuluba / Feburuye kalo tile 26, san 2026 / #kɔkanko

Jɛkafɔ: Ciden kura fila ka bɔli

Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmaa, a ka bon ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta, ye kunbɛnni kɛ alamisadon feburuyekalo tile 25 san 2026, Mali kɔkanciden kura fila la kɔkan. U Nàna a ka cikanw Sɔrɔ ka Sɔrɔ k'u ka baara daminɛ u ka baarakɛyɔrɔw la.

Olu ye Mali lasigiden dantigɛlen ye Arabu Saudite masaya la ani min bugunnatigɛra ka kɛ Mali lasigiden ye Libi.

Kunba Ibrahima Dahirou Dembélé, n’o ye Mali lasigiden ye Arabie Saoudite jamana na, nowanburukalo tile 5 san 2025, o de kɛra fɔlɔ ye min sɔrɔla Tɛmɛsira jamanakuntigi fɛ.

Ɲɛmaaba y’a daminɛ k’a sinsin a kan ko a bɛ ka nin cidenyabaara kura in daminɛ ni hakilina ɲuman ye. O kɔfɛ, a y’a ka cidenyabaara jira sira saba fɛ : ka sɔrɔko jɛ-ka-baara sabati Mali ni Arabu Sawudi jamana cɛ, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, fɛnsɔrɔko baarabolodalen yiriwali fɛ ; lakana jɛ-ka-baara sabatili, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, kalanko, fɛɛrɛdɛmɛ, ani kunnafonifalen siratigɛ la; ani ka sigida, laadalakow ani diinɛ jɛ-ka-baara sabati ni Arabu Saudite masaya ye.

Kɛlɛtigikunba Maouloud Ould Mohamed ye kɔkanciden filanan ye min sɔrɔla jamanakuntigi fɛ. A ka baara ye Mali ni Libi ka jɛkafɔ sabatili ye.

A ka kumaw la lajɛ kɔfɛ, ciden Maouloud Ould Mohamed y’a sinsin mara lakanani kan, sɔrɔko jɛɲɔgɔnyaw sabatili, ani fɛɛrɛ minnu kun ye ka Malidenw ka ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo ɲɛ, minnu sigilen bɛ Libi jamana na.

Lasigiden fila ninnu, n’olu ye kunba Ibrahima Dahirou DEMBÉLÉ ani Kɛlɛtigi-Maouloud Ould MOHAMED ye, olu y’u ka waleɲumandɔn jira dannaya min dara u kan, k’u ka cɛsiri jira kokura walasa ka cidenyabaara min kalifara u la, k’o kɛ ni kantigiya ni baarakɛcogo ɲuman ye.

=== FASARI 349 (taamasiyɛn 5057) — 2026-02-27 10:56:46 CEST ===
kɔkankow kibaruya labanw  Moritani, Alzeri, Marɔku, Arabie Saoudite, Faransi, Ameriki, Turquie, Ukraine, Irisi ani Sinuwa.

Jamana kofɔlenw ka ɲɛtaa nafamaba minnu kɛra jamana kɔkanko kow la bi, san 2026 feburuyekalo tile 27, olu kuncɛlen filɛ nin ye:

Moritani, Marɔku ani Alzeri 

Marɔku: Marɔku kɔkankow minisiri Nasser Bourita ye yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya laɲini laban jira sɛbɛn dɔ kɔnɔ ka bɔ ɲɛ 40 kɔnɔ, lajɛ dɔ senfɛ Madrid, Ameriki, Alzeri ani Moritani ka lasigidenw nali ye kɛnɛ kan. Masakɛ Mohamɛdi VI fana ye jamana wɛrɛw ka lasigiden kura dɔw Sɔrɔ Rabat.

Alzeri: Alzeri y'a sen don marabolow ka jɛkafɔw la Sahara Sbɛnko la, ka sɔrɔ jamana kɔkankow minisiriso kalatigiba, Lounes Mokramane ye Qatar lasigiden bisimila walasa ka k'u fila ka jɛkafɔ sinsin. Alzeri jamana kɔkanko jamana minisiri, Sofiane Chaib, y’a ka lajɛw fanga Sinsin ni Misira lasigiden ye walasa ka lasigidenw ka jɛkafɔ sinsin.

Moritani: Nouakchott ye kurayali kɛ a ka minɛli la ni “ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo ɲuman” jɔyɔrɔ ye ka ɲɛsin Sahara Sɛbɛn ma, k’a sababu kɛ kunnafoniw ye ka bɔ sɔrɔko ɲɔgɔndɛmɛ fɛrɛli la ni Iran ye nafolodonniko la ani  fɛɛrɛbɔ la.

Arabie Saoudite ani kɔrɔnfɛla Cɛmancɛ jamanaw

Saudi Arabia jamana kɔkankow minisiri ye jamana-ɲɛmaa-jɛkuluw ka welekan caman Kɛ n’a ɲɔgɔnnaw ye Misira, Oman, Turquie ani Pakistan jamanaw na walasa ka baro Kɛ marabolo yiriwaliw kan. Saudi Arabia jamana kɔkankow minisiri kɔrɔsigi ye Israɛl ka sigili farifoniw jalaki, k'a gɛlɛya ko Masaya ka dɛmɛ bɛ sariya sigili la Palestini dugukolo minɛlenw ko la.

Irisi ni Ukɛrɛni (Dɔnniya kɛlɛ kɛnɛba) .
Duniya jamanaw ka degunw bɛ ka taa a fɛ walasa ka kɛlɛ in ban, Duniya Kelenyatɔnba ka lajɛba ye wele bila Irisi ma jɔli kama teliyala ka bɔ fanga baarali la.

O yɔrɔ la kɔkanko lamagaw bɛ yen Washinton bɛ k'o ɲɛ minɛ (Trump ka ɲɛmaaso) ka hadamadenya basigi jaakuya ani ka ɲɛmaaw jalaki ka bɔ dankarili la kɛlɛyɔrɔw la minnu sirilen don diɲɛ jɛɲɔgɔnyaw la.

Fangabaw (Ameri, Faransi, Sinuwa, Turki)
Fasow ka Kelenya: Trump ka ɲɛmaaso bɛ ka ɲɛmɛnɛli kɛ"kɔlanko cunnkan" dɔ la walasa ka MINURSO ka cidenyabaara ban Saharako la ani ka ɲɔnini kɛ ni bɛn siri kura ye kɔrɔnfɛla cɛmancɛ la.
Turquie: A bɛ t’a fɛ a jɔyɔrɔ la i n’a fɔ marabolo cɛsiribaa, jɛkafɔ min bɛ senna ni Riyad ni Kayira ye, ka ɲɛsin mara in ka gɛlɛyaw ma.
Sinuwa ni Irisi: Laseliw y’a jira ka bɔ u fila ka nɔw cayali la Farafinna kɔgɔduguyanfan Sɛbɛnw kɔnɔ, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, jamana dɔw bɛ ka taa a fɛ k'u ka lafasali jɛɲɔgɔnya Sinsin ni Pekin ni Moskow ye.
Faransi: A ka jɛɲɔgɔnya bɛ ka yeli kɛ ni Arabumara tilebiyanfan ye seginnkanw la minnu bɛ ka taa a fɛ, diɲɛ  kura yɛlɛmaw Suma la, ni Moritani ka jɛɲɔgɔnya ye a ka laadalakow la ni Pari ye walasa ka Suguya sinsin.

=== FASARI 365 (taamasiyɛn 5138) — 2026-02-24 08:10:43 CEST ===
Mali: Lajɛ fɔlɔ min kɛra Timbuktu maloforo yiriwali baarabolodalen kan

Timbuktu maloforo yiriwali baarabolodalen (PMPRT) ɲɛmɔgɔjɛkulu ka lajɛ gansan fɔlɔ, san 2025 nafolosɔrɔta kama, o kɛra san 2026 feburuyekalo tile 17, sɛnɛko minisiriso ka nafolo ni minɛnko ɲɛmɔgɔso (DFM) ka lajɛso kɔnɔ Bamakɔ.

Dakabanɔgɔ in ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ sɛnɛko minisiri, dɔgɔtɔrɔ Ibrahima Samaké bolo. Nin lajɛ in kɛra kalo damadɔw baarabolodalen in daminɛ fɔlɔ kɔfɛ, ɔkutɔburukalo tile 16, san 2025, Timbuktu, minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ, gɔfɛrɛnaman kuntigi, ɲɛmaaba Abdoulaye Maïga fɛ.

San duuru PMPRT in musaka bɛ bɔ Farafinna tilebinyanfan yiriwali waribon fɛ (BOAD) ka se Faransi CFA miliyari 8,762 ma. A laɲini ye ka dɛmɛ don faantanya dɔgɔyali la Timbuktu mara la, a kɛtɔ ka maloforo kɛnɛw yiriwa ani ka togodalamɔgɔw ka ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo ɲɛ cogo sabatilen na.

Baarabolodalen in ɲɛsinnen bɛ du 2500 ma, wala min surun nafa sɔrɔbaga 15.000 ɲɔgɔn na, k’a ɲɛsin kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la musow ni funankɛninw ma. Maloforo kɛnɛw yiriwali n’u sɛnɛni sababu fɛ, faamaw b’a fɛ ka sɛnɛko sabati, ka sɛnɛkɛlaw ka sɔrɔ yiriwa, ka baloko sabati o yɔrɔ la.

Nin ɲɛmɔgɔjɛkulu ka lajɛ fɔlɔ in ye fɛɛrɛba ye walasa ka baarabolodalen in ɲɛtaa lajɛ ani ka fɛɛrɛw ɲɛfɔ walasa ka baara bolodalenw waleya ka ɲɛ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 366 (taamasiyɛn 5140) — 2026-02-23 23:49:33 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamana ka san 2026 bolodalen sɛnɛ dugukolo yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔli kama

Sinuwa ka san 2026 laɲini na, dugukoloko laɲini kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen tɛ yen walasa ka sɛnɛko dugukolo yɛrɛta sɔrɔ. O nɔ na, a b’a sinsin sɛnɛyɔrɔ ci bilenmanw kan dugukolo kama minu ka ɲi Sɛnɛ kama, ani k'a cɛɲi ɲɛ ni ka wasa sɔrɔ a baarali la, faso ka balo lakana kafoli la. Sisan fɛɛrɛ in kunba ye "ka sɛnɛyɔrɔ sabati, ka dɔ fara sɛnɛfɛnw bɔta kan yɔrɔ kelen-kelen bɛɛ la, ani ka labɛncogo ɲuman kɛ", ka bɔ o nɔ na fɛrɛba la dugukolo minu ka ɲi Sɛnɛ kama.

Fɔlɔ. Ka jɔlanw ɲɛsin fɛɛrɛw kan: ka baloko lakana minu ka ɲi Sɛnɛ kama,ani ka bɔta hakɛ sinsin.

**Ka tugu layini bilenman na kosɛbɛ dugukolo kama minu ka ɲi sɛnɛ na :** fɛɛrɛ in b’a ɲini k’a jɛya ko "ka tugu layini bilen na kosɛbɛ sɛnɛfɛnw na", ka Sumannikɛlan ɲɛnabɔ kosɛbɛ sɛnɛfɛnw minɛni ni musakaw cɛ, ani k'a lajɛ ko layini bilenman min ye mu miliyari 1,8 ye (hectare miliyɔn 120) sɛnɛta dugukolo kan, o kana tiɲɛ. O kɔrɔ ye ko siniɲɛsigi sinsin tɛ "dugukolo kura sɔrɔli" kan, nka sɛnɛyɔrɔ kɔrɔw minɛni walima u tiɲɛni balili kan.

**Ka jajɔ kɛ ka Sɛnɛ dugukolow dilan min cɛɲi kɔrɔtalen don ka kɛɲɛ ni mara ni kuluw ye :** ka bɔ Baara jɔnjɔn dɔ la min bɛna kɛ "sɛnɛ tigɛyɔrɔ kama, togodaw, ani sɛnɛkɛlaw" fɛ san 2026 kɔnɔ,Ka jajɔ kɛ ka Sɛnɛ dugukolow dilan min cɛɲi kɔrɔtalen don ka kɛɲɛ ni mara ni kuluw ye , o ye ka dɔ fara bɔta furancɛ kelen kelen bɛɛ kan"ka bɔ dugukolow Kelenyali sababu fɛ, ani ka fɛnsɔrɔsiraw yiriwa Sɔnni kama, ani ka sɛnɛ bɔgɔ ɲɛ,o Laɲini o de ye ka sɛnɛ kuraya san 2035 kɔnɔ, yɔrɔ la togodaw maraw b'u daamu ni ɲɛnamaya jɔlan kura ye.

**Dugukolow labaarali kɛlɛli magow kama min tɛ sɛnɛko ye ani dugukolo bilalenw seginni :** fɛɛrɛ in bɛ sinsin jɛya kan dɔɔnin dɔɔnin ani jɛkulu sɛnɛ dugukolow labaarali kan magow kama min tɛ sɛnɛko ye, ani dugukolo bilalenw labɛnni, ni ka sɛnɛ dugukolow labɛncogo ɲuman kɛ ani ka sɛnɛyɔrɔ dɔw baara lasegin minnu bilalen don wari sɔrɔko kɔsɔn walima k'a baarakɛbaliya .

**II. Mun na ɲɛni tɛ fɔ "ka i yɛrɛ ɲɛ"?**

**Lamini feregew ani nafolomafɛnw:** Sinuwa jamana ka dugukolo nafolo sɛnɛtaw danmadɔ bɛ yen, minnu caman bɛ sɔrɔ yɔrɔw la minnu bɛ se ka sɛnɛ. Labɛnnibaw bɛ se ka kɛ sababu ye ka sigida gɛlɛyaw lawuli.

**Ka jigi da diɲɛ balo sugu kan:** Sɛnɛfɛn dɔw la, i n’a fɔ Sunbalasiyɔ ni kaba, olu mago bɛ donni na. Sɛnɛko fɛɛrɛ bɛ sinsin bakolo lakana kan o min jɔlen don yɛrɛwasa kan sigidalaka min dɛmɛnen don ni dontaw ye''. 

**San ni dugu cɛ ka bangeta nɔbila jugumanbaw:** jiwooyow ni jaw min bɛ segin ka bɔ basigi gɛlɛya la bɔli kama ani mɛli,sinsinni fɛɛrɛw bɛ yɛlɛma ka taa faratikow kɛlɛli la ani ka dɔ fara dili kan fɛɛrɛbɔko la. 

**San ni dugu cɛ ka bangeta nɔbila jugumanbaw** Yala aw b’a fɛ ne ka yɔrɔ kolomaw jɔcogo labɛn sɛnɛyɔrɔ jɔli siratigɛ la san 2026 kɔnɔ walasa k’aw dɛmɛ ka faamuyali ɲuman sɔrɔ jamana ye nafolo caman don yɔrɔ min na wa? A ka nafolomafɛnw fanba la.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 367 (taamasiyɛn 5143) — 2026-02-23 21:48:11 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamana ka san 2026 bolodalen sɛnɛ dugukolo yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔli kama

Sinuwa ka san 2026 laɲini na, dugukoloko laɲini kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen tɛ yen walasa ka sɛnɛko dugukolo yɛrɛta sɔrɔ. O nɔ na, ​​a b’a sinsin sɛnɛyɔrɔ ci bilenmanw kan dugukolo kama minu ka ɲi Sɛnɛ kama, ani k'a cɛɲi ɲɛ ni ka wasa sɔrɔ a baarali la, faso ka balo lakana kafoli la. Sisan fɛɛrɛ in kunba ye "ka sɛnɛyɔrɔ sabati, ka dɔ fara sɛnɛfɛnw bɔta kan yɔrɔ kelen-kelen bɛɛ la, ani ka labɛncogo ɲuman kɛ", ka bɔ o nɔ na fɛrɛba la dugukolo minu ka ɲi Sɛnɛ kama.

=== FASARI 368 (taamasiyɛn 5155) — 2026-02-23 17:53:09 CEST ===
Irisi jamana b’a cɛsiri ka sɛnɛ dugukolow labɛn kokura walasa ka yɛrɛta kɛ tiɲɛ ye san 2026 kɔnɔ.

Irisi jamana bɛ ka farifoni kɛ Waleyaw fɛrɛli la kosɛbɛ walasa ka sɛnɛ dugukolow labɛn kokura. ka balo lakana ani ka yɛrɛwasa sɔrɔ. Wa a bɛ ka bɔ jigiyaw la ka Dugukolo labɛnni fɛɛrɛ kɛ ka se fo san 2030, a ban waati kuntaalajanw ka dafa san 2026.

A kɛrɛ kunba bɛ fara ɲɔgɔn kan nin Teliyako in kama waati kɔnɔ fo ka se san 2026 ma ani min bɛ tugu o la:
Dugukolo labɛnni : a bɛ gofɛrɛnɛman ka waleya jaakuyalen kɔnɔ, baara daminɛ na ni min ye kabini san 2022, ka bɔ labɛnni fɛɛrɛ la, min ka surun hectare miliyɔn 2 ɲɔgɔn na, ka bɔ sɛnɛ dugukolow la min tun tɛ ka baara san saba kuntaala kɔnɔ (2024–2026).
Waleyaw hakɛ : Gofɛrɛnɛman ka waleyaw kɔnɔna na (fo ka se san 2030 ma), ka bɔ labɛnni fɛɛrɛw la, min man dɔgɔ  hectare miliyɔn 13 ma dugukolo bonyaw bɛɛ lajelen faralen ɲɔgɔn kan o hakɛ ye hectare miliyɔn 44 ye. 
Dancɛw labɛnni : K’a daminɛ san 2026 marisikalo tile fɔlɔ la, Irisi jamana ka sɛnɛko minisiriso bɛna yamaruya sɔrɔ dansigili la ani sɛnɛ dugukolow dancɛw yɛlɛmani na (dugukolo minnu ka ɲi Sɛnɛ kama, ani gɛnnikɛ yɔrɔw, ani sɛnɛ dugukolo ɲuman kɔrɔw min tɛ ka baara la) walasa u baara fara ɲɔgɔn kan ni wasaba ye.
Yɛrɛwasa : San 2025-2026, Irisi bɛna hakɛ kɔrɔtalen kɛ tiɲɛ ye ka bɔ yɛrɛta karajago jɔlanw na fɛn min b'o la o ye pɔmitɛri ye (97,9% san 2025) ani nakɔfɛnw (a bɛ surunya 90%).
Gofɛrɛnaman ka dɛmɛ : ka bɔ jigiya kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw na a ka ca rubulu miliyari 500 ye ka sɛnɛ dɛmɛ san 2026 kɔnɔ, ka bɔ o la, nafolo bɛna di dugukolo labɛnni ni jatedenya na.
Jatedenya yɛlɛmali: San 2026, jamanakuntigi ye cikan bɔ ni ɲɛsinfanw yɛlɛmali ye 50% ka bɔ sɛnɛ dilanni na ka taa jatedenya labɛnni na.

O jagɛlɛya in bɛ laɲini kɛ ka taa dɔ farali kan suman sɔrɔta bɛɛ la, fɛn min b' o la o ye ka bɔ dugukolow Kelenyali la, ani ka sigida  suguw magow jaabi.

=== FASARI 369 (taamasiyɛn 5167) — 2026-02-23 14:31:20 CEST ===
Ukraine bɛ ka sɛnɛ dugukolow labɛn a ka yɛrɛwasa kama san 2026

San 2026, Ukraine ye cɛsiraba di sɛnɛkɛ dugukolow labɛnni baara ma,o min tun labilanen don walima a nɔgɔnen don juguya baaraw jaabi kama walasa ka balo lakana lajɛ ani ka sɛnɛ dugukolow lakunun. Gofɛrɛnaman ye sariya dɔ ta dugukolo labɛnni teliya kan, hali ni faratiw bɛ yen, ka nafolo caman bila mugu pɛrɛntaw bɔli kama. 

Dugukolo seginni yɔrɔ kolomaw san 2026 kɔnɔ:
mugu pɛrɛntaw bɔli n’u labɛnni sigiyɔrɔyali : a bɛ ka bɔ jigiya kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw na a ka ca UAH miliyari 2 ye o bɛna kɛ mugu pɛrɛntaw bɔli ye hadamadenya siratigɛ la san 2026. Hectare 12000 ni kɔ seginna baara la kaban, wa gɔfɛrɛnaman b’a fɛ ka mugu pɛrɛntaw bɔli kɛ fo ka se 90% ma marabolo minnu jiginen don mara kɔrɔ, san laban na.
Gofɛrɛnɛman ka dɛmɛ : San 2026 baarakɛnafolo ye UAH miliyari 13,1 bila sɛnɛko siratigɛ dɛmɛni na, o la, bolomafaraw, minɛnw musakaw, ani juruw bɛ di sɛnɛkɛlaw ma minnu faralen don dugukolow sigiyɔrɔyali labɛnni kan.
Sɛnɛko jateminɛ : jateminɛw b'a jira, fo n'a kɛra sɛnɛkɛlaw ye u bɛna sɛnɛ kɛ san 2026 kɔnɔ ka tɛmɛ hectare miliyɔn 22–23 ɲɔgɔn kan, o bɛna kɛ sababu ye ka suman sabatilen sɔrɔ sigida magow jaabi la an' a bɔli la.
Sariyasen kura : K’a daminɛ san 2026 zanwuyekalo tile fɔlɔ la, saalen jatebɔsɛbɛn kura donna jamana ka sɛnɛkɛ dugukolow dɔnɔni na .
Jago ɲinini lafiyalen:nin baarakɛta in bɛ jate kunkurun dɔ ye ka bɔ Ukraine ka lafiya fɛɛrɛ taabolo sabanan na (2026–2032), min sinsinnen bɛ yiriwali, kurayali kan, ani waridonnaw samali. 
Nin fɛɛrɛ ninnu kun ye ka dugukolo segin sɔrɔko siratigɛ la joona, ka sɔrɔko sabatili yiriwa ani ka baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sabati Ukraine ye.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 370 (taamasiyɛn 5172) — 2026-02-23 13:27:01 CEST ===
Türkiye ka sɛnɛko dugukoloko baarabolodalen laɲini ye ka yɛrɛta sɔrɔ sɛnɛko la san 2026 kɔnɔ.

Turquie ye san 2024-2028 fɛɛrɛbɔ bolodalen dɔ waleya ani sɛnɛfɛnw dilanni bolodacogo bɛɛ lajɛlen labɛn walasa ka dɔ fara sɛnɛko sabatili kan ani ka dɔ bɔ dulonni na fɛn minnu bɛ bɔ kɔkan. Fɛn minnu bɛ tugu San 2026 laɲiniw na ani sisan yiriwali nafamaw la Olu ye ninnu ye :

Sɛnɛfɛnw dilanni bolodacogo ni jaabi ɲuman sɔrɔli : Ni "ji-kɛnɛya" misali kura ye, o laɲini ye ka sɔrɔko lakanani lakana ani ka dɔ fara sɛnɛfɛnw sɔrɔli kan fɛɛrɛbɔ siratigɛ la san 2026 kɔnɔ.
Yɛrɛwasa : Turki ye yɛrɛwasa hakɛ ka ca kaban fɛn caman na (misali la, 113% nakɔfɛnw na, 156% jiriden mugantaw la). Nka, san 2026 bolodalenw kun ye ka gɛrɛ yɛrɛta dafalen na, n’o ye ka dɔ fara sigida la sɛnɛfɛnw bɔli kan, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, yɔrɔ minnu na u b'u dulon, fɛnw dondonni na, i n’a fɔ siyɔw, jiriden kolow, ani bagan balow.
Yɛrɛta Dugukolo labɛnni ni fɛnsɔrɔsiraw la : ka bɔ fɛɛrɛw la ka fɛnsɔrɔsiraw dafa Sɛnɛyɔrɔ 7 kama o bolodara san 2026. I n'a fɔ a bɛ jigiya cogo min na ko  sɛnɛ dugulolow hakɛ bɛna tɛmɛ hectare miliyɔn 7,3 kan san 2026.
Sɛnɛ jatebɔ : Minisiriso b’a fɛ ka sɛnɛko jatebɔ bɛɛ lajɛlen dafa, o min bɛ jate jɔlan ye tigitigiyali kama ka bɔ sɛnɛko kunnafoniw na san 2026 kɔnɔ.
Baarakɛnafolo ni dɛmɛn : Sɛnɛko ni kungo minisiriso ka baarakɛnafolo san 2026 kɔnɔ,o bɛ turiki liira miliyari 542 bɔ ; wa a bɛ jigiyaw la ka turiki liira miliyari 168 kɛrɛnkɛrɛn ka bɔ a la sɛnɛko dɛmɛw tilenen kama. 

Walasa ka kunnafoni wɛrɛw sɔrɔ, aw bɛ se ka sɛnɛko ni kungo minisiriso ka san 2024-2028 fɛɛrɛbɔ sɛbɛn lajɛ walima ka tugu sisan laɲiniw kɔ san 2026 baarakɛcogo waleyaw sababu fɛ.
Yala aw b’a fɛ ka dɛmɛ hakɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw dɔn fɛn dilannen kulu kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen dɔ la (misali la, Alikaama walima tile den) nin labɛn in kɔnɔ wa?

=== FASARI 371 (taamasiyɛn 5193) — 2026-02-23 08:49:50 CEST ===
Sɛnɛ dugukolow labɛnni Ameriki ka ɲɛsin a dakun filanan ma san 2026

 Ka da kunnafoniw kan minnu bɔra san 2026 feburuyekalo la, sɛnɛko dugukolo marali n’a lakanani Ameriki jamana na san 2026 San nafolosɔrɔta kama, cogoyabala o sinsinnen don ji labaarali Ladilikan kan Tilebi fɛ, ni waati dantigɛlen kuntigiya jɔnjɔnw banni ye Ladilikan waleyaw kama san 2026 marisikalo la. 
O bɛ Ɲɛtaa kunba fara ɲɔgɔn kan San 2026 kɔnɔ, olu ye :
 Ka Koloradugu baji baara san 2026 kɔfɛ : Ameriki dugukolo labɛnni ɲɛmaaso bɛ ka baaraba haminankoba ɲɛminɛ,o bɛ banban san caman kɔnɔ a ka c'a la a tɛna jɔli ye, walasa ka baarakɛcogo bilasiralikanw latigɛ baji fila la Powell baji ni Mead baji la san 2026 kɔfɛ, ani bɛnkan waati latigɛlen don ka kɛ Ɔkutɔburukalo tile 1 san 2026. Lamini nɔfɛko kunnafonisɛbɛn (EIS) sɛbɛn dɔ bɔra Zanwuyekalo tile 9 san 2026, min bɛ fɛɛrɛ wɛrɛw jira ka ɲɛsin jiko nataw ma ka kɛɲɛ ni ja basigilen ye.
 nafolomafɛnw mara waleya waati dantigɛlenw : Ameriki dugukolo labɛnni ɲɛmaaso ka sigida nafolo lakanani baarada (NRCS) n'o tugulen don Ameriki sɛnɛko minisiriso la o ye waati laban dantigɛlen jɔnjɔnw boloda nafolomafɛnw mara waleyaw kama, yɔrɔla Marisikalo tile 13  san 2026 dantigɛra, i  n'a fɔ waati laban tɔgɔsɛbɛn, digiko filanan ye ka bɔ mara waleyaw la sɛnɛkɛ dugukolow la Niyɔriki. Ani Kuunteekiti mara la, ka bɔ latigɛw la ka da yele tɔgɔsɛbɛn kama mara waleyaw la nafolomafɛnw jɔnjɔnw na, o bolodara ka kɛ Marisikalo tile 20 san 2026.
 Dugukolo labɛnni ni a sabatili : o b'a sinsin dugukolo labɛnni kan San 2026 Dakota kɔgɔduguyanfan fɛ (marisikalo tile 3-4, san 2026) jɛɲɔgɔnya kan sɛnɛko ni dugukolo labɛnni ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ, ka fara sinsinni kan sɛnɛkɛ bɔgɔ kɛnɛya kan, ani dugukolo sigida dɔnniya, ani ka fanga bɔli ɲagami sɛnɛ dugukolow la.
 Lamini sariyaw : lamini Lakanani kɔlɔsibagaw y’a jira k'u sɔnna ka Fɛnɲɛnamafagalan dicamba baara walasa ka fonfonni kɛ ni a ye tilenenyara kɔɔri ni Sunbalasiyɔ la san 2026 ni 2027 sɛnɛ waati filaw la, tilenyara binjuguw kɛlɛli tɔɔrɔ kan minnu bɛ se ka binfagalanw kɛlɛ. 
Lamini sariyaw: Sɛnɛko fɛɛrɛw : baara bɛ ka boli sisan wasabulon kɔnɔ"Sɛnɛ sariya ni baloko ani jamanadenw lakanani seginni kan san 2026" (n'a dɔnnen don tugun ko san 2026 sɛnɛko sariyasun) o min bɛ dannaya dɔw fara ɲɔgɔn kan A bɛ tali Kɛ sɛnɛko tigitigi la.Ani waleya kuntaalajan kuraw Waati dantigɛlen Nɔnɔ bɔli kama. 

 Nin Teliyako ninnu bɛ dulon ka taa magow la ka taa Mɛli sinsin na, ani jiko wasa ɲɛnabɔli ɲɛmaaso la, ani ka ja nɔ furakɛ sɛnɛ bɔli fɛnw na.

=== FASARI 372 (taamasiyɛn 5253) — 2026-02-22 13:25:18 CEST ===
Faransi sɛnɛko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔli san 2026 kɔnɔ

Faransi sɛnɛko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔli san 2026 kɔnɔ, o ye jamana Fɛɛrɛw kuntilenna jɔnjɔn ye, wa a y'o sinsin  san 2026 nafolosɔrɔko sariyasunba kan kɔsa in na, min bɛ ɛrowari miliyɔn 300 ni kɔ bila sɛnɛkɛlaw ka kɔrɔtɔko dɛmɛ bolodalen dɔ la.

Nin ye sisan koɲɛw ni san 2026 kɔnɔna na, a ɲɛfɔcogo jɔnjɔnw ye:
1. Yɛrɛwasa cogoya ka kɛɲɛ ni kow tigɛcogo ye ka bɔ tɔnɔ sɔrɔli jaakuyali tigɛyɔrɔ dɔw la 
Hali nin bɛɛ la Faransi a ma wuli a bɛ to k'a jigi da fɛnw dondonni kan yɔrɔ dɔw la: 
Tigɛyɔrɔ Wasalen minnu ye yɛrɛta ye: Faransi bɛ bɔli kɛ tɔnɔba la Suman kisɛw la ani Nɔnɔ ni Sogo ka fɛn bɔtaw la (a sego bɛɛ lajɛlen na).
Yɔrɔ minnu ka fɛgɛ : Faransi tɛ yɛrɛta kɛ tiɲɛ ye siyɔw la, wa a bɛ kunkurunba don jiriden mugantaw la ani Nakɔfɛnw na. nka fanw ta la, Faransi mago bɛ ka siyɛ kulukulu kura 575 ɲɔgɔn jɔ san 2026 kɔnɔ Walasa a ka se k’a  ka yɛrɛ wasa mara.

Dulonni bɛɛ: walanda kura dɔ bɛ jirali kɛ ko Faransi tɛ datuguni kɛ fo n'a bɔra 60% dɔrɔn de la ka bɔ a ka Sigida magoɲɛfɛnw mago la cogoya tilenen na.

Gofɛrɛnaman ye tileya damadɔ daminɛ walasa ka "baloko kuntigiya segin":
Sɛnɛko ɲɛnabɔli sariya (2025): Nin sariya in bɛ san tan kuntilenna sigi sen kan, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la, a laɲini ye ka foro hakɛ sabati ka se 400.000 ma san 2035 kɔnɔ.
Falen fɛnw balocogo bolodacogo : o Laɲini ye ka Falen fɛnw foro hakɛ kɛ siɲɛ fila ye walasa ka dɔ bɔ jigida la sunbala siyɔ ladonni na.
Nka Jamana ka fɛɛrɛ, balo ni Baloko kama ani waati cogoya (SNANC): o jɛnsɛnna san 2026 feburuyekalo la, nin fɛɛrɛ in bɛ dusu don kɛ ka sigida fɛn bɔtaw dunni caya, ka sɔrɔ ka dan sigi sogow donni na.

Kurayali : Sɛnɛ yiriwali bolofara dɔ bɛna daminɛ san 2026 daminɛ na sɛnɛ jurudon fɛ walasa ka sɛnɛkɛla kamalenninw dɛmɛ ani ka forow kuraya.

3. Gɛlɛyaw ni jɔrɔnankow
San 2026 bɛ daminɛ cogo gɛlɛn na: 
Baarakɛyɔrɔw Gɛlɛya : Sisan sɛnɛkɛla 50% ɲɔgɔnna bɛna lafiɲɛbɔ kɛ san 2030, o b’a to mɔgɔw ka bɔnsɔn kurayali nafa ka bon san 2026 daminɛna.
Kafo ka sigida : San 2026 Pari diɲɛ sɛnɛko jirali daminɛna gɛlɛyabaw cɛma, min kɛra ni negebɔ ye ka bɔ tɔn dɔw fɛ minnu ye ɲɔgɔndan tilenbaliya ni sɔrɔ bɛrɛbɛrɛ jalaki.


Yala aw b’a fɛ ka kunnafoni caman sɔrɔ dɛmɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen kan min bolodara san 2026 nafolosɔrɔ sariyasun kɔnɔ sɛnɛko siratigɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnen dɔ kama wa?

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 373 (taamasiyɛn 5299) — 2026-02-22 01:40:04 CEST ===
Arabie Saoudite: A ye yɛrɛta hakɛ caman sɔrɔ (71% kɔnɔw, 129% nɔnɔ, 100% fanw) san 2023-2026 kɔnɔ, wa a bɛ ka taa a fɛ ka wari bila manabuguw ni sɛnɛko fɛɛrɛw la walasa ka Baloko sinsin.

Nin jatebɔ ninnu b’a jira ko Saudi Arabia ka Sugo 2030 ye ɲɛtaa sɔrɔ sɛnɛko siratigɛ la ka kɛ sɔrɔko jɔyɔrɔba ye. Ka fara yɛrɛta dafalen sɔrɔli kan fɛn bɔtaw la i n’a fɔ nɔnɔ ni fanw, fɛrɛli bɛ kalinin kɛ Manabuguw la min bɛ fɛɛrɛ hakilitigi jira walasa ka se sɔrɔ waati gɛlɛyaw kan.

Nin ɲɛtaa in kunbabaw filɛ nin ye, ka kɛɲɛ ni sigida, jiko ani sɛnɛko minisiriso ka kunnafoniw ye :

Kɔnɔw : O hakɛ panna ka se 71% ma O bɛ dɔ bɔ ka jigi da fɛnw kan minnu bɛ na jamana kɔnɔ cogoyabala, ka fara laɲini hakɛ kɔrɔtalen kan siniko kama.

Nɔnɔ: O bɔnna ni hakɛ 129% ye Masaya yɛlɛmana ka kɛ mara ka fɛn bɔta kuntigi ye ani fɛɛrɛw Minnu dulonnen bɛ a la.

Sɛnɛko fɛɛrɛbɔ : O b'a Sinsin Sisan, ka danbe ka taa jɛgɛ mara ma ani sɔnni labɛnen labɔli jiko kama mun kɔsɔn ka dalakan ta nin kɛlen in na.

Yala i b’a fɛ ka kunnafoni caman sɔrɔ wariko siraw kan sɛnɛko jɔlenw fɛɛrɛw la walima ka teliya ka wari don ka bɔ Sɛnɛ yiriwali nafolosɔrɔsiraw kɛsu kɔnɔ wa?

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 374 (taamasiyɛn 5307) — 2026-02-21 16:29:56 CEST ===
Marɔku : O b’a sinsin Sɔnniko Kurayali kan ani ka sɛnɛ nafa cakɛda fɛɛrɛw yiriwa (shɔw, nakɔfɛnw) walasa ka waati yɛlɛma kunbɛn.

Marɔku ye "Bɔnsɔn binkɛnɛ" fɛɛrɛ ta san bafila ni mugan bafila ni bisaba walasa ka sɛnɛko dugukolow sinsin u ka se ka jɔ waati yɛlɛma ɲɛkɔrɔ, k’a sinsin sɔnniko kuraw labɛnni kan (min bɛ kɛmɛsarada biwɔɔrɔ ni duuru  datugu ni jiko fɛɛrɛw ye) ani ka sɛnɛ nafa cakɛda fɛɛrɛw labɛn (shɔw, nakɔfɛnw). Nin fɛɛrɛ in kun ye ka Baloko sɔrɔ kɔgɔji ji kɔgɔbɔli fɛ, sɛnɛ tigitigi tali fɛ, ani ɲininiw kɛli kisɛw kan minnu bɛ se ka ja kɛlɛ.

Marɔku ka cɛsiri jɔnjɔnw sɛnɛko biɲɛdimi na, olu ye :

Sɔnniko kurayali : Ka sɔnniko fɛɛrɛw kɛ ka labɔli kɛ (sɔnniko sigida la) walasa ka yɔrɔw minta kɛmɛsarada biwɔɔrɔ ni duuru ɲɔgɔn dafa sisan, n'o laɲini ye seli ye kuraya labɔli ma.

Sɛnɛ nafabɔsira yiriwali : Ka nafabɔsira fɛɛrɛw bila jɔyɔrɔ fɔlɔ la (nakɔfɛnw, shɔw) ani k’u banbanni dalakanta, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la jigin jigin maraw la ani yɔrɔ minnu ka sɔrɔ ka dɔgɔ.

Sɛnɛ hakilitigi ni waati kuntaalajan : Ka "sɛnɛ hakilitigi" ta ni droniw, dugukolo jateminɛnanw, ani sɛnɛ jatedenya ye walasa ka ji kisi fo ka se kɛmɛsarada bisaba ma.

Sɛnɛko ɲinini : Kisɛ suguya kura minnu bɛ se ka ja ni kɔgɔ kɛlɛ, olu labɛnni jamana ka sɛnɛko ɲinini baarada fɛ (INRA) ani sanfɛkalansow.

Fɛɛrɛ bɛɛjɛfanga : Sisan fɛɛrɛ minnu bɛ senna (bafila ni mugan ani duuru bafila ni mugan ani wɔɔrɔ) olu kun ye ka sɛnɛkɛla misɛnninw dɛmɛ ani ka sɛnɛ sabatili sinsin walasa ka sanjiko dɛsɛ nɔ nɔgɔya Baloko la.

Nin fɛɛrɛ in ye yelifɛnba dɔ ye walasa ka yɔrɔko bɛnkan sɔrɔ ani ka jamana ka Baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 375 (taamasiyɛn 5312) — 2026-02-21 15:40:48 CEST ===
Marɔku : O b’a sinsin Sɔnniko Kurayali kan ani ka sɛnɛ nafa cakɛda fɛɛrɛw yiriwa (shɔw, nakɔfɛnw) walasa ka waati yɛlɛma kunbɛn.

=== FASARI 376 (taamasiyɛn 5316) — 2026-02-21 14:22:18 CEST ===
Marɔku : O b’a sinsin jiko biɲɛdimi kan ani ka sɛnɛ nafa cakɛda fɛɛrɛw yiriwa (shɔw, nakɔfɛnw) walasa ka waati yɛlɛma kunbɛn.

=== FASARI 377 (taamasiyɛn 5325) — 2026-02-21 10:25:03 CEST ===
Alzeri : San 2026 sirako bolodalen sinsinnen bɛ suman sɔrɔli caya kan, ka dɔnniya fɛɛrɛw ta, ka sɛnɛko nafolodon sariyaw lajɛ,"ka Dugukolo di a sɛnɛbagaw ma", ani ka sogo sɔrɔli teliya.

A ka san 2026 sirako bolodalen kɔnɔ, Alizeri ye fɛɛrɛba dɔ ta walasa ka Baloko yɛrɛta sɔrɔ. Nin fɛɛrɛ in sinsinnen bɛ suman sɔrɔli caya kan walasa ka ɲininiw dafa (a laɲini ye ka yɛrɛta sɔrɔ Alikaama gɛlɛn na) dɔnniya fɛɛrɛw ni bi fɛɛrɛw tali fɛ. A fana bɛ ka sɛnɛko wariko sariyaw lajɛ kokura walasa ka "Dugukolo di a sɛnɛbagaw ma" sariyakolo baara, ka sɔrɔ ka sogo sɔrɔli teliya o waati kelen na (jɛman ni bilenman bɛɛ) walasa ka dɔ bɔ fɛnw dondonni na jamana kɔnɔ.

San 2026 sɛnɛko siratigɛ la, a ka samasen jɔnjɔnw :

Sumanw sɔrɔli caya : Sirako in kun ye ka sɛnɛfɛnw sɔrɔ hectare kelen kɔnɔ, kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la Alikaama gɛlɛn, walasa ka yɛrɛta sɔrɔ, n’a laɲini ye ka sɔrɔ hakɛ cayalenba sɔrɔ, n’o Girinya ye miliyɔn 50 ye san 2025 waati kɔnɔ.

Dɔnniya taabolo : Ka fɛɛrɛw ta minnu sinsinnen bɛ dɔnniya ɲininiw kan, ka kisɛw yiriwa, ani ka sɛnɛyɔrɔw labɛn ka kɛɲɛ ni mara kelen-kelen bɛɛ ka jogo kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw ye.

Sɛnɛko nafolodonni sariyaw : Sɛnɛko bilasirali sariya seginni walasa ka sariyasunba fɛɛrɛw barika bonya ani ka sɛnɛfɛnw bɔli kɔlɔsi, ka sɔrɔ ka t’a fɛ ka sɛnɛko dugukolo tigiyali sabati. ka Dugukolo di a sɛnɛbagaw ma : Ka sɛnɛfɛnw baaracogo yiriwa sɛnɛfɛnw dilannikɛla lakikaw fɛ ani ka dɔ bɔ fɛnw na minnu bɛ na jamana kɔnɔ k’a sɔrɔ u ma dɛsɛ lase sugu ma.

Sogo bɔli : Ka fɛɛrɛ kɔrɔtɔlenw di walasa ka dɔ fara jamana ka sogo bilenman ni sogo jɛman sɔrɔli kan walasa ka jamana kɔnɔ ɲininiw dafa.

Nin sen ninnu bɛ na ni jiko gɛlɛyaw ni sanji dɔgɔyali gɛlɛyaw jaabi ye, wa u laɲini ye ka sɛnɛko kɛ jamana sɔrɔko jɔyɔrɔba ye.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 378 (taamasiyɛn 5333) — 2026-02-21 09:19:59 CEST ===
Moritani ka Brakna mara: Gofɛrɛnaman ka dɛmɛnw sɛnɛko siratigɛ la

Zuluyekalo tile 14, san 2025

Moritani gɔfɛrɛnaman ka bolonɔbila minnu kɛra sɛnɛko siratigɛ la Brakna mara la san 2025 cɛmancɛ la, olu sinsinnen tun bɛ jiko siratigɛ la, ani ka sɛnɛkɛlaw dɛmɛ walasa u ka se ka baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sabati, ka fara foro lajɛbaw kan walasa ka baarabolodalenw kɔlɔsi minnu bɛ senna.

Baarakɛminɛnw ni baarabolodalen jɔnjɔnw Brakna (Zuluyekalo san 2025)

Sɛgɛsɛgɛli ni kɔlɔsili taamaw : San 2025 zuluyekalo la, sɛnɛko minisiri (Amm Ould Bibat) ye foro lajɛ kɛ Boghé ni Bababé maraw la walasa ka sɛnɛko baarabolodalenw sɛgɛsɛgɛ minnu bɛ senna, n’u kun ye ka ɲɛnamaya kɛcogo ɲɛ ani ka sɛnɛfɛnw yiriwa.

Brakna Tilebi jiko baarabolodalen : Baara bɛ senna sɛnɛyɔrɔbaw yiriwali la, o la, hectare 1500 labɛnni bɛ kɛ Malo sɛnɛni kama, ani hectare 110 bilala nakɔsɛnɛ kama musow ka Kafo ye. Dɛmɛni kɛli Ntamaro sɛnɛni na : San 2025, jamana ye jaabi sɔrɔ Ntamaroforo ɲumanba la yɔrɔw la minnu tɛ jigin jigin ye, o b’a jira ko gɔfɛrɛnaman ye dɛmɛ don sɛnɛfɛnw suguya caman sɛnɛni na laadala yɔrɔw kɔkan.

Sanjiba lakanani : Ka fɛɛrɛko ni mara jɛkuluw baara walasa ka sanjiba nɔfɛkow kɔlɔsi ani ka sɛnɛyɔrɔw lakana bajida maraw kɔnɔ, Brakna fana sen bɛ o la, walasa sɛnɛko wuli ka jɔw ka taa ɲɛ.

Sɛnɛko fɛɛrɛ siratigɛw san 2025 kɔnɔ

Baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔli : Gofɛrɛnaman ye yɛrɛta sɔrɔli sigi senkan, k’a kɛ laɲini ye, k’a jira ko a bɛna se ka yɛrɛta 84% sɔrɔ nakɔfɛnw na san laban na.

Dugukolo kan jɛɲɔgɔnyaw : Ka barow kɛ ni diɲɛ jɛɲɔgɔnw ye (i n’a fɔ Sinuwa ani Itali) walasa ka baarabolodalenbaw lakunu i n’a fɔ Mban sɛnɛko baarabolodalen min bɛ Brakna.

Baara dɛmɛni : Ka bolonɔ bila bɛnkansɛbɛnw na minnu kun ye ka baara ni sɛnɛ dɛmɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la Brakna mara la, sɛnɛ minisiriso ka kɔlɔsili tilennen kɔnɔ.

Kunnafoni labanw bɛ se ka tugu ɲɔgɔn kɔ ni kunnafoniw ye minnu bɛ kɛ fanga fɛ i n’a fɔ Moritani kunnafonidilaw ka tɔnba walima sɛnɛko ni Baloko minisiriso ka ɲɛ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 379 (taamasiyɛn 5341) — 2026-02-21 06:51:38 CEST ===
Jamanaw ka cɛsiriw walasa ka sɛnɛko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sɔrɔ: Moritani, Alzeri, Marɔku, Arabu Sawudi, Faransi, Ameriki, Turki, Ukraniya, Irisila ani Sinuwa san 2026

Sɛnɛ yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya cɛsiri minnu kɛra san 2026 kɔnɔ, olu bɛ ka sinsin kosɛbɛ sigida suman sɔrɔli caya kan, ka fɛɛrɛ kuraw ta, ani ka nafolo sabatili yiriwa. Arabu jamanaw (Alzeri, Arabie Saoudite, Maroko, ani Moritani) b’a ɲini ka dɔ bɔ fɛnw na bɛ kɛ ni minnu ye jamana kɔnɔ, sirako bɛɛjɛfanga dɔ sababu fɛ, k’a sɔrɔ fangabaw (Sina, Ameriki, Irisila, Ukraniya, ani Turki) b’u sinsin fɛnw  lakanani banbanni kan, jaabi caman sɔrɔli, ani sɛnɛko fɛɛrɛ hakilitigiw.

Alzeri : San 2026 sirako bolodalen sinsinnen bɛ suman sɔrɔli caya kan, ka dɔnniya fɛɛrɛw ta, ka sɛnɛko wariko sariyaw lajɛ, ka "dugukolo di a  sɛnɛbagaw ma", ani ka sogo sɔrɔli teliya.

Arabie Saoudite : o ye kɛmɛsarada la caman sɔrɔ (71% kɔnɔw, 129% nɔnɔ, ani 100% fanw) san 2023-2026 kɔnɔ, wa a bɛ ka taa a fɛ ka wari bila sow yɔrɔ tagalen ni sɛnɛko fɛɛrɛw la walasa ka baloko Sinsin. Marɔku : O b’a sinsin jiko biɲɛdimi kan ani ka sɛnɛ nafa cakɛda fɛɛrɛw yiriwa (Nankɛfɛnw, nakɔfɛnw) walasa ka waati yɛlɛma kunbɛn.

Moritani: O bɛ sɛnɛyɔrɔw labɛnni sabati bajida la walasa ka dɔ fara Malo ni Alikaama sɔrɔli kan.

Duniya fangaw ani Sɛnɛ hakilitigi

Nin fanga tila-tila Forobayali in, n’o kɛra Ntɛnɛ feburuyekalo tile 16 san 2026, o ye fɛɛrɛ tigɛli ye Mali sɛnɛko bolofara bolo wuli waati la.

Ɲɛmɔgɔya kura : Dɔgɔtɔrɔ Ibrahima Samakɛ y’a ka baara daminɛ foroba la, n’o ye sɛnɛko minisiri ye, ka kɛ minisiri Daniel Siméon Kelema nɔ ye.
Baarakɛda ka dɔnniya sɔrɔli : Minisiri fila bɛɛ y’u ka waleɲumandɔn jira Tɛmɛsira ɲɛmɔgɔba, ɲɛmaaba Assimi Goïta la, ani minisiriɲɛmɔgɔba, ɲɛmaaba major Abdoulaye Maïga la, u ka dannaya kosɔn.
Weelekan min bɛ taa ɲɛ : Minisiri min bɛ bɔ baara la, o y’a ɲini baarakɛlaw fɛ, k'u su ka kantigiya ni cɛsiri kelen di a nɔnabila ma walasa gɔfɛrɛnaman ka walew ka taa ɲɛ.
Sɔrɔko laɲini : Minisiri min bɛ na, n’o ye dɔgɔtɔrɔ Samakɛ ye, o y’a jira ko a b’a fɛ ka sɛnɛko sɛmɛntiya ka kɛ sɔrɔ yiriwali sababu lakika ye Mali kɔnɔ, ka togodala sekobaw ta. Nin ɲɛnajɛ duman in b’a jira ko faamaw ka cɛsiri ye ka nin baarakɛda in sabati ani k’a lakunu, o min nafa ka bon kosɛbɛ jamana ka Baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya la.

Yala aw b’a fɛ ka kunnafoni wɛrɛw sɔrɔ dɔgɔtɔrɔ Ibrahima Samake ka ɲɛnamayasɛbɛn kan walima sɛnɛko fɔlɔfɔlɔko minnu ɲɛfɔlen don nin ɲɛmɔgɔya kura in kama wa?

🇷🇺🇲🇬Irisi ni Madagasikari jamana sɛnɛko minisiriw ye baro kɛ jɛkafɔ yiriwali kan sɛnɛko siratigɛ la.

Oksana Luth ni Jose Rasatarimanana ka kumaɲɔgɔnya kɛra Irisi jamana sɛnɛko minisiriso la.

🔄Madagasikari bɛ jiidi ni jiridenw ye an ka jamana  yɔrɔw la minnu bɛ tu kɔrɔ,  kakawo kisɛw,nkanfin, ani vanili. Irisi jamana fana labɛnnen don ka dɔ fara jamana kɔnɔ fɛnw feereli kan, kɔnɔw ni suman kisɛw fana sen bɛ o la.

🌱Irisi ye bagaji minɛnw dilanni ni u feerela ŋana dɔ ye. Oksana Lut y’a jira ko se ɲuman bɛ yen k’u di jamana ka sugu la.

🍚Irisi labɛnnen don k’a ka dɔnniya ni fɛɛrɛbɔ yiriwaliw tila ɲɔgɔn na, i n’a fɔ Sigida Malo suguya caman, minnu bɛ bɛn Madagasikari waati cogoya ma.

🎓Madagasikari kalanden minnu bɛ ka kalan kɛ sɛnɛ minisiriso ka sɛnɛko kalansobaw la. Irisila labɛnnen don ka dɔ fara o jɛkafɔ in kan.

🤝Walasa k’u ka jɛɲɔgɔnya sabati ka taa a fɛ sɛnɛko siratigɛ la, ɲɔgɔn nafa yɔrɔw la, jɛkuluw ye baro kɛ walasa ka bɛnkansɛbɛn dɔ bolonɔ bila jamana fila ninnu ka sɛnɛko baaradaw ni ɲɔgɔn cɛ.

=== FASARI 390 (taamasiyɛn 5446) — 2026-02-19 19:16:24 CEST ===
Mali Gofɛrɛnaman 



#CIGMA #KUNNAFONIW | Kunnafonidila min bɔra Minisiriw ka tɔnba/



O Sɛbɛn in bɛ kunnafoni Forobayali dɔ tila ka bɔ Mali minisiriɲɛmɔgɔso lajɛ la feburuyekalo tile 18 san 2026, n’o ɲɛmɔgɔya tun bɛ tɛmɛsira jamanakuntigi Assimi Goïta bolo, n’o bɛ sariyasen fila ɲɛfɔ ka ɲɛsin baarakɛlaw ka baarakɛcogo sigicogo n’a dugukolo tilali ma bolifɛnko ɲɛmaasow ma, ka fara marako sigili caman kan minisirisow kɔnɔ.
Kunnafoni kunbabaw b’a jira ko Mali jɔyɔrɔba bɛ Indaba minɛko lajɛba la, ka minɛnko sinsinni ni litiyamu ɲinini sabati, ka fara diɲɛ musow ka don labɛnni kan, musow ka josariyaw kan jamana min bɛ se ka muɲu.
Sigida lakanani gɛlɛyaw cɛma, lajɛ in bɛ kuma misisonya kɛlɛli jɛkafɔ kan Farafinna tilebinyanfan sariya kura dɔ sababu fɛ, wa a bɛ dengue kunbɛnni dege, o b’a jira ko gɔfɛrɛnaman y’a sinsin sɔrɔko camanba ni foroba kɛnɛyako kan.

=== FASARI 405 (taamasiyɛn 5556) — 2026-02-17 11:30:46 CEST ===
Jamanakuntigiya ka kibaruya labanw: Moritani, Alzeri, Marɔku, Arabie Saoudite, Faransi, Ameriki, Turquie, Ukraine, Irisila ani Sinuwa.


Jamanakuntigiw ka kibaruyaw ni fɛɛrɛko Yɛlɛmayɛlɛma minnu  bɔra kosɛbɛ jamana kofɔlenw na, olu kuncɛlen filɛ nin ye i n’a fɔ a fɔra cogo min na san 2026 feburuyekalo la:
Marɔku, Alzeri ani Moritani (Sahara ko)
Madrid ka kumaɲɔgɔnya: Feburuyekalo tile 9, san 2026, fɛn dɔ yɛlɛmana, n’o ye ko Maroko ni Alzeri jamana kɔkankow minisiriw sigilen bɛ kumaɲɔgɔnya tabali kelen kan Madrid, Ameriki ka dɛmɛ kɔnɔ, walasa ka kuma Sahara kɛlɛ kan.
Alzeri: Feburuyekalo tle 17, san 2026, jamanakuntigi Abdelmadjid Tebboune ye Nizɛri jamanakuntigi Abderrahmane Chiani bisimila, yɔrɔ min na, Alzeri ka dɛmɛn min bɛ Nizɛri ka yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya la, o y’a jira, wa Tebboune sɔnna weleli ma ka taa bɔ Nizɛri kɔfɛ.
Marɔku: San 2026 Zanwuyekalo laban na, masakɛ Mohamɛdi VI y’a ka foli lase Marɔku jamanadenw ma, a kɛlen kɔ ka Farafinna jamanaw ka ntolatanba kɛ ka ɲɛ, k’a jira ko Marɔku ka kantigiya bɛ Farafinna gun kan.
Moritani: Jamanakuntigi Mohamed Ould Cheikh Ghazouani b’a ka foro baaraw kɛ ka t’a fɛ, k’a sɔrɔ a ye kuma fɔ Kaédi dugumɔgɔw ye feburuyekalo tile 8 san 2026. Kalataw fana kɛra walasa ka baarakɛlaw ka tɔnw ka ɲɛmaasow kuraya Nouakchott.
Ameriki ni Faransi
Jamana ka Kelenya: so jɛman ye laseli kɛ feburuye kalo tile 12 san 2026, jamanakuntigi Donald Trump (min y’a ka fanga filanan daminɛ san 2025 Zanwuyekalo la) ni Latin Ameriki jamanakuntigiw ka lajɛba don ye. Ameriki ka fanga bɛ cɛsiri jɔyɔrɔ la Sahara ka kumaɲɔgɔnyaw la ani Jenɛfu kumaɲɔgɔnya lamini na Irisi ni Ukraine cɛ.
Faransi: Jamanakuntigi Emmanuel Macron donna “jɔkɔrɔtiɲɛ” dakun na ni san 2026 baarakɛnafolo dayɛlɛli ye, k’a sɔrɔ u bɛ ka sinsin kosɛbɛ Elyse ɲɔgɔndanko nataw kan ani tɔnw ka sɔsɔliw kan sarako ni baarakɛlaw ka bεnkanw kan.
Irisi jamana ni Ukɛrɛni jamanaw
Jenɛfu jɛkafɔw : 2026 sàn feburuyekalo tle 17, jɛkafɔ saba dafalen kura daminɛna Jenɛfu jamana na, irisila, Ukrayina ani Ameriki jamana ka cidenjɛkuluw cɛ, n’o kun ye ka kɛlɛ in ban, k’a sababu kɛ Krεmlin ka hakilisigi ye ko kumaɲɔgɔnya in na, jamanako bɛna Dòn a la.
Al-Maidan: Ukraine jamanatigi Volodymyr Zelensky y’a jira Munich jamana ka lajɛba la san 2026 feburuyekalo tile 14 ko Erɔpu jamanaw ka bɔli kumaɲɔgɔnya tabali kan,k'o ye filiba ye.
Irisi: Irisi jamana gofɛrɛnaman ye gazoli bɔli dabila fo ka se san 2026 feburuyekalo laban ma walasa ka sigida sugu dɛmɛ.
Arabie Saoudite ani Turquie
Arabie Saoudite: Feburuyekalo tile 12, san 2026, masakɛ Salman bin Abdulaziz ye masakɛ ka yamaruyaw di minnu tun bɛ dankarili kɛ ani ka mɔgɔba dɔw sigi, i n’a fɔ wariko minisiri ani sariyatigiba. Masakɛ ka kiritigɛso y’a fɔ fana ko sanji delili ka kɛ Masaya yɔrɔ bɛɛ la.
Turquie: Feburuyekalo tile 15, san 2026, jamanakuntigi Recep Tayyip Erdogan ye taama bolodalen dabila Arabu ka Kelenya jamanaw na, k’a sababu kɛ kɛnɛya gɛlɛya dɔ ye min binna Emirati jamanakuntigi kan.
Sinuwa jamana
Diɲɛ faabaw, Pari fana sen bɛ o la, Olu ye Siniwaw ka san kura ɲɛnajɛw ye san 2026 feburuyekalo daminɛ na, k’a sɔrɔ Pekin bɛ ka taa a fɛ k’a jɔyɔrɔ sabati diɲɛ sɔrɔko ni kɔkankow siratigɛ la.

#CIGMA #KUNNAFONIW | Sunkalo 2026 fɛɛrɛ kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenw : DRCC bɛ tajimanfɛnw sɔrɔli n’u labatoli kɔlɔsi suguw kɔnɔ

K’a sɔrɔ u bɛ kɔlɔsiliba kɛ tajimanfɛnw bɔli n'u tilali baara la, DRCC jɛkulu minnu bɛ Bamakɔ mara la, olu ye sɛgɛsɛgɛli gɛlɛnw kɛ tajimanfɛnw feereyɔrɔw la dugu kɔnɔ bitikiw kɔnɔ sugubaw kɔnɔ.

Kabini salon, sinsin kɛra kɛrɛnkɛrɛnnenya la fɛnw kan minnu bɛ kɛ ka minfɛnw labɛn suntigɛ la, i n’a fɔ kankanliba, Lemuruji, Ɲamaku, Ntamaro ani Lemurukunmunnin.

=== FASARI 410 (taamasiyɛn 5634) — 2026-02-16 01:48:12 CEST ===
Sinuwa jamanakuntigiya ka dɛmɛn sɛnɛko la bi

Ka kɛɲɛ ni fɛɛrɛ laadilikanw ye minnu bɔra san 2026 daminɛ na, Sinuwa jamanakuntigiya bɛ fɔlɔ ka « togodala ko saba » (sɛnɛ, togoda maraw, ani sɛnɛkɛlaw) ɲɛnabɔ, wa ni k’a sinsin togodaw lakununni bɛɛ lajɛlen sabatili kan. Dɛmɛn yɔrɔ kolomaw jɔ ni kan: bɔko kura Fɛɛrɛ  dɔ farali suman kisɛw bɔli fanga kan ni jinɛ kilo miliyari 100 ye (tɔni miliyɔn 50) ani ka suman kisɛ ni tulu sɔrɔli basigi kɛ tiɲɛ ye,Ka sɔrɔko dalakan sinsin suman kisɛ sɛnɛkɛlaw ye ani ka sɔngɔ fɛɛrɛw ɲɛ, dɛmɛ ani hakilisigi; Ani ka dɛmɛ tugubaarabolodalen kɔrɔbɔtaw la , n’o ye ka dugukoloko bɛnkansɛbɛnw janya ka se san 30 wɛrɛ ma.

=== FASARI 411 (taamasiyɛn 5651) — 2026-02-16 00:02:30 CEST ===
Jamanakuntigiya ka dɛmɛ Ameriki sɛnɛko la bi

Ka da kunnafoniw kan minnu bɔra kɔsa in na, san 2025 labanw na ani san 2026 daminɛ na, jamanakuntigiya ka fanga bɛ ka dɛmɛba lase Ameriki sɛnɛko ma wariko ni sariyako siratigɛ la, a sinsinnen don kosɛbɛ dɛmɛ tilennenw kan, ani damankasi balawu waatw la, ani suguw dɛmɛni jago siratigɛ la. Kabini san 2026 feburuyekalo la, sɛnɛ marabolo ye o bila fasodenya lakanani yɔrɔ koloma la, k’a laɲini ka sɛnɛkɛlaw sɔrɔ yiriwa, ka doniba fɛkɛya, ani ka mɔgɔ tanga jamana wɛrɛw ka ɲɔgɔndan ma.

=== FASARI 413 (taamasiyɛn 5665) — 2026-02-15 22:34:04 CEST ===

Arabu Saudite bɛ ka sɛnɛ dɛmɛ barikama di san ba fila ni mugan wɔɔrɔ kɔnɔ waleyaw fɛ i n'a fɔ "tɔgɔda" (a bɛ se riyali ba bi duuru ni naani  ma san kɔnɔ) ani ka sɛnɛkɛla misɛnninw dɛmɛ (i n'a fɔ riyali 30 ntamarosun kelen na). === FASARI 414 (taamasiyɛn 5669) — 2026-02-15 22:22:02 CEST ===
Arabie Saoudite ka Sɛnɛko Dɛmɛ Bi

Arabu Saudite bɛ ka sɛnɛ dɛmɛ barikama di san 2026 kɔnɔ waleyaw fɛ i n'a fɔ "tɔgɔda" (a bɛ se riyali 54.000 ma san kɔnɔ) ani ka sɛnɛkɛla misɛnninw dɛmɛ (i n'a fɔ riyali 30 ntamarosun kelen na). Lamini, jiko ani sɛnɛko minisiriso b’a ɲini ka dɛmɛ don sow tigɛyɔrɔ minu tanganen don ntamarow, daabaw, ani jɛgɛw siratigɛ la, ka dɛmɛ tilennen di mɔgɔ ma minnu tɔɔrɔla balawu fɛ, o la, min mɛli ni Baloko lakana banban.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 415 (taamasiyɛn 5693) — 2026-02-15 16:32:26 CEST ===
Marɔku masaya ka sɛnɛko dɛmɛni bi
Marɔku ye waleya kɔrɔtɔlen dɔ daminɛ min nafa ye dirham miliyari 3 ye (san 2026 feburuyekalo) ni masakɛ ka cikanw ye walasa ka sɛnɛyɔrɔw dɛmɛ minnu tɔɔrɔla sanjiba fɛ, o la, dirham miliyɔn 300 bɛ di sɛnɛkɛlaw ni baganmaralaw ma k’a ɲɛsin tukun sɛnɛkɛlaw ni baganmaralaw ma, ka fara dirham miliyari 1,7 kan walasa ka sɛnɛko ni sɔniko nafolosɔrɔsiraw labɛn kokura, ka sɔrɔ ka t’a fɛ ka dɛmɛ don sɛnɛko nafolodonni na (falenfɛnw ani baganw ka cakɛdaw) ani sɔniko sigida la.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 416 (taamasiyɛn 5705) — 2026-02-15 15:08:54 CEST ===
Alzeri jamanakuntigiya ka Sɛnɛ Dɛmɛ Bi

San 2026 feburuyekalo la, Alzeri jamanakuntigiso tora k’a ka dɛmɛ barika bonya sɛnɛko siratigɛ la, k’a kɛ fɛɛrɛ tigɛlen ye walasa ka dumuniko sɔrɔ, k’a sinsin kosɛbɛ seko ni dɔnko yiriwali kan ani ka sɛnɛyɔrɔw bonya.

Jamanakuntigiso ka dɛmɛn fɛɛrɛ jɔnjɔnw sɛnɛko la (san 2026 daminɛ na) .

Sɛnɛko baarakɛnafolo cayara : Jamana ye dinari miliyari 764,2 (dɔlari miliyari 5,8 ɲɔgɔn) di sɛnɛko ma san 2026 nafolo sariya kɔnɔ, o ye 4% faralen ye ni i y’a suma ni san tɛmɛnnen ta ye, walasa ka togodala ni kungo yiriwali waleyaw dɛmɛ.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 417 (taamasiyɛn 5718) — 2026-02-15 14:19:09 CEST ===
Moritani jamanatigiya ka Sɛnɛ Dɛmɛ Bi

Kɔsa in na, a ka kuma kɔnɔna na, san 2026 feburuyekalo tile 12, Moritani jamanakuntigi Mohamed Ould Cheikh Ghazouani y’a sinsin a kan ko a ka kan ka "sɛnɛko girin ka jɔ" bɛɛ lajɛlen daminɛ jamana kɔnɔ walasa ka baloko yɛrɛmahɔrɔnya sabati. A ka taama senfɛ Gorgol mara la, a y’a ɲɛfɔ ko jamana ka ɲɛsinfan sisan, o ye fɛɛrɛ dalakantali ye min laɲini ye ka sɛnɛfɛnw sɛmɛntiya ka kɛ nafa faralen ye jamana sɔrɔko la.

----------------------------------------

=== FASARI 421 (taamasiyɛn 5746) — 2026-02-14 22:28:23 CEST ===
Irisi jamana ka gɔfɛrɛnaman
Feburuyekalo tile 8 nɛgɛ kanɲɛ 18h45
Gofɛrɛnaman ka baara. Dɔgɔkun kɔnɔkobaw ni latigɛw bɛ sɔrɔ sɛgɛsɛgɛli kɔnɔ:

✅Irisi jamanatigi ka cikan kɔnɔ, minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ ye baarakɛta kɛ Berezil jamana na. Mikhail Mishustin y’a sen don Irisi ni Berezil jamana ka jɛkafɔba ka lajɛ 8nan na. Berezil ye Irisi ka jamana kɔkan sɔrɔko jɛɲɔgɔnba ye Latɛn Ameriki, a bɛ kɛ o mara ka jagokɛta bɛɛ lajɛlen tilancɛ ɲɔgɔn ye. Minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ fana ye ɲɔgɔn Sɔrɔ ni Berezil jamanatigi Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva ye, ka Taa Irisi ni Berezil jagokɛlaw ka tɔnba dayɛlɛli la.

✅Irisi jamana ni Belarus jamanaw ka minisiriw ka lajɛ dɔ kɛra Moskow. Irisi ni Belarus jamana ka baarakɛdabaw bɛna se k’u ka fɛn dilannenw sɛbɛn iko jɛkulu jamana ka fɛnw. O tɛmɛnen kɔ, sisikurun siratigɛ la, sisikurun min bɛ taa ni mɔgɔw ye Irisi ni Belarus cɛ, o labɛnna ka daminɛ san 2026 awirilikalo la.

Minisiriɲɛmɔgɔ ye ɲɔgɔn sɔrɔ ni Risi jamana ka cidenjɛkulu (CPRF) ani irisi laada kulu mɔgɔ dɔw ye walasa ka baro kɛ labɛnw kan gɔfɛrɛnaman ka san o san jatebɔsɛbɛn kama.

----------------------------------------
